# Vorkerland effects: lifecycle, campaign state, diplomacy and postwar actions.
# Navigation: campaign_state_effects = control/legitimacy/assistance;
# collapse_effects = materialization; diplomacy_effects = regional relations;
# focus_decision_effects = wartime orders; phase_effects = handoff and postwar orders.
# Registered section markers below are also consumed by validators.

# --- dirty_zone_effects ---
# Dirty Zone opening presentation helpers.
# The reveal lifecycle itself remains owned by ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.85.

ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_dirty_zone_smoke = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened }

		# The main Dirty Zone haze is created globally at startup as actors 1 and 2.
		# Recreate both ids with the same inert mesh but no particle emitter. Using
		# the existing ids replaces the live particle actors and also makes the
		# cleanup deterministic after save-load.
		create_entity = {
			entity = ADISCORD_global_clear_entity
			id = 1
			x = 42
			y = 21
			z = -100.000
			province = 16531
			state = 42
			rotation = 1.2
			scale = 0.03
			min_zoom = 5555.0
		}
		create_entity = {
			entity = ADISCORD_global_clear_entity
			id = 2
			x = 42
			y = 21
			z = -100.000
			province = 12863
			state = 42
			rotation = 1.2
			scale = 0.02
			min_zoom = 5555.0
		}

		# The Unity Tower landmark carries its own independent smog/fire particles.
		# Recreate that actor too so opening the Zone cannot leave a local smoke
		# source behind. Preserve the correct intact/ruined visual state.
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_unity_tower_visual = yes
		}
		else = {
			40 = {
				destroy_entity = 610040
				create_entity = {
					entity = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pyramid_clear_entity
					id = 610040
					x = 3159.92992
					y = 725.68
					z = 2.83464
					rotation = 5.410520681
					scale = 0.15
					min_zoom = 2000
				}
			}
		}

		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone_smoke_cleared
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_dirty_zone_opening_presentation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone_presentation_finalized }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone_presentation_finalized
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_dirty_zone_smoke = yes

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone_news_published }
				RUS = { exists = yes }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone_news_published
			RUS = { news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_zone.1 } }
		}
	}
}

# Save-load repair: startup recreates the two global pollution actors and the
# Vorkerland landmark. If the Zone was already opened, this later on_startup
# hook overwrites all three with their durable particle-free variants.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_dirty_zone_opening_visual = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_dirty_zone_smoke = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_dirty_zone_remainder = {
	# Follow the current owner of the adjacent Starolesye state on save-load;
	# never resurrect an annexed republic or take land from another country.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened
			461 = { is_owned_by = EXZ }
			51 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = EXZ } }
		}
		51 = {
			owner = {
				461 = {
					add_core_of = SLA
					set_state_owner_to = PREV
					set_state_controller_to = PREV
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- campaign_state_effects ---
# Applied once to the 37 listed central-theatre states when wars open. The states
# are listed rather than scanned because this sits in the phase layer, where a
# world scan is forbidden. An attacker_modifier cannot carry remove_trigger;
# ADISCORD_vorkerland_retire_civil_war_modifiers clears it at the end.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_central_attrition = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_attrition_applied } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_attrition_applied
		32 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		33 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		200 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		201 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		75 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		106 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		107 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		121 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		36 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		37 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		38 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		39 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		324 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		102 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		109 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		111 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		325 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		81 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		110 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		124 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		79 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		306 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		308 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		309 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		327 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		82 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		323 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		108 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		122 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		123 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		27 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		35 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		315 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		316 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		317 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		318 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		320 = { set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
	}
}
# Comeback valve. A claimant pushed to the brink digs in harder, which stops the
# first side to lose momentum from simply dissolving and keeps all three alive
# long enough for the war to be decided by play rather than by the opening
# positions. Hysteresis between 0.65 and 0.55 prevents the idea from flickering.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_organized_defence = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			has_war = yes
			surrender_progress > 0.65
			NOT = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence }
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			# The claimant guard leads deliberately. This effect is called from
			# on_state_control_changed for both the gaining and the losing side,
			# and opening the limit with has_idea evaluated it in every scope the
			# hook produces, which logged 146 "is not A valid Idea" errors per
			# collapse. The sibling branch above never did, because it tests the
			# tag first and short-circuits.
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence
			OR = {
				has_war = no
				surrender_progress < 0.55
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence
	}
}

# Iconic objectives. Twelve named centres each swing legitimacy once per
# campaign when they change hands between claimants. One parameterised effect
# serves all twelve; the caller supplies the identity and the value.
#
#	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_id = 1 }
#	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_value = 0.15 }
#	ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_iconic_objective = yes
#
# Called from on_state_control_changed, so ROOT is the new controller and FROM
# is the previous one, matching the convention documented in
# ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_check_first_claimant_capital_fall.
#
# The loser is deliberately not simply punished: it loses legitimacy, which is
# combat power, but gains war support. Losing a symbol rallies the population
# even as it discredits the government, and that keeps a losing claimant in the
# war instead of collapsing the moment it is pushed off its own capital.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_iconic_objective = {
	ROOT = {
		set_temp_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_value
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		add_war_support = 0.04
	}
	FROM = {
		set_temp_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_value
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta value = -1 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		add_war_support = 0.05
		add_stability = -0.02
	}
	# A global array is the ordinary array argument with a global. prefix. There
	# is no separate global-array effect; a mistyped one parses cleanly and then
	# silently does nothing, which would leave the once-per-campaign guard open.
	add_to_array = {
		array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_objectives_taken
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_objective_id
	}
}

# Legitimacy ledger for the three claimants. The caller sets the temp parameter
# and calls this; the parameter is never mutated here, so a caller may reuse it.
#
#	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.08 }
#	ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
#
# _mod and _level are derived from the ledger on every write rather than being
# accumulated separately, so they cannot drift out of step with it once the
# ledger saturates at a clamp boundary.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy min = -1 max = 1 }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_derived = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_doctrine_dynamic_state = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_leader = yes
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_derived = {
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_mod
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy
	}
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_mod value = 0.2 }
	# [-1..1] mapped onto the integer band [0..5] used to gate events and decisions.
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_level
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_level value = 1 }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_level value = 2.5 }
	round_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_level
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_legitimacy = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_mod value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_level value = 2 }
	# Prewar preparation sets the opening mandate. WKR reads the WRK focuses
	# through copy_completed_from; the compact is a public agreement of both sides.
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			WRK_capability_council_mandate = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0.10 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			WRK_capability_charter_mandate = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				tag = WKR
				tag = VAD
			}
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0.05 }
	}
	# A district-leaning prewar balance carries the districts' consent to the heir.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_lean value = 1 compare = equals }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_preparation_heir = yes
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_derived = yes
	}
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 0 }
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_dynamic }
}

# Theatre priority follows the active central war, not elapsed calendar time.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_theatre_priority = {
	# COUNTRY WKR/VAD/TVA: only the listed minor opponents receive the combat
	# overmatch. Player and AI claimants share its wartime lifecycle.
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority }
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority
			OR = {
				has_war = no
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority
	}
}

# Preserve a live incumbent whenever neither rival has strictly greater
# legitimacy. Only when there is no qualifying incumbent does the deterministic
# WKR/VAD/TVA fallback elect a replacement.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_leader = {
	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes }
			NOT = { VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
			NOT = { TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes }
			NOT = { WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = VAD.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
			NOT = { TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = VAD.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader = 2 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes }
			NOT = { WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = TVA.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
			NOT = { VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = TVA.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader = 3 }
	}

	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader } }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader value = 1 } }
		WKR = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader value = 2 } }
		VAD = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_incumbent_leader value = 3 } }
		TVA = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes }
			NOT = { VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
			NOT = { TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		WKR = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes }
			NOT = { TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy value = VAD.ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy compare = greater_than } } }
		}
		VAD = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_live_claimant = yes } }
		TVA = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_leader }
	}
}

# Seventeen explicit checks keep campaign reconciliation bounded.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_recount_central_control = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 0 }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre }
		PREV = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 1 } }
	}
}

# Clear all coalition overlays first, then add at most one against the live
# claimant holding strictly the most central-theatre states (more than eight).
# Legitimacy ties fall back to WKR, so they cannot select the territorial leader.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = {
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_wkr } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_wkr }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_vad } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_vad }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_tva } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_tva }

	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			is_ai = yes
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { tag = WKR }
			WKR = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_control_leader = yes
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 8 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_wkr
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			is_ai = yes
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { tag = VAD }
			VAD = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_control_leader = yes
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 8 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_vad
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
			is_ai = yes
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { tag = TVA }
			TVA = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_central_control_leader = yes
				check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_control_score value = 8 compare = greater_than }
			}
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_tva
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_campaign_state = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_recount_central_control = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_recount_central_control = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_recount_central_control = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_legitimacy_leader = yes
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		WKR = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_theatre_priority = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		VAD = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_theatre_priority = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		TVA = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_theatre_priority = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_claimant_coalition = yes
		}
	}
}

# --- collapse_dirty_effects ---
# Northern tribal land stays outside the contaminated belt regardless of its owner.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_northern_dirty_modifiers = {
	58 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	59 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	60 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	61 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	62 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	63 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	64 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
	65 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = yes }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_remove_dirty_state_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state }
		owner = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state } }

		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_sla = {
	log = "[ADISCORD Vorkerland] SLA exclusion-zone setup started"
	# EXZ owns and controls every closed-zone state from game start. Keep the
	# explicit owner-then-controller order when a successor materialises.
	49 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	50 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	51 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	155 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	176 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	187 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	191 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	233 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	329 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	330 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	461 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	710 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	711 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	712 = { add_core_of = SLA set_state_owner_to = SLA set_state_controller_to = SLA }
	log = "[ADISCORD Vorkerland] SLA state ownership assigned"
	SLA = {
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zone_fragile_administration
		set_capital = { state = 49 }
		build_railway = { level = 2 fallback = yes start_state = 49 target_state = 191 }
		ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_hazard_infantry = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		load_oob = "SLA_vorkerland_collapse"
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 40
			producer = SLA
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0
			amount = 24
			producer = SLA
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 800 producer = SLA }
	}
	log = "[ADISCORD Vorkerland] SLA setup completed"
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_dirty_cascade_if_ready = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = SLA
			country_exists = MLR
			country_exists = RZA
			country_exists = SCA
			country_exists = ERT
			country_exists = IRT
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_cascade_complete
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_cascade_recovery_scheduled
		# The closed zone is a political map, not a truce. One day after the last
		# administration appears, the reactor perimeter and the eastern corridor
		# open their own local rivalries.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_closed_zone_bracket_scheduled } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_closed_zone_bracket_scheduled
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.91 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_start_khan_border_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = RUS
			country_exists = SLA
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_resolved }
			RUS = { NOT = { has_war_with = SLA } }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started
		49 = { add_claim_by = RUS }
		176 = { add_claim_by = RUS }
		RUS = {
			add_manpower = 30000
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 18000 producer = RUS }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 120 producer = RUS }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_anti_tank_equipment_2163 amount = 180 producer = RUS }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 350 producer = RUS }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_anti_air_equipment_2163 amount = 100 producer = RUS }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 producer = RUS }
			declare_war_on = { target = SLA type = annex_everything }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_close_khan_border_war_flags = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started }
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_resolved
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_khan_border_war = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_close_khan_border_war_flags = yes
	# Extend the award through occupied SLA land before peace returns occupation.
	# Each pass consumes at least one state; disconnected captures stay with SLA.
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				is_owned_by = SLA
				any_neighbor_state = {
					is_owned_by = RUS
					is_controlled_by = RUS
				}
			}
		}
		every_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_by = SLA
				any_neighbor_state = {
					is_owned_by = RUS
					is_controlled_by = RUS
				}
			}
			set_state_owner_to = RUS
			set_state_controller_to = RUS
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = SLA }
		white_peace = SLA
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = SLA SLA = { owns_state = 51 } } SLA = { set_capital = { state = 51 } } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_khan_border_war = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_sla_campaign_active }
			country_exists = SLA
			has_war_with = SLA
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_holds_sla_playable = yes
					SLA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_target_defeated = yes capital_scope = { OR = { is_controlled_by = RUS controller = { is_subject_of = RUS has_war_with = SLA } } } }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_campaign_target value = 1 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_settle_dirty_target = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						controls_state = 49
						controls_state = 176
					}
					AND = {
						has_global_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started days > 180 }
						OR = { controls_state = 49 controls_state = 176 }
					}
					has_global_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_khan_border_war_started days > 270 }
					SLA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rus_target_defeated = yes }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_khan_border_war = yes
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_intervene_wave_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = { EFL = { exists = yes } }
		SCA = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = EFL }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 125 producer = EFL }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_intervene_wave_3 = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL = { exists = yes } }
		ERT = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = VAL }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 125 producer = VAL }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { CIN = { exists = yes } }
		IRT = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 250 producer = CIN }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 125 producer = CIN }
		}
	}
}

# --- collapse_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_unity_tower_visual = {
	# Shared actor 610040. Create it from the landmark state so a later WRK
	# annex cannot drop it. Destroy first: same-id replace is not reliable.
	# min_zoom 2000 is the authored strategic-haze threshold for this actor.
	40 = {
		destroy_entity = 610040
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_restored }
			create_entity = {
				entity = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pyramid_entity
				id = 610040
				x = 3159.92992
				y = 725.68
				z = 2.83464
				rotation = 5.410520681
				scale = 0.15
				min_zoom = 2000
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved }
			create_entity = {
				entity = ADISCORD_unity_tower_ruins_entity
				id = 610040
				x = 3159.92992
				y = 725.68
				z = 2.83464
				rotation = 5.410520681
				scale = 0.15
				min_zoom = 2000
			}
			set_entity_animation = { id = 610040 animation = "ruins" }
		}
		else = {
			create_entity = {
				entity = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pyramid_entity
				id = 610040
				x = 3159.92992
				y = 725.68
				z = 2.83464
				rotation = 5.410520681
				scale = 0.15
				min_zoom = 2000
			}
		}
	}
}

# The three postwar claimant routes use their own focus to finance the same
# landmark restoration. The global guard keeps the news and actor replacement
# single-shot across focus retries, handoff, and save-load.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_unity_tower = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_restored } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_restored
		40 = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_unity_tower_visual = yes }
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_news.4 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_animate_unity_tower_destruction = {
	40 = {
		destroy_entity = 610040
		create_entity = {
			entity = ADISCORD_unity_tower_collapse_entity
			id = 610040
			x = 3159.92992
			y = 725.68
			z = 2.83464
			rotation = 5.410520681
			scale = 0.15
			min_zoom = 2000
		}
		set_entity_animation = { id = 610040 animation = "collapse" }
	}
}

# goto_province only moves the local camera when ROOT is the human country.
# Schedule the approach on that tag; an AI-only campaign skips to detonation.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_unity_tower_camera_approach = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { exists = yes is_ai = no } }
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.3 hours = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { VAD = { exists = yes is_ai = no } }
		VAD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.3 hours = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { TVA = { exists = yes is_ai = no } }
		TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.3 hours = 1 } }
	}
	else = {
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.4 hours = 1 } }
	}
}

# The legal split is already public. The civil-war paper, super event and Tower
# clip wait for the first central bracket declarations so they match the first
# big war rather than the partition.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_announce_first_central_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_news_shown } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_news_shown
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_unity_tower_destruction_resolved
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_unity_tower_camera_approach = yes
		}
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_superevent_news.1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = {
	# Do not run an unscoped spirit scrub here. Most old autonomies had only the
	# shared WRK crisis spirit, so the former blanket cleanup left their national
	# spirit row empty and made a local transition look like a world-wide wipe.
	# Every removal below is tied to a tag that actually starts with that idea.
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR } }
		remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = vorkerism
					has_country_leader_ideology = neo_vorkerism
				}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate
					}
				}
			}
			add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VLA }
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = VLA_national_spirit
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = ZAO tag = PWR tag = ROM tag = SOL tag = TRU } }
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = TVA }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_ideological_fanaticism
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = WTD }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_worx_protocols
	}
	else_if = { limit = { tag = WPA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wpa_municipal_compact }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = WPS } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wps_factory_councils }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = EBA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_eba_free_quays }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = ZTA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zta_golden_river_order }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = TGD } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tgd_grid_collapse }
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = CSL }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_csl_isolated_city
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = EYR tag = EGC tag = RIV tag = REV tag = YOR tag = NDN tag = SWB tag = VHV tag = OSV tag = PSD tag = DVA tag = SRA tag = IBL tag = IBA } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
	}
	# This thin locked template turns one political mobilisation into frontage
	# without acquiring a duplicate long-term force-design track.
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_minor_combatant = yes }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_emergency_militia_template = yes
	}
	# Volunteer-only is the engine default. Combatants already raise formations
	# and cannot wait for 150 PP before a recruitable pool exists.
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_limited_conscription = yes
	# Every collapse participant receives cores on the territory assigned during
	# its one-time setup. This also covers legacy tags whose old history files do
	# not know about the post-collapse partition.
	every_owned_state = { add_core_of = ROOT }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_limited_conscription = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_idea = limited_conscription }
			OR = {
				has_idea = disarmed_nation
				has_idea = volunteer_only
				tag = RUS
			}
		}
		add_ideas = limited_conscription
	}
}

# One bounded mobilisation per minor. Two three-battalion formations are enough
# to close a hole or form a reserve, but not enough to erase the advantage of a
# 12-15 formation main claimant. PREV is the country owning the capital scope.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_emergency_levies = {
	add_manpower = 1800
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 3600 producer = ROOT }
	PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_emergency_militia_template = yes }
	capital_scope = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "division_template = \"Emergency Militia\" start_experience_factor = 0.05 start_equipment_factor = 0.75"
			owner = PREV
			count = 2
		}
	}
}

# WKR normally inherits WRK's armed forces, but annexation transfers divisions
# without copying WRK's recruitable template database. Materialize an editable
# line division and mobile group alongside the locked emergency guard. The
# one-shot reserve itself remains small: three border guards in state 33 and
# three formations in the state 32 capital reserve (was 2+2; raised because
# WKR could not hold two claimant fronts against TVA's nineteen formations).
# WKR also inherits WRK's twelve pre-collapse formations and one mobile group.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_wkr_force_templates_v2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_force_templates_v2_applied }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_force_templates_v2_applied
		division_template = {
			name = "Worker Rifle Division"
			template_counter = 3
			division_names_group = WRK_INF_01
			is_locked = no
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
			regiments = {
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 3 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 3 }
				ADISCORD_line_artillery = { x = 2 y = 0 }
			}
		}
		division_template = {
			name = "Workerland Mobile Group"
			template_counter = 5
			division_names_group = WRK_INF_01
			is_locked = no
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_combat_platform = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_combat_platform = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_combat_platform = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				ADISCORD_combat_platform = { x = 0 y = 3 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 2 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 2 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_mechanized_infantry = { x = 2 y = 2 }
			}
			support = {
				engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				artillery = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				maintenance_company = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				signal_company = { x = 0 y = 3 }
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_wkr_home_guard = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_wkr_force_templates_v2 = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_home_guard_deployed_v1 }
			owns_state = 32
			controls_state = 32
			owns_state = 33
			controls_state = 33
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_home_guard_deployed_v1
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_worker_home_guard_template = yes
		add_manpower = 12000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 960 producer = WKR }
		33 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "division_template = \"Worker Home Guard\" start_experience_factor = 0.10 start_equipment_factor = 0.85"
				owner = WKR
				count = 3
			}
		}
		32 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "division_template = \"Worker Home Guard\" start_experience_factor = 0.10 start_equipment_factor = 0.85"
				owner = WKR
				count = 3
			}
		}
	}
}

# Political caches and subject status finish rebuilding after the initial map
# effect. This explicit, idempotent second pass is called once by event .41; it
# replaces the old monthly repair idea without scanning every country.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_finalize_conflict_spirits = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR } }
		remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
		if = {
			limit = { tag = WKR }
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_arsenal_command
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = vorkerism
					has_country_leader_ideology = neo_vorkerism
				}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate
					}
				}
			}
			add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_imperial_chancery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VLA }
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = VLA_national_spirit
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = ZAO tag = PWR tag = ROM tag = SOL tag = TRU } }
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = TVA }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_ideological_fanaticism
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = WTD }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_worx_protocols
	}
	else_if = { limit = { tag = WPA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wpa_municipal_compact }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = WPS } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wps_factory_councils }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = EBA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_eba_free_quays }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = ZTA } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_zta_golden_river_order }
	else_if = { limit = { tag = TGD } add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tgd_grid_collapse }
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = CSL }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_csl_isolated_city
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = EYR tag = EGC tag = RIV tag = REV tag = YOR tag = NDN tag = SWB tag = VHV tag = OSV tag = PSD tag = DVA tag = SRA tag = IBL tag = IBA } }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_mobilized_periphery
	}
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_conflict_spirits_finalized
}

# A versioned, idempotent assertion for the main claimant spirits. The initial
# partition and deferred political-cache rebuild already clean WRK; the fresh
# war-outbreak tick makes one final pass after those systems have finished.
# Progress already bought through decisions is never downgraded.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_spirit_progression = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR } }
		remove_ideas = WRK_ashes_of_the_crown
		remove_ideas = WRK_hourglass_of_discord
		remove_ideas = WRK_constitution_of_the_republic
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_leader_ideology = vorkerism
					has_country_leader_ideology = neo_vorkerism
				}
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_front_republic
						has_idea = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution_renewed_mandate
					}
				}
			}
			add_ideas = WRK_birthplace_of_the_first_revolution
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = WKR }
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_arsenal_command
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = TVA }
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
						has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate_2
						has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate_3
					}
				}
			}
			add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
		}
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
	}
}

# A republic no longer lacks a metropolis once it accepts the live Vorkerland
# claimant's sovereignty. WKR is the wartime worker claimant; WRK is restored
# only after reunification.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
			OR = {
				is_subject_of = WKR
				is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_republics_from_the_ruins
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_activate_vla_auxiliaries = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = WKR tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			VLA = { exists = yes is_subject_of = ROOT }
		}
		# A finite contract delivery represents Volnograd opening its arsenals.
		if = {
			limit = { VLA = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_auxiliary_contract_activated } } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 3000 producer = VLA }
			VLA = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_auxiliary_contract_activated }
		}
		VLA = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = WKR
					WKR = { has_war_with = VAD }
					NOT = { has_war_with = VAD }
				}
				add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = WKR enemy = VAD hostility_reason = asked_to_join }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = WKR
					WKR = { has_war_with = TVA }
					NOT = { has_war_with = TVA }
				}
				add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = WKR enemy = TVA hostility_reason = asked_to_join }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_war_with = VAD }
					NOT = { has_war_with = VAD }
				}
				add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation enemy = VAD hostility_reason = asked_to_join }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_war_with = TVA }
					NOT = { has_war_with = TVA }
				}
				add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation enemy = TVA hostility_reason = asked_to_join }
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_complete_protectorate_enforcement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			VLA = {
				exists = yes
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_protectorate_enforcement
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = VLA } white_peace = VLA }
		puppet = VLA
		set_autonomy = { target = VLA autonomy_state = autonomy_district_in_Vorkerland freedom_level = 0.10 }
		VLA = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_protectorate_enforcement
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joined_worker_republic
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_republics_from_ruins = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_activate_vla_auxiliaries = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_initial_combatants = {
	WKR = {
		WRK = {
			PREV = { ADISCORD_copy_development_from_prev = yes }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_arsenal_command
		# WKR is dormant at campaign start, so it is outside the initial
		# every_country technology pass. Grant only the three variants consumed
		# below before adding equipment or loading the claimant air OOB.
		set_technology = {
			ADISCORD_tech_armored_carrier_program = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_semi_autonomous_combat_modules = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_reclaimed_jet_platforms = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_battlefield_attack_aircraft = 1
			popup = no
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
		add_manpower = 10000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 9000 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 90 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 140 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 36 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_combat_platform_2170 amount = 360 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163 amount = 400 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163 amount = 30 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 amount = 15 producer = WKR }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_wkr_home_guard = yes
		# One ready mobile group gives the worker claimant a counterattack reserve
		# without doubling the Vadl claimant's opening mobile reserve.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_mobile_group_deployed_v1 } }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_workerland_mobile_group_template = yes
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_mobile_group_deployed_v1
			32 = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"1st Workerland Mobile Group\" division_template = \"Workerland Mobile Group\" start_experience_factor = 0.35 start_equipment_factor = 0.90"
					owner = WKR
					count = 1
				}
			}
		}
		add_fuel = 10000
		load_oob = "WRK_vorkerland_collapse_air"
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_air_wings_deployed
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_air_mission_contract_v2_applied
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_air_sustainment_v1_applied
	}
	VAD = {
		WRK = {
			PREV = { ADISCORD_copy_development_from_prev = yes }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_land = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_armi_mobile_group_template = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_armored_core = yes
		load_oob = "VAD"
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_imperial_chancery
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
		add_manpower = 32000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 15000 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 60 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 24 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_combat_platform_2170 amount = 360 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163 amount = 400 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163 amount = 30 producer = VAD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 amount = 15 producer = VAD }
		add_fuel = 10000
		load_oob = "VAD_vorkerland_collapse_air"
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_air_wings_deployed
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_air_mission_contract_v2_applied
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_armi_mobile_group_template = yes
		75 = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"1st Armi Mobile Group\" division_template = \"Armi Mobile Group\" start_experience_factor = 0.35 start_equipment_factor = 0.90"
				owner = VAD
			}
		}
	}
	TVA = {
		WRK = {
			PREV = { ADISCORD_copy_development_from_prev = yes }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_civil_war_exhaustion = yes
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_last_stand
	}
	TGD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes }
	ZAO = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 4000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 8500 producer = ZAO }
	}
	PWR = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 8000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 16000 producer = PWR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 120 producer = PWR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 72 producer = PWR }
	}
	VLA = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 8000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 18000 producer = VLA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 120 producer = VLA }
	}
	NAM = {
		# Do not call prepare_conflict_country here: that cores every owned
		# state, including the overseas colonies the resource-war contract
		# keeps un-cored. The levy is a cash reserve for the starting army
		# until the extraction war loads its own OOB.
		add_manpower = 8000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 16000 producer = NAM }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 120 producer = NAM }
	}
	ROM = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 10000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 18000 producer = ROM }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 140 producer = ROM }
	}
	SOL = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5000 producer = SOL }
	}
	TRU = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_manpower = 11000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 20000 producer = TRU }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 160 producer = TRU }
	}
	# One northern bloc receives a temporary operational initiative. Together
	# with careful AI plans this creates a moving campaign instead of equal
	# militias burning organisation in permanent attacks.
	random_list = {
		34 = { ZAO = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 240 } } }
		33 = {
			WPA = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 240 } }
			WPS = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 240 } }
		}
		33 = {
			PSD = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 240 } }
			PWR = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 240 } }
		}
	}
}

# War launchers detach inherited factions, but retain a regional coalition
# while its accepted auxiliary and claimant leader still share that faction.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { tag = WRK }
			is_subject = no
			is_in_faction = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction }
			NOT = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
						faction_leader = { exists = yes ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
					}
					AND = {
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
						is_faction_leader = yes
						any_allied_country = {
							exists = yes
							is_in_faction_with = PREV
							has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
		else = { leave_faction = yes }
	}
}

# The normal ROM-DVA content producer is on_war. The delayed regional verifier
# calls this fallback once so receiver-order races cannot leave ROM without its
# local war content or its finite 180-day mission.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_assert_rom_dva_war_content_initialized = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = ROM
			ROM = {
				is_subject = no
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
			OR = {
				ROM = { has_war_with = DVA }
				NOT = { country_exists = DVA }
				DVA = { has_capitulated = yes }
				DVA = { is_subject = yes }
			}
		}
		ROM = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_initialize_war_content = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_war_with = DVA
					NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_valley_offensive_resolved }
					NOT = { has_active_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_break_valley_administration }
				}
				activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_break_valley_administration
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_regional_war_factions = {
	ZAO = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	WPA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	WPS = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	PSD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	PWR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	VLA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	TGD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	ROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	DVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	TRU = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
	ZTA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_teardown_confederation = {
	# Run after the partition exists. Each participant is freed through its actual
	# overlord, not an assumed WRK scope, and leaves any inherited faction. Tags
	# created by the partition therefore enter the conflict as independent states.
	WKR = {
		if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = WKR autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
	}
	VAD = {
		if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = VAD autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
