	}
	IBL = {
		if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = IBL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
	}
	if = { limit = { WKR = { is_faction_leader = yes } } WKR = { dismantle_faction = yes } }
}

# Shared builder and postcondition repair for independent regional war instances. It is
# called only by one-day delayed events and restores only a live canonical
# rivalry; an eliminated country can never be respawned by it.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_regional_wars = {
	# IVN owns the northern ceasefire/restoration chain while its operation is
	# active. Southern and local rivalries remain independent and still launch.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPA
				ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = WPA } }
				WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			ZAO = { diplomatic_relation = { country = WPA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			ZAO = { declare_war_on = { target = WPA type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPS
				ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = WPS } }
				WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			WPS = { diplomatic_relation = { country = ZAO relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = ZAO type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PSD
				ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } }
				PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			ZAO = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PSD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			ZAO = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PWR
				ZAO = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
				PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			PWR = { diplomatic_relation = { country = ZAO relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			PWR = { declare_war_on = { target = ZAO type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = WPA country_exists = PSD
				WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } }
				PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			WPA = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PSD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			WPA = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = WPA country_exists = PWR
				WPA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
				PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			WPA = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PWR relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			WPA = { declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = WPS country_exists = PSD
				WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } }
				PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			WPS = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PSD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = WPS country_exists = PWR
				WPS = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PWR } }
				PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			WPS = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PWR relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			WPS = { declare_war_on = { target = PWR type = annex_everything } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = PWR country_exists = PSD
				PWR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = PSD } }
				PSD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			}
			PWR = { diplomatic_relation = { country = PSD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
			PWR = { declare_war_on = { target = PSD type = annex_everything } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VLA country_exists = EBA
			VLA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = EBA } }
			EBA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		VLA = { diplomatic_relation = { country = EBA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		VLA = { declare_war_on = { target = EBA type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VLA country_exists = TGD
			VLA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = TGD } }
			TGD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		VLA = { diplomatic_relation = { country = TGD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		VLA = { declare_war_on = { target = TGD type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = TGD country_exists = EBA
			TGD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = EBA } }
			EBA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		TGD = { diplomatic_relation = { country = EBA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		TGD = { declare_war_on = { target = EBA type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = SOL country_exists = SRA
			SOL = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = SRA } }
			SRA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		SOL = { diplomatic_relation = { country = SRA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		SOL = { declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = SOL country_exists = CSL
			SOL = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = CSL } }
			CSL = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		SOL = { diplomatic_relation = { country = CSL relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		SOL = { declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = SRA country_exists = CSL
			SRA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = CSL } }
			CSL = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		SRA = { diplomatic_relation = { country = CSL relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		SRA = { declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = ROM country_exists = DVA
			ROM = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = DVA } }
			DVA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		ROM = { diplomatic_relation = { country = DVA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		ROM = { declare_war_on = { target = DVA type = annex_everything } }
		# Initialise from the declaration, not by stamping the flag alone.
		# Setting the flag without the body raced the delayed rom_tru.1
		# trigger and the next-day assert, so ROM kept neither its staff
		# idea nor the valley mission. The flag is the initialise effect's
		# own guard; the mission is retried by the delayed assert once
		# has_war_with is visible.
		ROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_initialize_war_content = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = TRU country_exists = ZTA
			TRU = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ZTA } }
			ZTA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		TRU = { diplomatic_relation = { country = ZTA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
		TRU = { declare_war_on = { target = ZTA type = annex_everything } }
	}
}

# The regional decision runs this once after faction membership has had a full
# day to leave the diplomatic cache. All rivalries are independent war objects,
# so the capitulation of one minor cannot white-peace the rest of its theatre.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_regional_fronts_after_detach = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_regional_wars = yes
	if = {
		limit = { IVN = { exists = yes } }
		IVN = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.64 days = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { RUS = { exists = yes } }
		RUS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.64 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# The parameterised local-war builder. The rivalry table lives in the single
# scripted trigger ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_local_attacker_of_PREV; this body is
# written once and covers every authored edge of the active bracket stage. A new
# rivalry costs one line of data and cannot drift away from the existence,
# subject, capitulation, pact and duplicate-war guards. Re-running the effect is
# a no-operation for an edge that already exists.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_local_bracket_wars = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_can_open_local_war = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_staged_local_belligerent = yes
		}
		every_other_country = {
			limit = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_can_open_local_war = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_staged_local_belligerent = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_local_attacker_of_PREV = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = PREV }
			}
			# An inherited confederation pact would otherwise refuse the
			# declaration for the whole first year of the collapse.
			diplomatic_relation = { country = PREV relation = non_aggression_pact active = no }
			declare_war_on = { target = PREV type = annex_everything }
		}
	}
}

# A local administration trades construction capacity for supply, organisation
# and reserves in the terrain it governs. The modifier follows the state, so a claimant
# that takes the ground does not inherit the defender's advantage.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_regional_autonomy = {
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = {
			is_core_of = PREV
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_autonomy } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_autonomy }
	}
}

# One shared grant for every autonomy holder. NAM is included because its
# extraction war is opened by the collapse and is fought at home against the
# Eflor-Azhar restitution alliance and the mainland rebellion at the same time.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_local_bracket_autonomy = {
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_can_open_local_war = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_holds_regional_autonomy = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_regional_autonomy = yes
	}
	# Afrela's volunteer corps is the only foreign force Macri can receive. A
	# single expedition modifier makes that contribution large enough to matter
	# instead of arriving as one understrength division.
	if = {
		limit = {
			PIV = { exists = yes }
			NOT = { PIV = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_macri_volunteer_corps } } }
		}
		PIV = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_macri_volunteer_corps } }
	}
}

# Central district stage. It runs from the same authorisation that unlocks the
# claimant war decisions, so the nine district governments open their own war
# before any claimant can reach them.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_central_local_brackets = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_stage_central }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_stage_central
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_local_bracket_wars = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_local_bracket_autonomy = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_announce_first_central_war = yes
	}
}

# Closed-zone stage. The six administrations are materialised on separate dates,
# so their bracket is opened once the whole cascade is complete.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_launch_closed_zone_local_brackets = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_cascade_complete
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_stage_closed_zone }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_stage_closed_zone
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_retry_used
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_local_bracket_wars = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_local_bracket_autonomy = yes
	}
}

# One bounded postcondition pass for either stage. A rejected declaration is
# retried exactly once; a terminal edge is never revived, because the builder
# only ever touches living, free, uncapitulated participants.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_local_bracket_wars = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_staged_local_war_graph_active_or_terminal = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_retry_used
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_retry_used } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_local_bracket_wars_retry_used
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_open_local_bracket_wars = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.92 days = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] A staged local rivalry could not be opened after one retry."
	}
}

# FROM is the selected claimant; ROOT is the regional winner. Establish faction
# membership before joining wars so delayed verification can resolve its host.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_coalition_of_FROM = {
	FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = no tag = WKR }
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = faction_vorkerland_emergency_coalition
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = no tag = VAD }
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = faction_vorkerland_central_administration_bloc
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = no }
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = faction_vorkerland_directorate_union
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_faction_leader = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } }
			add_to_faction = ROOT
		}
	}
	set_major = yes
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_splice_into_coalition_host_war = yes
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
	# Secured district archives endorse only the first coalition the district
	# joins; the flag survives leaving and rejoining.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAD_secure_administrative_archives
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_archives_endorsed }
			FROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_archives_endorsed
		FROM = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.05 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		}
	}
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.93 days = 1 }
}

# The faction identifies the host independently for each regional winner.
# Inside ROOT below, PREV is the enemy and PREV.PREV is the faction leader.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_splice_into_coalition_host_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
		faction_leader = {
			if = {
				limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes exists = yes has_war = yes }
				every_enemy_country = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } }
					ROOT = {
						add_to_war = {
							targeted_alliance = PREV.PREV
							enemy = PREV
							hostility_reason = asked_to_join
							single_target_only = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Postcondition and one bounded retry for the coalition splice. The winner must
# end up inside the host's faction and inside all of its current wars.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_coalition_membership = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
			is_in_faction = yes
			faction_leader = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
				exists = yes
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				NOT = {
					any_enemy_country = {
						NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used }
			is_in_faction = yes
			faction_leader = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes
				exists = yes
				has_war = yes
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_splice_into_coalition_host_war = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.93 days = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_join_paid
			add_political_power = 25
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_coalition_obligation = yes
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] A regional coalition entry failed its membership or war postcondition."
	}
	VAD_settle_coalition_guarantees = yes
}

# Start both bounded northern escalation checks from the verified launch path,
# never from startup or a recurring pulse.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_northern_escalation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_scheduled }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { IVN = { exists = yes } }
			IVN = {
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.83 days = 730 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.84 days = 1095 }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_scheduled
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { RUS = { exists = yes } }
			RUS = {
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.83 days = 730 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.84 days = 1095 }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_scheduled
		}
	}
}

# Select exactly one living canonical northern edge for the two-year initiative.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_select_northern_escalation_front = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPA ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = WPA } WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_zao_wpa
		ZAO = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = ZAO WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = ZAO } ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_wps_zao
		WPS = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PSD ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_zao_psd
		ZAO = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = PWR country_exists = ZAO PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = ZAO } ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_pwr_zao
		PWR = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PSD WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_wpa_psd
		WPA = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PWR WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PWR } PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_wpa_pwr
		WPA = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PSD WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_wps_psd
		WPS = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PWR WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PWR } PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_wps_pwr
		WPS = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { country_exists = PWR country_exists = PSD PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_pwr_psd
		PWR = { add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_operational_initiative days = 365 } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_1
	}
}

# The three-year stage only switches the verified AI policy; it neither creates
# nor resolves wars.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_intensify_surviving_northern_fronts = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = WPA ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = WPA } WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = ZAO WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = ZAO } ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = ZAO country_exists = PSD ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = PWR country_exists = ZAO PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = ZAO } ZAO = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PSD WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = WPA country_exists = PWR WPA = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PWR } PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PSD WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = WPS country_exists = PWR WPS = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PWR } PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
				AND = { country_exists = PWR country_exists = PSD PWR = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } has_war_with = PSD } PSD = { NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_escalation_stage_2
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_tva = {
	TVA = {
		transfer_state = 36
		transfer_state = 37
		transfer_state = 38
		transfer_state = 39
		transfer_state = 324
	}
	36 = { add_core_of = TVA set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	37 = { add_core_of = TVA set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	38 = { add_core_of = TVA set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	39 = { add_core_of = TVA set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	324 = { add_core_of = TVA set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	TVA = {
		set_politics = { ruling_party = technocracy elections_allowed = no }
		promote_character = {
			character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
			ideology = dorian_technocracy
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = technocracy
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Dorian_Worx
		}
		set_capital = { state = 36 }
		ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_industrial = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_energy = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_institutional = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_land = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_air = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_armored_core = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_directorate
		add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_ideological_fanaticism
		add_manpower = 22000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 12000 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 180 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 96 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0 amount = 90 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_combat_platform_2170 amount = 360 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163 amount = 400 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163 amount = 30 producer = TVA }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 amount = 15 producer = TVA }
		add_fuel = 10000
		load_oob = "TVA_vorkerland_collapse"
		load_oob = "TVA_vorkerland_collapse_air"
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_air_wings_deployed
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_air_mission_contract_v2_applied
	}
}

# A small committee of grid engineers and emergency planners seizes the
# Oitfort industrial district for Doctor Worx. WRK keeps its capital and
# remains a full claimant in the civil war.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_setup_wtd = {
	WTD = {
		transfer_state = 34
	}
	34 = { add_core_of = WTD set_state_controller_to = WTD }
	WTD = {
		promote_character = { character = WTD_Central_Engineering_Council ideology = technocracy_ideology }
		set_capital = { state = 34 }
		ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_industrial = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_energy = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		load_oob = "WTD_vorkerland_collapse"
		add_manpower = 7500
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 11000 producer = WTD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 80 producer = WTD }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 36 producer = WTD }
	}
}

# The Oitfort committee is Doctor Worx's technical protectorate. Normalize any
# stale subject/faction relation first, then bind it directly to TVA so the
# regional outcome is visible before the central showdown begins.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_align_wtd_with_worx = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WTD country_exists = TVA }
		WTD = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = WTD autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worx_aligned_technocrats
		}
		TVA = {
			puppet = WTD
			set_autonomy = { target = WTD autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.15 }
		}
	}
}

	310 = { add_core_of = SRA set_state_controller_to = SRA }
	SRA = {
		promote_character = { character = SRA_Helio_Marr ideology = humanism_ideology }
		set_capital = { state = 198 }
		ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
		ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_conflict_country = yes
		load_oob = "SRA_vorkerland_collapse"
		add_manpower = 3500
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5000 producer = SRA }
	}
}
