ADISCORD_vorkerland_finish_civil_war_exhaustion = {
	WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_civil_war_exhaustion = yes }
	event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_civil_war_exhaustion = yes }
	VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_civil_war_exhaustion = yes }
	TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_civil_war_exhaustion = yes }
}

# Central victory records the claimant and unlocks border-by-border
# reunification decisions. Normal capitulation and peace mechanics retain all
# actual state transfers; these effects deliberately annex and puppet nobody.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_worker_map = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	WKR = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_vlad_map = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	VAD = {
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad } set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_preserved }
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_missing } set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_unaccounted_for }
		if = { limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_killed } set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_martyr }
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_dorian_map = {
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier } }
	TVA = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
}

# --- diplomacy_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_record_regional_diplomacy_outcomes = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_sol = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_sra = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_csl = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_terminal_verified
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_vla = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_eba
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_tgd
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_vla
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_eba = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_vla
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_tgd
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_eba
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_tgd = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_vla
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_eba
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_tgd
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_terminal_verified
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_retire_dead_breakaway_claims = yes
}

# A breakaway of the collapse that no longer exists gives up its claims. Its
# cores would otherwise let the peace conference that ends the central war
# release it again as a puppet on part of a region it had already lost. SOL
# keeps its cores: the protectorate and the restoration release it by name.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_retire_dead_breakaway_claims = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = CSL } }
		104 = { remove_core_of = CSL }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = SRA } }
		198 = { remove_core_of = SRA }
		307 = { remove_core_of = SRA }
		310 = { remove_core_of = SRA }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = DVA } }
		144 = { remove_core_of = DVA }
		145 = { remove_core_of = DVA }
		319 = { remove_core_of = DVA }
		321 = { remove_core_of = DVA }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = ZTA } }
		199 = { remove_core_of = ZTA }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = EBA } }
		197 = { remove_core_of = EBA }
		311 = { remove_core_of = EBA }
		312 = { remove_core_of = EBA }
		313 = { remove_core_of = EBA }
		314 = { remove_core_of = EBA }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = TGD } }
		105 = { remove_core_of = TGD }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = WPA } }
		195 = { remove_core_of = WPA }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = WPS } }
		196 = { remove_core_of = WPS }
		322 = { remove_core_of = WPS }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = PSD } }
		93 = { remove_core_of = PSD }
		94 = { remove_core_of = PSD }
		194 = { remove_core_of = PSD }
		328 = { remove_core_of = PSD }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VHV } }
		315 = { remove_core_of = VHV }
		316 = { remove_core_of = VHV }
		317 = { remove_core_of = VHV }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = OSV } }
		318 = { remove_core_of = OSV }
		320 = { remove_core_of = OSV }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = NDN } }
		27 = { remove_core_of = NDN }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = SWB } }
		35 = { remove_core_of = SWB }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = REV } }
		82 = { remove_core_of = REV }
		323 = { remove_core_of = REV }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = EYR } }
		75 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
		102 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
		106 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
		109 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
		111 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
		325 = { remove_core_of = EYR }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = EGC } }
		81 = { remove_core_of = EGC }
		110 = { remove_core_of = EGC }
		124 = { remove_core_of = EGC }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = RIV } }
		79 = { remove_core_of = RIV }
		306 = { remove_core_of = RIV }
		308 = { remove_core_of = RIV }
		309 = { remove_core_of = RIV }
		327 = { remove_core_of = RIV }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = YOR } }
		102 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		107 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		108 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		109 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		111 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		121 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		122 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
		123 = { remove_core_of = YOR }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_vad_sol_alliance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			country_exists = VAD
			country_exists = SOL
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_sol_invitation_intent
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_solland_liaison_prepared
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sol
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_accepted }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_resolved }
			SOL = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_pending
		set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_invitation_dispatch_guard days = 3 }
		SOL = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.2 days = 1 } }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_offer_wkr_vla_alliance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = VLA
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_vla_invitation_intent
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_volnograd_winner_vla
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_resolved }
			VLA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_pending
		set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_invitation_dispatch_guard days = 3 }
		VLA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.3 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# The seven-day public decision reserves the diplomacy lane immediately. Its
# remove/cancel effects then hand the lane either to the active war or back to
# the central-showdown controller without leaving stale attempt state behind.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_vad_solar_intervention = {
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_fresh_release
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_cancel_vad_solar_intervention_reservation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_paid NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active } }
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_paid
		add_political_power = 50
	}
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solar_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_showdown_requested }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active }
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			NOT = { has_war = yes }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_has_solar_intervention_border = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_attempted
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_sra
				SRA = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SRA }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
			SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = take_state_focus generator = { 76 104 198 307 310 } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.6 days = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_solar_winner_csl
				CSL = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CSL }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
			CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = take_state_focus generator = { 76 104 198 307 310 } }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.6 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_cancel_vad_solar_intervention_reservation = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_vad_solar_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending }
			OR = {
				AND = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra has_war_with = SRA }
				AND = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl has_war_with = CSL }
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_paid
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
				SRA = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SRA }
			}
			SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = take_state_focus generator = { 76 104 198 307 310 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
				CSL = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
				NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CSL }
			}
			CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = take_state_focus generator = { 76 104 198 307 310 } }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.9 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending }
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_failed_vad_solar_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] VAD Solar intervention failed after one retry."
	}
}

# A failed claimant cannot leave the central-preparation gate permanently
# occupied. This is safe from capitulation, peace, and bounded verifier hooks.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_failed_vad_solar_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed
				NOT = { country_exists = VAD }
				VAD = { has_capitulated = yes }
				AND = {
					VAD = {
						exists = yes
						NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
						NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending }
					}
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified }
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending }
					OR = {
						AND = {
							has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
							OR = {
								NOT = { country_exists = SRA }
								VAD = { NOT = { has_war_with = SRA } }
							}
						}
						AND = {
							has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
							OR = {
								NOT = { country_exists = CSL }
								VAD = { NOT = { has_war_with = CSL } }
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							OR = {
								has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
								has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		VAD = {
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_paid } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_paid add_political_power = 50 }
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_fresh_release
		VAD = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_failed
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_restore_sol_as_vad_puppet = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized }
			owns_state = 76 controls_state = 76
			owns_state = 104 controls_state = 104
			owns_state = 198 controls_state = 198
			owns_state = 307 controls_state = 307
			owns_state = 310 controls_state = 310
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified }
		}
		76 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		104 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		198 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		307 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		310 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { country_exists = SOL } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_fresh_release
			release_autonomy = {
				target = SOL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet
				freedom_level = 0.10
			}
			SOL = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = SOL_vorkerland_restoration_administration
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			SOL = {
				if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
				if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			}
			puppet = SOL
			set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_puppet freedom_level = 0.10 }
			SOL = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
				transfer_state = 76
				transfer_state = 104
				transfer_state = 198
				transfer_state = 307
				transfer_state = 310
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.5 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.7 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized }
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_failed_vad_solar_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] SOL materialization failed after one retry."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_sol_restoration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
			SOL = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject_of = VAD
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				owns_state = 76 controls_state = 76
				owns_state = 104 controls_state = 104
				owns_state = 198 controls_state = 198
				owns_state = 307 controls_state = 307
				owns_state = 310 controls_state = 310
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_fresh_release }
			SOL = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_initialized } }
					ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
					ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
					ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
					load_oob = "SOL"
					set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_initialized
				}
			}
		}
		SOL = {
			set_capital = { state = 76 }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restored_by_vad
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_independence_cosmetic = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_accepted
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_sra
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_target_csl
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_verify_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_fresh_release
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.8 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_failed_vad_solar_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] SOL restoration failed after one retry."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_arm_wkr_solar_counter_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_has_solar_counter_border = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solar_counter_intervention_ready
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_showdown_requested
		unlock_decision_tooltip = WKR_solar_counter_activate_plan
		create_wargoal = { type = annex_everything target = VAD expire = 30 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4 days = 21 }
	}
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_regional_allies_to_showdown = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_sol_alliance_accepted
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_verified
			}
			country_exists = SOL
			country_exists = VAD
		}
		SOL = {
			if = { limit = { VAD = { has_war_with = WKR } NOT = { has_war_with = WKR } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAD enemy = WKR hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
			if = { limit = { VAD = { has_war_with = TVA } NOT = { has_war_with = TVA } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAD enemy = TVA hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted
			country_exists = VLA
			country_exists = WKR
		}
		VLA = {
			if = { limit = { WKR = { has_war_with = VAD } NOT = { has_war_with = VAD } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = WKR enemy = VAD hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
			if = { limit = { WKR = { has_war_with = TVA } NOT = { has_war_with = TVA } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = WKR enemy = TVA hostility_reason = asked_to_join } }
		}
	}
	# Mandatory majors preserve coalition fronts after their leader capitulates.
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
			OR = { is_in_faction_with = WKR is_in_faction_with = VAD is_in_faction_with = TVA }
		}
		set_major = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_mandatory_major
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.93 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_disengage_regional_allies_before_formation = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = SOL }
		SOL = {
			if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR has_war_with = WKR } white_peace = WKR }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA has_war_with = TVA } white_peace = TVA }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VLA }
		VLA = {
			if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR has_war_with = WKR } white_peace = WKR }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA has_war_with = TVA } white_peace = TVA }
		}
	}
}

# The WKR air programme starts production without granting a second starting
# air force. Repeated focus completion must not duplicate the production lines.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_bootstrap_wkr_air_sustainment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_air_sustainment_bootstrap_applied }
		}
		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163 creator = "WKR" }
			requested_factories = 2
			progress = 0.30
			efficiency = 55
		}
		add_equipment_production = {
			equipment = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 creator = "WKR" }
			requested_factories = 1
			progress = 0.25
			efficiency = 50
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163 amount = 24 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170 amount = 12 producer = WKR }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_air_sustainment_bootstrap_applied
	}
}

# Build a shallow redoubt in one live-front state when the focus lands. The
# bounded random state keeps the focus useful without sealing the entire front
# behind an instant fort belt. Before a front exists, one fixed southern home
# redoubt guarantees that the early AI focus still has a physical reward.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_build_wkr_live_frontline_fortifications = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						controller = { has_war_with = WKR }
					}
				}
			}
			random_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					any_neighbor_state = {
						controller = { has_war_with = WKR }
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = bunker
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
					province = {
						all_provinces = yes
						limit_to_border = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { controls_state = 33 }
			33 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = bunker
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
					province = 3248
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Launch every still-valid Solarino war independently. The next-day verifier
# retries only missing edges once; no pulse or country-wide polling owns this
# operation.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_wkr_solyarino_intervention = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solyarino_counter_active
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_verified
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_carried
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_returned
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed

	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			OR = {
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			}
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_reserved }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solar_intervention_active }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_restoration_pending }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_has_solyarino_intervention_border = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_sol_target = yes }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
			SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SOL type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_sra_target = yes }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
			SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_csl_target = yes }
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
			CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
					has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
					has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
				}
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.10 days = 1 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solyarino_counter = yes
		}
		else = {
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_wkr_solyarino_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol }
				NOT = { country_exists = SOL }
				SOL = { has_capitulated = yes }
				SOL = { is_subject = yes }
				has_war_with = SOL
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra }
				NOT = { country_exists = SRA }
				SRA = { has_capitulated = yes }
				SRA = { is_subject = yes }
				has_war_with = SRA
			}
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl }
				NOT = { country_exists = CSL }
				CSL = { has_capitulated = yes }
				CSL = { is_subject = yes }
				has_war_with = CSL
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_verified
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solyarino_counter = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_sol_target = yes
			}
			SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SOL type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_sra_target = yes
			}
			SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_valid_csl_target = yes
			}
			CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.11 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_wkr_solyarino_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] WKR Solarino intervention failed after one retry."
	}
}

# Once the shared claimant war is live, VAD may contest the same unaligned
# southern governments. This response never creates a separate claimant edge.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solyarino_counter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solyarino_counter_active }
			country_exists = VAD
			VAD = { exists = yes is_subject = no has_war_with = WKR NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol }
					country_exists = SOL
					SOL = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
					NOT = { has_war_with = SOL }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SOL }
				}
				SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
				declare_war_on = { target = SOL type = annex_everything }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra }
					country_exists = SRA
					SRA = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
					NOT = { has_war_with = SRA }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = SRA }
				}
				SRA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
				declare_war_on = { target = SRA type = annex_everything }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl }
					country_exists = CSL
					CSL = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
					NOT = { has_war_with = CSL }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = CSL }
				}
				CSL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
				declare_war_on = { target = CSL type = annex_everything }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solyarino_counter_active
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_wkr_solyarino_intervention = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			OR = {
				NOT = { country_exists = WKR }
				WKR = { OR = { is_subject = yes has_capitulated = yes } }
				WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
				# Terminal release. Both outcome branches require the Solarino wars to
				# finish, so a grinding side war would otherwise hold this flag forever
				# and block reunification for every claimant, not just WKR. Once the
				# showdown has left one survivor the intervention can no longer decide
				# anything, and an in-flight protectorate handover is left untouched.
				AND = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_single_surviving_claimant = yes
					NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending }
				}
			}
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solyarino_counter_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WKR }
			WKR = {
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_carried
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_returned
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { WKR = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
			WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { VAD = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
			VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { TVA = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } }
			TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes }
		}
	}
}

# A capitulation or peace callback schedules this only once until the next-day
# event consumes the guard. It is deliberately country-scoped and pulse-free.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_queue_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.12 days = 1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_check_wkr_solyarino_outcome = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_wars_finished = yes
			owns_state = 76 controls_state = 76
			owns_state = 104 controls_state = 104
			owns_state = 198 controls_state = 198
			owns_state = 307 controls_state = 307
			owns_state = 310 controls_state = 310
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.13 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_wars_finished = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_wkr_solyarino_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] WKR Solarino peace ended without the complete five-state package."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_materialize_sol_worker_protectorate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verified }
			owns_state = 76 controls_state = 76
			owns_state = 104 controls_state = 104
			owns_state = 198 controls_state = 198
			owns_state = 307 controls_state = 307
			owns_state = 310 controls_state = 310
		}
		# Gordon belongs to the canonical SOL history object. If WKR defeated a
		# splinter after SOL had already disappeared, explicitly recover him from
		# that dormant object before releasing the canonical shell.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_carried } }
			SOL = {
				set_nationality = {
					target_country = WKR
					character = WRK_Richard_Gordon
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_carried
		}
		76 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		104 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		198 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		307 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		310 = { add_core_of = SOL }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { country_exists = SOL } }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release
			release_autonomy = {
				target = SOL
				autonomy_state = autonomy_district_in_Vorkerland
				freedom_level = 0.10
			}
			SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
		}
		else = {
			SOL = {
				if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
				if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			}
			puppet = SOL
			set_autonomy = { target = SOL autonomy_state = autonomy_district_in_Vorkerland freedom_level = 0.10 }
			SOL = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes
				transfer_state = 76
				transfer_state = 104
				transfer_state = 198
				transfer_state = 307
				transfer_state = 310
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = SOL
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_carried
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_returned }
			}
			set_nationality = {
				target_country = SOL
				character = WRK_Richard_Gordon
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_returned
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.15 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.14 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized }
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_wkr_solyarino_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] SOL worker protectorate materialization failed after one retry."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_sol_worker_protectorate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_gordon_returned
			SOL = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject_of = WKR
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				owns_state = 76 controls_state = 76
				owns_state = 104 controls_state = 104
				owns_state = 198 controls_state = 198
				owns_state = 307 controls_state = 307
				owns_state = 310 controls_state = 310
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release }
			SOL = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_initialized } }
					ADISCORD_grant_2150_technology_baseline = yes
					ADISCORD_grant_technology_profile_fragment_low_tech = yes
					ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
					load_oob = "SOL"
					set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_initialized
				}
			}
		}
		SOL = {
			set_capital = { state = 76 }
			set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_government_installed } }
				promote_character = {
					character = WRK_Richard_Gordon
					ideology = pragmatism_ideology
				}
				set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_government_installed
			}
			set_cosmetic_tag = SOL_vorkerland_worker_protectorate
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_protectorate
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_solyarino_counter_active
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_fresh_release
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sol
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_sra
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_target_csl
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_pending
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_verify_retry
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_outcome_check_pending
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.16 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_pending
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_materialized
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_verify_retry
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_solyarino_intervention_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_sol_worker_protectorate_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_wkr_solyarino_intervention = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][DIPLOMACY] SOL worker protectorate verification failed after one retry."
	}
}

# During the central war, neutral pockets may be attacked without adjacency.
# Coalition members are reached through their existing war; self and allies
# are never separate targets.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_remaining_central_fronts = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			is_subject = no
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EYR = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			EYR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = EYR type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EGC = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			EGC = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = EGC type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				RIV = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			RIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = RIV type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				REV = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			REV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = REV type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				YOR = {
					is_in_faction = no

					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }

		}
		if = {

					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes

				}
			}
			SWB = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }

					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = WKR type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				TVA = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			declare_war_on = { target = TVA type = annex_everything }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TVA
			is_subject = no
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EYR = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			EYR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = EYR type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				EGC = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			EGC = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = EGC type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				RIV = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}
			RIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = RIV type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				REV = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }

				}

				}
			}
			YOR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = YOR type = annex_everything }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NDN = {
					is_in_faction = no
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
					NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT }
				}
			}

# --- doctrine_effects ---
# Doctrine strength changes only when doctrine or legitimacy changes.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_doctrine_dynamic_state = {
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# VARIANT DOCTRINE
	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
	# Zero first so a claimant that has not declared still reads defined
	# values. The scale is 0.6 + 0.4 * legitimacy: a programme at +1
	# legitimacy is at full published strength, one at 0 is three-fifths,
	# and one that has lost the argument is a fifth of what it claimed.
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy }
	multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale = 0.4 }
	add_to_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale = 0.6 }
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale min = 0.20 max = 1 }

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_neo_vorkerist }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower = 0.015 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = -0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_utilitarian }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = -0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_imperial }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org = 0.04 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower value = -0.01 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_joint }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = -0.04 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = 0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_throughput }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_breakthrough = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = -0.06 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_preservation }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_defence = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_scale }
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_attack value = -0.06 }
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_stability value = 0.03 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_manpower value = VAD_doctrine_conscription }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_doctrine_org value = VAD_doctrine_army_org_factor }
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_focus_doctrine = yes
}

# Attach the claimant's doctrine overlay when a programme is published.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_focus_doctrine = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_neo_vorkerist
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_variant_utilitarian
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_doctrine } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_doctrine }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_imperial
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_variant_joint
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_doctrine } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_doctrine }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TVA
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_throughput
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_variant_preservation
			}
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine }
	}
}

# --- emergency_template_effects ---
# Idempotent runtime guarantees for every late-spawn civil-war template.
# These effects run in country scope before any capital/state scope is entered.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_emergency_militia_template = {
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Emergency Militia" } }
		division_template = { name = "Emergency Militia" template_counter = 7 division_names_group = VORKERLAND_EMERGENCY_INF_01 is_locked = yes force_allow_recruiting = yes regiments = { ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 0 } ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 1 } ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 2 } } }
	}
}
