# --- focus_decision_effects ---

# One receipt owns payment until delivery, cancellation or country handoff.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_clear_programme = {
	clear_variable = WKR_programme_deposit
	clr_country_flag = WKR_rifle_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_staff_course_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_support_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_workshop_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_ration_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_rail_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_artillery_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_pattern_contract_pending
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refund_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = WKR_programme_deposit } }
		add_political_power = WKR_programme_deposit
		hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_clear_programme = yes }
	}
}

# Runs in the unified country's scope; later callbacks on WKR find no receipt.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_settle_programme_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_variable = WKR_programme_deposit } }
		add_political_power = WKR.WKR_programme_deposit
		WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_clear_programme = yes }
	}
}

# A central campaign is launched as one operational wave. All targets were
# recorded before this delayed hook, so declarations in this block cannot
# change which other neighbours belong to the same wave.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_launch_central_minor_wave = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending
			OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA tag = WRK }
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr any_neighbor_country = { tag = EYR } EYR = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = EYR type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc any_neighbor_country = { tag = EGC } EGC = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = EGC type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv any_neighbor_country = { tag = RIV } RIV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = RIV type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev any_neighbor_country = { tag = REV } REV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = REV type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor any_neighbor_country = { tag = YOR } YOR = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = YOR type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn any_neighbor_country = { tag = NDN } NDN = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = NDN type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb any_neighbor_country = { tag = SWB } SWB = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = SWB type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv any_neighbor_country = { tag = VHV } VHV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = VHV type = annex_everything } }
		if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv any_neighbor_country = { tag = OSV } OSV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = OSV type = annex_everything } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.8 days = 1 }
	}
	else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_cleanup_central_minor_front = yes }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_clear_central_minor_assignment = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_cleanup_central_minor_front = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_retry
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_deadline_active
	remove_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_front_deadline
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_clear_central_minor_assignment = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_arm_central_minor_deadline = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_retry
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_deadline_active
	activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_front_deadline
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_finish_central_minor_wave = {
	set_country_flag = {
		flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_recovery_cooldown
		days = 14
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_cleanup_central_minor_front = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_prune_failed_central_minor_wave_targets = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr NOT = { has_war_with = EYR } EYR = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc NOT = { has_war_with = EGC } EGC = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv NOT = { has_war_with = RIV } RIV = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev NOT = { has_war_with = REV } REV = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor NOT = { has_war_with = YOR } YOR = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn NOT = { has_war_with = NDN } NDN = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb NOT = { has_war_with = SWB } SWB = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv NOT = { has_war_with = VHV } VHV = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv NOT = { has_war_with = OSV } OSV = { exists = yes is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } } } clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_retry_central_minor_wave_declarations = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr NOT = { has_war_with = EYR } any_neighbor_country = { tag = EYR } EYR = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = EYR type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc NOT = { has_war_with = EGC } any_neighbor_country = { tag = EGC } EGC = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = EGC type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv NOT = { has_war_with = RIV } any_neighbor_country = { tag = RIV } RIV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = RIV type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev NOT = { has_war_with = REV } any_neighbor_country = { tag = REV } REV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = REV type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor NOT = { has_war_with = YOR } any_neighbor_country = { tag = YOR } YOR = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = YOR type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn NOT = { has_war_with = NDN } any_neighbor_country = { tag = NDN } NDN = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = NDN type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb NOT = { has_war_with = SWB } any_neighbor_country = { tag = SWB } SWB = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = SWB type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv NOT = { has_war_with = VHV } any_neighbor_country = { tag = VHV } VHV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = VHV type = annex_everything } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv NOT = { has_war_with = OSV } any_neighbor_country = { tag = OSV } OSV = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = WKR has_war_with = VAD has_war_with = TVA } } } } declare_war_on = { target = OSV type = annex_everything } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_confirm_central_minor_wave_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending
			OR = {
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr has_war_with = EYR }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc has_war_with = EGC }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv has_war_with = RIV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev has_war_with = REV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor has_war_with = YOR }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn has_war_with = NDN }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb has_war_with = SWB }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv has_war_with = VHV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv has_war_with = OSV }
			}
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr } has_war_with = EYR NOT = { country_exists = EYR } EYR = { has_capitulated = yes } EYR = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc } has_war_with = EGC NOT = { country_exists = EGC } EGC = { has_capitulated = yes } EGC = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv } has_war_with = RIV NOT = { country_exists = RIV } RIV = { has_capitulated = yes } RIV = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev } has_war_with = REV NOT = { country_exists = REV } REV = { has_capitulated = yes } REV = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor } has_war_with = YOR NOT = { country_exists = YOR } YOR = { has_capitulated = yes } YOR = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn } has_war_with = NDN NOT = { country_exists = NDN } NDN = { has_capitulated = yes } NDN = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb } has_war_with = SWB NOT = { country_exists = SWB } SWB = { has_capitulated = yes } SWB = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv } has_war_with = VHV NOT = { country_exists = VHV } VHV = { has_capitulated = yes } VHV = { is_subject = yes } }
			OR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv } has_war_with = OSV NOT = { country_exists = OSV } OSV = { has_capitulated = yes } OSV = { is_subject = yes } }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_arm_central_minor_deadline = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_pending
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_retry }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_retryable_recorded_central_minor_front = yes
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_retry_central_minor_wave_declarations = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.9 days = 1 }
	}
	else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_finish_central_minor_wave = yes }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_confirm_central_minor_wave_retry = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_prune_failed_central_minor_wave_targets = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_minor_campaign_phase_available = yes
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_launch_retry
			OR = {
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_eyr has_war_with = EYR }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_egc has_war_with = EGC }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_riv has_war_with = RIV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_rev has_war_with = REV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_yor has_war_with = YOR }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_ndn has_war_with = NDN }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_swb has_war_with = SWB }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_vhv has_war_with = VHV }
				AND = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_target_osv has_war_with = OSV }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_arm_central_minor_deadline = yes
	}
	else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_finish_central_minor_wave = yes }
}

# The 240-day bound is diagnostic only. Central districts are genuine
# belligerents, so a long campaign must end through normal capitulation rather
# than a scripted state transfer, white peace, or annexation. Retire only this
# wave's bookkeeping; the live war itself continues and blocks the next wave.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_resolve_central_minor_wave_deadline = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_deadline_active
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = {
			flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_minor_front_protracted
			days = 180
			value = 1
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND] Central district campaign exceeded 240 days; the live war remains unresolved."
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_finish_central_minor_wave = yes
}

# Exact handoff hook from the visible consolidation stage to the existing
# simultaneous WKR-VAD-TVA edge queue. No central minor may remain live.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_schedule_final_showdown = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_can_commit_to_showdown = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_showdown_requested
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4 days = 1 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_wkr_retreat_levy_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_war = yes
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			surrender_progress > 0.20
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_retreat_levies_unlocked
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_retreat_levy_1_raised }
			has_manpower > 999
			capital_scope = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_retreat_levy_1_raised
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_workerland_militia_template = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"First Retreat Levy\" division_template = \"Workerland Militia\" start_experience_factor = 0.05 start_equipment_factor = 0.55"
				owner = PREV
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_raise_wkr_retreat_levy_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_war = yes
			NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			surrender_progress > 0.45

			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_retreat_levy_1_raised
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_retreat_levy_2_raised }
			has_manpower > 749
			capital_scope = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_retreat_levy_2_raised
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_workerland_militia_template = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"Last Worker Reserve\" division_template = \"Workerland Militia\" start_experience_factor = 0.02 start_equipment_factor = 0.45"
				owner = PREV
			}
		}
	}
}

		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_retreat_levy_2_raised
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_tva_collapse_militia_template = yes }
		capital_scope = {
			create_unit = {
				division = "name = \"Last Technical Reserve\" division_template = \"Collapse Militia\" start_experience_factor = 0.02 start_equipment_factor = 0.45"
				owner = PREV
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_send_vla_support_tranche_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_war = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_vla_alliance_accepted
			has_manpower > 2999
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 15000 } }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < 150 } }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_tranche_1_sent }
			VLA = {
				exists = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				has_war = yes
				OR = { is_in_faction_with = ROOT is_subject_of = ROOT }
			}
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -3000 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -30 }
		VLA = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 3000 producer = WKR }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 producer = WKR }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_allied_supply_advisers days = 60 }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_tranche_1_sent
		set_country_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vla_support_cooldown days = 90 value = 1 }
	}
}

# --- force_design_effects ---
# Roll once at outbreak, after country creation and before the first fronts.
# WRK may still hold the player awaiting the claimant-selection event.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_roll_central_initiative = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative_rolled }
			NOT = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
					exists = yes
					is_ai = no
				}
			}
			WKR = {
				exists = yes
				is_ai = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
			VAD = {
				exists = yes
				is_ai = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
			TVA = {
				exists = yes
				is_ai = yes
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative_rolled
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				WKR = {
					add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative days = 730 }
				}
			}
			1 = {
				VAD = {
					add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative days = 730 }
				}
			}
			1 = {
				TVA = {
					add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative days = 730 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Fresh-campaign AI bootstrap. collapse.2 calls this once for each main
# claimant immediately after the verified partition enters the wartime phase.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_bootstrap_ai_force_designs = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_ai = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_force_designs_bootstrapped }
			OR = { tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_force_designs_bootstrapped
		set_technology = {
			ADISCORD_tech_armored_carrier_program = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_semi_autonomous_combat_modules = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_reclaimed_jet_platforms = 1
			ADISCORD_tech_battlefield_attack_aircraft = 1
		}
		# One bounded delivery lets the AI build the authored 4+6 armoured group
		# and leaves a small reinforcement reserve while production ramps up.
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_combat_platform_archetype < 120 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = ADISCORD_combat_platform_2170
				amount = 160
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5000 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_0
				amount = 5000
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment < 40 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment_0
				amount = 40
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < 80 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment_1
				amount = 80
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { artillery_equipment < 12 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = artillery_equipment_1
				amount = 12
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_fighter_archetype < 12 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = ADISCORD_fighter_airframe_2163
				amount = 12
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_armored_carrier_archetype < 200 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163
				amount = 260
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_cas_archetype < 6 } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = ADISCORD_cas_airframe_2170
				amount = 6
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- itoran_armistice_effects ---
# The authored pre-collapse map records Itora's earlier border victory through
# PWR ownership of the three treaty states. Only that intact fresh-start
# arrangement receives the armistice; collapse expires it permanently.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_apply_precollapse_itoran_armistice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = PWR
			country_exists = IVN
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { exists = yes }
			owns_state = 90
			owns_state = 91
			owns_state = 93
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_expired }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_active
		90 = {
			set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
			set_demilitarized_zone = yes
		}
		91 = {
			set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
			set_demilitarized_zone = yes
		}
		93 = {
			set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
			set_demilitarized_zone = yes
		}
	}
}

# collapse.1 calls this immediately after recording collapse_started. The
# permanent guard prevents every later startup or intervention from restoring
# the treaty, while the explicit states keep ownership transfers irrelevant.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_expire_precollapse_itoran_armistice = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_expired }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_expired
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_active
		90 = {
			set_demilitarized_zone = no
			clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
		}
		91 = {
			set_demilitarized_zone = no
			clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
		}
		93 = {
			set_demilitarized_zone = no
			clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_precollapse_itoran_armistice_dmz
		}
	}
}

# --- party_identity_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_collegial_government = yes }
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = pragmatism
			name = VAD_staff_party
			long_name = VAD_staff_party_long
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started }
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = pragmatism
					name = VAD_vorkerland_imperial_party_wrk_subject
					long_name = VAD_vorkerland_imperial_party_wrk_subject_long
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_party_name = {
					ideology = pragmatism
					name = VAD_pragmatism_party
					long_name = VAD_pragmatism_party_long
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_started }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = VAD_vorkerland_imperial_party
				long_name = VAD_vorkerland_imperial_party_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = VAD_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = VAD_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = ZAO }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = ZAO_pragmatism_party
				long_name = ZAO_pragmatism_party_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = ZAO_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = ZAO_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = PWR }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = PWR_pragmatism_party
				long_name = PWR_pragmatism_party_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = technocracy
				name = PWR_technocracy_party_wrk_subject
				long_name = PWR_technocracy_party_wrk_subject_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = PWR_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = PWR_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = technocracy
				name = PWR_technocracy_party
				long_name = PWR_technocracy_party_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = VLA }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = VLA_pragmatism_party
				long_name = VLA_pragmatism_party_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = VLA_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = VLA_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = ROM }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = ROM_pragmatism_party
				long_name = ROM_pragmatism_party_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = etatism
				name = ROM_etatism_party_wrk_subject
				long_name = ROM_etatism_party_wrk_subject_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = ROM_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = ROM_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = etatism
				name = ROM_etatism_party
				long_name = ROM_etatism_party_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = SOL }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = SOL_pragmatism_party
				long_name = SOL_pragmatism_party_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = SOL_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = SOL_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { tag = TRU }
		if = {
			limit = { OR = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation is_subject_of = WKR } }
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = TRU_pragmatism_party
				long_name = TRU_pragmatism_party_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = chauvinism
				name = TRU_chauvinism_party_wrk_subject
				long_name = TRU_chauvinism_party_wrk_subject_long
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = pragmatism
				name = TRU_pragmatism_party_independent
				long_name = TRU_pragmatism_party_independent_long
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = chauvinism
				name = TRU_chauvinism_party
				long_name = TRU_chauvinism_party_long
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_all_party_identities = {
	VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
	ZAO = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
	PWR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
	VLA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
	ROM = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }
	SOL = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_sync_party_identity = yes }

}

# --- phase_effects ---
ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_phase_flags = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_prewar
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_collapse
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_preparation
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_central_showdown
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_reunification
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_postwar_integration
}

# Attacker attrition has no remove_trigger. Country overlays survive annexation
# and tag changes unless they are taken off here.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_retire_civil_war_modifiers = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative_rolled
	every_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_civil_war_attrition } }
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_theatre
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR } WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_claimant_war_modifiers = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD } VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_claimant_war_modifiers = yes } }
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_claimant_war_modifiers = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_claimant_war_modifiers = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_retire_district_oaths = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_clear_claimant_war_modifiers = {
	WKR_close_administration = yes
	VAD_close_administration = yes
	TVA_close_administration = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_cancel_field_trial = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measured_republic_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measured_republic_dynamic }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative }
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_initiative
	}
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_dynamic } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_dynamic } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_doctrine } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_doctrine } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_doctrine } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_doctrine } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_war_economy } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_war_economy } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_war_economy } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_war_economy } }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_theatre_priority }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_wkr } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_wkr }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_vad } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_vad }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_tva } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_against_tva }
	if = { limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence } remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_organized_defence }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_focus_lifecycle = {
	EYR = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	EGC = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	RIV = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	YOR = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { exists = yes } }
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		WKR = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		VAD = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		TVA = { focus_unlock = yes mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = STP }
		STP = { mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }
	}
}
