ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_collapse = {
	EYR = { load_focus_tree = { tree = generic_focus keep_completed = yes } }
	EGC = { load_focus_tree = { tree = generic_focus keep_completed = yes } }
	RIV = { load_focus_tree = { tree = generic_focus keep_completed = yes } }
	YOR = { load_focus_tree = { tree = generic_focus keep_completed = yes } }
	if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = WRK_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes }
	NAM = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = NAM_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	DAN = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = DAN_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	ZAO = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = ZAO_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	PWR = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = PWR_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	VLA = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VLA_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	ROM = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = ROM_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	SOL = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = SOL_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	TRU = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = TRU_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	WCG = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = WCG_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	EYR = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = EYR_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	EGC = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = EGC_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	RIV = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = RIV_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	YOR = { if = { limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = YOR_confederation } drop_cosmetic_tag = yes } }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_collapse = yes
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_final_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized_verified
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_war_outbreak_scheduled
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_assertion_started_v2
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_verified_v2
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_check_pending_v2
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_retry_v2
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_degraded_v2
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_temporary_claimant_cores = yes
}

# Ratification belongs to the centre and each autonomous administration.
# The regional guarantees do not decide who the player controls after the split.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_resolve_prewar_compact = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_compact_committed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5000 producer = WRK }
		}
	}
	# Each district ratifies independently; a refusal cannot erase another pact.
	EYR = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_compact_committed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = EYR }
		}
	}
	EGC = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_compact_committed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = EGC }
		}
	}
	RIV = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_compact_committed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = RIV }
		}
	}
	YOR = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_district_compact_committed
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received }
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_compact_supply_received
			add_stability = 0.03
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = YOR }
		}
	}
}

# Prewar reserve caches. COUNTRY WRK or a district; FROM is the target state.
# A cache belongs to the state, not to the country that stocked it: at the
# war outbreak it is delivered to the state's owner after the partition.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reserve_cache = {
	add_political_power = -25
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -1500 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_caches_placed value = 1 }
	FROM = {
		set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache
		add_to_array = { array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_caches value = THIS }
	}
}

# STATE scope. Mirrors the deterministic partition in apply_initial_map and its
# setup effects; the settlement itself reads the actual owner.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_recipient_tt = {
	if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 32 state = 33 state = 200 state = 201 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_wkr_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 36 state = 37 state = 38 state = 39 state = 324 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_tva_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 75 state = 106 state = 107 state = 121 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_vad_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 102 state = 109 state = 111 state = 325 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_eyr_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 81 state = 110 state = 124 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_egc_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 79 state = 306 state = 308 state = 309 state = 327 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_riv_tt
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { OR = { state = 108 state = 122 state = 123 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_to_yor_tt
	}
}

# Called once from the war-outbreak event. A cache still being stocked yields
# its rifles but not the reservists, who were never registered.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_settle_reserve_caches = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_caches
		owner = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1500 }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_ready } }
				add_manpower = 1500
			}
		}
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_cache_ready
	}
	clear_array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_reserve_caches
}

# WRK is annexed after the split, so completed prewar focuses alone cannot
# carry its country-scoped rewards into the wartime claimant. Regrant the
# bounded preparation package once from completed-focus truth; the country
# flag makes the bridge idempotent across the bounded cache retries.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_wrk_prewar_preparations = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_prewar_preparations_inherited_v1 }
		}
		WKR = { save_event_target_as = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir }
		if = {
			limit = { 40 = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			VAD = { save_event_target_as = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { 40 = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron value = 3 compare = equals } } }
			TVA = { save_event_target_as = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_measure_confederation_fault_lines }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 75
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_convene_council_of_republics }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 50
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_inventory_emergency_stores }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 9000 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_bind_districts_to_charter }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_stability = 0.04
				add_command_power = 10
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_disperse_railway_reserves }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 10 producer = WKR }
				army_experience = 20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_reopen_emergency_workshops }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = 100
					producer = WRK
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_collect_officer_returns }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_manpower = 1000
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_issue_continuity_orders }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_command_power = 20
				army_experience = 15
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_place_reserves_under_worker }
			WKR = {
				add_manpower = 1000
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5400 producer = WKR }
				add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_loyal_republics_mobilized days = 70 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK_capability_emergency_compact = yes
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_compact_ratified
			}
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_stability = 0.03
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5000 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_lean value = -1 compare = equals } }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 40
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 9000 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		# Tree of the chosen apparatus. Only one tree is ever drawn, so at most
		# one mandate and one alternative of each pair can be completed.
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_workers_guard }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_manpower = 3000
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 4000 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_chairmans_mandate }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_bagley_executive }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 40
				add_stability = 0.04
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_mandate_of_the_committees }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_manpower = 4000
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_escort_for_the_chairman }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_stability = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_reserve_headquarters }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_command_power = 20
				army_experience = 25
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_mandate_of_the_staff }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_command_power = 30
				army_experience = 30
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_closed_laboratories }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 150 producer = WKR }
				add_tech_bonus = {
					name = WRK_network_closed_laboratories
					bonus = 0.5
					uses = 1
					category = electronics
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_district_collegia }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_manpower = 3000
				add_stability = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_mandate_of_the_network }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 200 producer = WKR }
				add_war_support = 0.05
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_district_arsenal_lists }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 5000 producer = WKR }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_joint_government_draft }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_stability = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_emergency_command }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_command_power = 20
				army_experience = 20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_last_order_draft }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_political_power = 40
				add_war_support = 0.03
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_prototype_reserve }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 60 producer = WKR }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 producer = WKR }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_tower_duty_roster }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_command_power = 20
				add_political_power = 40
			}
		}
		32 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		33 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		34 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		35 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		36 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		37 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		38 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		39 = { WRK_unrest_settle_district = yes }
		32 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		33 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		34 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		35 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		36 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		37 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		38 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		39 = { WRK_cell_settle_district = yes }
		40 = { WRK_intrigue_settle = yes }
		36 = { WRK_prototype_settle = yes }
		37 = { WRK_cadre_settle = yes }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_camp_packages = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wrk_prewar_preparations_inherited_v1
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_identities = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		WKR = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized }
				# A daily leader-cache retry follows the current ruling party after
				# startup. It must not restore the original fate-based government.
				if = {
					limit = { WKR_administration_active = yes }
					if = {
						limit = { has_government = utilitarism }
						WKR_appoint_executive = yes
					}
					else = { WKR_appoint_chairman = yes }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
				set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_emergency
				set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_nikita_worcker = yes
				set_country_leader_portrait = {
					ideology = pragmatism
					portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_civilwar
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_utilitarian_republic
				# Repair/activate Anton before touching the ruling party so the engine
				# never has a frame where utilitarism lacks an authored leader.
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Anton_Bagley }
						}
					}
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_anton_bagley = yes
				}
				else = {
					promote_character = {
						character = WRK_Anton_Bagley
						ideology = utilitarian_accelerationism
					}
				}
				set_politics = { ruling_party = utilitarism elections_allowed = no }
				set_country_leader_portrait = {
					ideology = utilitarism
					portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Anton_Bagley
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
				set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_joint_government
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_appoint_joint_council = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collegial_staff_formed }
				VAD_appoint_collegial_staff = yes
			}
			else = {
				set_cosmetic_tag = VAD_vorkerland_restoration
				set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
				promote_character = {
					character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev
					ideology = imperial_restorationism
				}
				set_country_leader_portrait = {
					ideology = pragmatism
					portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Vlad_Petrichev_civilwar
				}
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		TVA = {
			set_politics = { ruling_party = technocracy elections_allowed = no }
			promote_character = {
				character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
				ideology = dorian_technocracy
			}
			set_country_leader_portrait = {
				ideology = technocracy
				portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Dorian_Worx
			}
		}
	}
	# Certify only a complete three-country pass initiated from the canonical WKR
	# controller. Exact leader-cache verification is retried separately and may
	# never block the structural war lifecycle.
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = VAD
			country_exists = TVA
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_assertion_started_v2
	}
}

# Repair only the fixed claimant materialization contract. The wider regional
# split and its OOBs are deliberately not replayed, so a retry cannot duplicate
# countries, divisions or equipment. Before wars begin the home-state transfer
# also rematerializes a missing dormant claimant tag; old mid-war saves never
# receive territorial rewrites from this bridge.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_collapse_materialization = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started } }
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WRK country_exists = WKR }
			WKR = { annex_country = { target = WRK transfer_troops = yes } }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_reset_temporary_claimant_cores = yes
		WKR = {
			transfer_state = 32
			transfer_state = 33
			transfer_state = 40
			transfer_state = 200
			transfer_state = 201
			set_capital = { state = 32 }
		}
		32 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
		33 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
		40 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
		200 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
		201 = { set_state_controller_to = WKR }
		VAD = {
			transfer_state = 75
			transfer_state = 106
			transfer_state = 107
			transfer_state = 121
			set_capital = { state = 75 }
		}
		75 = { set_state_controller_to = VAD }
		106 = { set_state_controller_to = VAD }
		107 = { set_state_controller_to = VAD }
		121 = { set_state_controller_to = VAD }
		TVA = {
			transfer_state = 36
			transfer_state = 37
			transfer_state = 38
			transfer_state = 39
			transfer_state = 324
			set_capital = { state = 36 }
		}
		36 = { set_state_controller_to = TVA }
		37 = { set_state_controller_to = TVA }
		38 = { set_state_controller_to = TVA }
		39 = { set_state_controller_to = TVA }
		324 = { set_state_controller_to = TVA }
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_claimant_home_cores = yes
	WKR = {
		load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_focus keep_completed = yes }
		focus_unlock = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
	VAD = {
		load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_focus keep_completed = yes }
		focus_unlock = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
	TVA = {
		load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_focus keep_completed = yes }
		focus_unlock = yes
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	}
	# Independence/faction cleanup and army relocation are part of the fresh
	# partition only. A v2 bridge may run after wars have begun; in that case it
	# must repair lifecycle metadata without moving fronts or breaking alliances.
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started } }
		WKR = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = WKR autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		VAD = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = VAD autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		TVA = {
			if = { limit = { is_subject = yes } overlord = { set_autonomy = { target = TVA autonomy_state = autonomy_free } } }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
		WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_relocate_legacy_armies = yes }
	}
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_claimant_identities = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_materialization_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized = yes }
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Structural split failed: claimant existence, independence, or one of the fourteen home-state ownership/controller postconditions is false."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized = yes
			NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_materialized = yes }
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Political split failed: the WKR route leader, Vlad/joint VAD government, or Doctor Worx technocracy is not ruling after the daily cache rebuild."
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WRK }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Dormant WRK still exists after the WKR handoff."
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = WKR } }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] WKR did not materialize."
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = VAD } }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] VAD did not materialize."
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { country_exists = TVA } }
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] TVA did not materialize."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_claimant_identity_mismatches = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR = {
				has_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				NOT = {
					OR = {
						WKR_presidential_course = yes
						WKR_executive_course = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] The current western government has not activated its authored leader role. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized } }
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
			NOT = {
				WKR = {
					has_government = pragmatism
					has_country_leader_ideology = neo_vorkerism
					has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker ruling_only = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] WKR failed to activate Nikita Worcker's transferred neo-vorkerist leader role. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized } }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
			NOT = {
				WKR = {
					has_government = utilitarism
					has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Anton_Bagley ruling_only = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] WKR failed to activate Anton Bagley's utilitarian leader role. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed
			NOT = { VAD = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council ruling_only = yes } } }
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] VAD failed to activate the joint council. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_government_formed }
			NOT = { VAD = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev ruling_only = yes } } }
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] VAD failed to activate Vlad Petrichev. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				TVA = {
					has_government = technocracy
					has_country_leader = { character = TVA_Dorian_Worx ruling_only = yes }
				}
			}
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][IDENTITY] TVA failed to activate Doctor Dorian Worx. The war lifecycle will continue."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_collapse_materialized = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized = yes
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_assertion_started_v2
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_final_retry
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialized_verified
		# Dynamic/materialized claimants do not reliably run the country selector.
		# Install each claimant's tree only after the daily tag cache and all claimant
		# postconditions have verified; keep_completed preserves prewar progress.
		WKR = {
			load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_focus keep_completed = yes }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_wrk_prewar_preparations = yes
		}
		VAD = {
			load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_focus keep_completed = yes }
		}
		TVA = {
			load_focus_tree = { tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_focus keep_completed = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identities_verified_v2 }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_check_pending_v2 }
			}
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant_identity_check_pending_v2
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.14 days = 1 } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_war_outbreak_scheduled }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_regional_consolidation = yes
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_war_outbreak_scheduled
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.2 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_retry } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_materialization_failure = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_collapse_materialization = yes
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.2 days = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_final_retry } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_final_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_materialization_failure = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_collapse_materialization = yes
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.2 days = 1 } }
	}
	else = {
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_materialization_failed
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_log_materialization_failure = yes
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Collapse materialization stopped after two bounded repair passes; regional and claimant wars remain locked."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_regional_war_launch = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_repair_regional_wars = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_assert_rom_dva_war_content_initialized = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_intervention_active }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_graph_active_or_terminal = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_rom_dva_edge_initialized_or_terminal = yes
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began
		# Northern escalation is a consequence of a verified live/terminal graph,
		# never of the declaration attempt itself.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_northern_escalation = yes
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_ivanland_planning_unlocked_v4
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
		# Regional theatres continue independently. As soon as their opening war
		# graph is verified, the three claimants may use focus-unlocked decisions
		# to absorb adjacent central minors one bounded front at a time.
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_central_preparation = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_regional_war_factions = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.64 days = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_degrade_regional_launch = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_degrade_regional_launch = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_failed
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_degraded
	log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Regional war graph failed bounded verification; central preparation queued."
	WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.3 days = 1 } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_regional_consolidation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_failed
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_regional_consolidation
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_finished }
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_northern_escalation = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_central_preparation = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_consolidation_complete = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_consolidation_verified
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_central_preparation = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_central_preparation = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_scheduled
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
		WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.63 days = 1 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_war_launch_final_retry
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_northern_wars_began }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_degrade_regional_launch = yes
	}
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_central_preparation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_consolidation_clock_started }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_central_preparation = yes
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_consolidation_clock_started
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_dispatch_treaties = yes
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_border_launch_failed
		# Each surviving claimant carries the same fallback; phase.4 is idempotent.
		WKR = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_consolidation_deadline country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4 days = 180 } } }
		VAD = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_consolidation_deadline country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4 days = 180 } } }
		TVA = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_consolidation_deadline country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.4 days = 180 } } }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_showdown_edge_queue = {
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_attempted
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_verified
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_attempted
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_attempted
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_retry
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_verified
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
	clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_launch_round_started
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = VAD
			WKR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			VAD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = TVA
			WKR = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			TVA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = VAD
			country_exists = TVA
			VAD = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
			TVA = { is_subject = no NOT = { has_capitulated = yes } }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
	}
	set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_showdown_claimants = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.5 days = 1 }
}

# All claimants leave inherited factions before any declaration is attempted.
# The one-day gap above is deliberate: it lets the diplomatic cache observe all
# three detachments together instead of opening one claimant edge at a time.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_showdown_claimants = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		WKR = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction } }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA } TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_leave_inherited_faction = yes } }
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR country_exists = VAD }
		WKR = { diplomatic_relation = { country = VAD relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR country_exists = TVA }
		WKR = { diplomatic_relation = { country = TVA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD country_exists = TVA }
		VAD = { diplomatic_relation = { country = TVA relation = non_aggression_pact active = no } }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_wkr_vad = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_verified }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_failed }
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = VAD
			WKR = { NOT = { has_war_with = VAD } }
		}
		WKR = { declare_war_on = { target = VAD type = annex_everything } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_attempted
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_wkr_tva = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_failed }
			country_exists = WKR
			country_exists = TVA
			WKR = { NOT = { has_war_with = TVA } }
		}
		WKR = { declare_war_on = { target = TVA type = annex_everything } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_attempted
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_vad_tva = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_verified }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_failed }
			country_exists = VAD
			country_exists = TVA
			VAD = { NOT = { has_war_with = TVA } }
		}
		VAD = { declare_war_on = { target = TVA type = annex_everything } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_attempted
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_all_showdown_edges = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_detach_showdown_claimants = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_wkr_vad = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_wkr_tva = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_showdown_edge_vad_tva = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.5 days = 1 }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_wkr_vad = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
			WKR = { has_war_with = VAD }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_retry
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_verified }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] WKR-VAD edge failed after one retry."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_wkr_tva = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
			WKR = { has_war_with = TVA }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified
		# This verifies only the WKR-TVA edge. TVA's WTD protectorate is called
		# separately once its own WKR war is visible in the diplomatic cache.
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_wtd_tva_temporary_alliance_check = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_retry
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] WKR-TVA edge failed after one retry."
	}
}

# Once the live WKR-TVA war has entered the diplomatic cache, schedule one
# country-scoped call for TVA's WTD protectorate. Event .47 performs the actual
# add_to_war and has no recurring fallback.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_wtd_tva_temporary_alliance_check = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WTD = {
				exists = yes
				is_subject_of = TVA
				NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				NOT = { has_war_with = WKR }
			}
			TVA = { exists = yes has_war_with = WKR }
		}
		WTD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse.47 days = 1 } }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_vad_tva = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
			VAD = { has_war_with = TVA }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_verified
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_retry }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_retry
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_verified }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_failed
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] VAD-TVA edge failed after one retry."
	}
}

# A rejected declaration is terminal for the current bounded launch round, not
# for the campaign. Release the visible controller after a short cooldown so a
# player or AI claimant can request a clean queue without reloading the save.
# Any edges which did enter the diplomatic cache remain live and will be
# recognised as already satisfied by the next queue verification.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_unwind_failed_showdown_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started }
		}
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_central_showdown_requested
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_launch_round_started
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_preparation_expired
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_required
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_attempted
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_vad_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_required
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_attempted
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_wkr_tva_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_required
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_attempted
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_retry
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_verified
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_edge_vad_tva_failed
		clr_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed
		set_global_flag = {
			flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_retry_cooldown
			days = 7
		}
		log = "[ADISCORD][VORKERLAND][RECOVERY] Showdown launch round released for a bounded retry."
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_advance_showdown_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_launch_round_started }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_launch_round_started
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_all_showdown_edges = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_showdown_queue_initialized }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_wkr_vad = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_wkr_tva = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_showdown_edge_vad_tva = yes
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_central_showdown = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed } }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_all_showdown_edges = yes
		}
		else = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_unwind_failed_showdown_launch = yes
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_verify_central_showdown = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_central_showdown = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_regional_allies_to_showdown = yes
		WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_attempt_vad_solyarino_counter = yes }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_announce_verified_showdown = yes
		if = {
			# Recovery/single-survivor path: with no live pair to fight, advance
			# through the same guarded reunification pipeline instead of stalling.
			limit = { NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_required = yes } }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = yes
		}
	}
}
ADISCORD_vorkerland_begin_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_reunification }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_coalition_victory_ready = yes
		}
		# State-control and peace callbacks can run on a defeated country.
		# The surviving claimant must own the delayed event until it unifies the country.
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					WKR = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_reunification = yes
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.6 days = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				VAD = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					VAD = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_reunification = yes
			VAD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.6 days = 1 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				TVA = {
					exists = yes
					is_subject = no
					NOT = { has_capitulated = yes }
				}
				OR = {
					TVA = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_unifier }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_has_live_marked_central_unifier = no
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_set_phase_reunification = yes
			TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase.6 days = 1 } }
		}
	}
}

# Run in a losing claimant's scope before the winner absorbs it. Its
# subjects become sovereign first, so annexing the defeated claimant cannot
# silently drag an unrelated regional administration into the unified country.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_losing_claimant_subjects = {
	every_subject_country = {
		overlord = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = PREV
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prepare_claimants_for_formation = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_disengage_regional_allies_before_formation = yes
	# Detach Oitfort from every temporary civil-war relation before a claimant is
	# consumed. WTD remains a real wartime participant, but every route absorbs
	# state 34 once the winner has unified the country.
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WTD }
		WTD = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				overlord = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = WTD
						autonomy_state = autonomy_free
					}
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { country_exists = WKR has_war_with = WKR } white_peace = WKR }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = VAD has_war_with = VAD } white_peace = VAD }
			if = { limit = { country_exists = TVA has_war_with = TVA } white_peace = TVA }
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_failed
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_fighting_for_worx
		}
	}
	# Formation consumes the claimant governments and ends their coalitions.
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		WKR = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction
			if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		VAD = {
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction
			if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		TVA = {
			if = { limit = { is_faction_leader = yes } dismantle_faction = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY the unified country: Oitfort is a wartime technical administration, not a postwar state.
# Phase.7 handles a human WTD player before this effect. Consume the committee
# and its units, leaving coring to the existing integration decision.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_wtd_after_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WTD }
		WTD = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				overlord = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = WTD
						autonomy_state = autonomy_free
					}
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } white_peace = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_cleanup_preserved
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_retry
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_join_failed
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wtd_fighting_for_worx
		}
		34 = { remove_core_of = WTD }
		annex_country = { target = WTD transfer_troops = yes }
		# annex_country can leave the tag alive on leftover tiles.
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = WTD }
			WTD = { every_owned_state = { set_state_owner_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } }
		}
		34 = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
				set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY the unified country: Zshat's treaty preserves its command only during the civil war.
# Phase.7 handles a human EGC player before the army and territory are absorbed.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_egc_after_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = EGC }
		EGC = {
			if = {
				limit = { is_subject = yes }
				overlord = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = EGC
						autonomy_state = autonomy_free
					}
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } white_peace = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_coalition_obligation = yes
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending
			clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_signed
			clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
		}
		annex_country = { target = EGC transfer_troops = yes }
		# Native annexation can leave residual ownership or foreign control.
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = EGC }
			EGC = {
				every_owned_state = {
					set_state_owner_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				}
			}
		}
		81 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } }
		110 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } }
		124 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY the unified country: Riven and Yorsk keep their governments only during the civil war.
# Their treaty with a claimant does not survive reunification on any route;
# phase.7 hands a human RIV/YOR player to the unified country before this effect.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_treaty_districts_after_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = RIV }
		RIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_treaty_district = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = YOR }
		YOR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_treaty_district = yes }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY RIV/YOR, called only by the reunification absorption above.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_absorb_treaty_district = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject = yes }
		overlord = {
			set_autonomy = {
				target = PREV
				autonomy_state = autonomy_free
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes } leave_faction = yes }
	if = { limit = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation } white_peace = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_ally
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_reconcile_coalition_obligation = yes
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_signed
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_independence_guaranteed
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
	event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { annex_country = { target = PREV transfer_troops = yes } }
	# Native annexation can leave residual ownership on leftover tiles.
	if = {
		limit = { exists = yes }
		every_owned_state = {
			set_state_owner_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			set_state_controller_to = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		}
	}
}

# STATE. A claimant's earned core passes to the unified country only where it
# holds the state; every claimant core on the state is consumed either way.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_settle_claimant_core = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			OR = {
				is_core_of = WKR
				is_core_of = VAD
				is_core_of = TVA
			}
		}
		remove_core_of = WKR
		remove_core_of = VAD
		remove_core_of = TVA
		add_core_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
	}
	else = {
		remove_core_of = WKR
		remove_core_of = VAD
		remove_core_of = TVA
	}
}

# The WRK tag is retired by the unification. Its remaining cores pass to the
# unified country so a later peace cannot release the old tag beside it.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_inherit_retired_cores = {
	every_state = {
		limit = { is_core_of = WRK }
		add_core_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
		remove_core_of = WRK
	}
}

# Characters still listed under the retired WRK tag - advisers, commanders and
# the election candidates - join the unified country.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_adopt_retired_roster = {
	WRK = {
		every_character = {
			set_nationality = { target_country = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
		}
	}
}

# Carry only earned claimant cores into the unified country. Every state is
# explicit and must belong to it after the losing claimant shells are annexed;
# temporary control drift cannot erase an already earned integration core.
