# ROOT is the new controller in on_state_control_changed. A claimant does not
# count as fallen merely because its capital state changes hands. The first-fall
# news is reserved for a real regional defeat: one controller must hold every
# strict home state of that claimant. This makes border raids and brief capital
# occupations insufficient to publish a global collapse headline.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_check_first_claimant_capital_fall = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			32 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			33 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			40 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			200 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			201 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = WKR }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_wkr_capital_fell_first
		WKR = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_wkr_capital_fall = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			75 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			106 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			107 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			121 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = VAD }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_vad_capital_fell_first
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_vad_capital_fall = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_edges_verified = yes
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_launch_failed }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell }
			36 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			37 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			38 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			39 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			324 = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			NOT = { tag = TVA }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capital_fell
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_tva_capital_fell_first
		TVA = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				add_war_support = -0.02
				add_command_power = -5
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_dispatch_tva_capital_fall = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_offer_first_claimant_command_choice = {
	# on_capitulation calls this inside FROM, the winner, so the report has to be
	# scoped back to ROOT. Otherwise the news would name the side that won as the
	# side that fell.
	ROOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_report_claimant_capitulation = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_showdown_started
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_central_war_finished }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capitulated }
			NOT = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			ROOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_is_main_claimant = yes }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_first_claimant_capitulated
		if = {
			limit = { tag = WTD TVA = { exists = yes } }
			TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = VLA WKR = { exists = yes } }
			WKR = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { tag = SOL VAD = { exists = yes } }
			VAD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { is_subject = yes }
			OVERLORD = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 } }
		}
		else = {
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.5 }
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_offer_post_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_reunification_verified
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_event_offered }
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian
			}
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_event_offered
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.6 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_finish_reunification = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_choice_resolved }
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_postwar_choice_resolved
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worker_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.7 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_joint_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.8 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story_worx_finale_reported
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.9 }
		}
	}
}

# --- war_economy_effects ---
# Claimant factory scaling is the only recurring civil-war producer.
# Called from the tag-specific monthly hooks for WKR and VAD.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_refresh_war_economy_dynamic_state = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count value = num_of_factories }

	# Capture what this claimant started with on the first tick after it is
	# created, then measure every later month against that. Re-captured while
	# it is still below one so a claimant that somehow spawns with no industry
	# does not divide by zero.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
				value = 1
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count
		}
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline min = 1 max = 1000 }

	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_factory_count
	}
	divide_variable = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_baseline
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale min = 0.25 max = 1 }

	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_strip_the_civil_economy
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_levy_the_crown_estates
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_wkr_keep_the_bread_ration
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_vad_protect_the_district_markets
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance value = -1 }
	}

	# Stance weighted by retained industry. Gain axes use this, cost axes use
	# the bare stance.
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
	}
	multiply_variable = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
	}

	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority value = 0 }

	# The councils argue about the shop floor: output against the ration.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_output = 0.10 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_goods = -0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest = -0.05 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority = -0.10 }
	}

	# The chancery argues about the warrants: men and haste against consent.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_manpower = 0.015 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_bite
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_mobilization = 0.15 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_unrest = -0.04 }
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_stance
		}
		multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_authority = -0.15 }
	}

	# Percentage of the original industrial base still in hand, for the tooltip.
	set_variable = {
		var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale_display
		value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale
	}
	multiply_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_war_economy_scale_display = 100 }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Worker councils, WKR only. Seated councils run the factories; a mobilised
# council has left them for the front for the rest of the civil war. Only focus
# rewards and the mobilisation decision write the counts, so the aggregate is
# refreshed on those writes instead of on a pulse.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = {
	set_variable = { var = WKR_working_councils value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_working_councils value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_output value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_output value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_output value = 0.03 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_stability value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_stability value = 0.01 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_conscription value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_conscription value = 0.004 }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_conscription value = WKR_council_doctrine_conscription }
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_seat_council = {
	effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_seated_dummy }
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils value = 1 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = yes
	}
}

# Reserves one working council at the start of its mobilisation so it cannot be
# sent twice. The caller refreshes the aggregate.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized value = 1 }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_battalion = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_worker_home_guard_template = yes
	capital_scope = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "name = \"WKR_factory_battalion_unit\" division_template = \"Worker Home Guard\" start_experience_factor = 0.15 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

# Each resolved trial adds an iteration. Payment snapshots the success chance;
# subsequent training, repairs and focus rewards affect only the next order.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_add_iteration = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_iterations value = 1 }
	TVA_refresh_administration = yes
}

# Temporary price inputs are shared by payment and both refund destinations.
# The stored kind fixes the contract while later focuses change future orders.
TVA_calculate_trial_price = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_pp value = 30 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 9000 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_manpower value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 6000 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_manpower value = 600 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_pp value = 35 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = 120 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_pp value = 25 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = 80 }
	}
}

TVA_return_trial_payment = {
	add_political_power = TVA_trial_price_pp
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = TVA_trial_price_rifles producer = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = TVA_trial_price_support producer = ROOT }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_manpower value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		add_manpower = TVA_trial_price_manpower
	}
}

TVA_release_trial_receipt = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid
	clear_variable = TVA_trial_kind
	clear_variable = TVA_trial_chance
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_begin_field_trial = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_context = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_affordable = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid }
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_kind value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_raise_technical_battalions }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_kind value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_test_remote_fire_control }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_kind value = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_test_adaptive_logistics }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_kind value = 4 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = TVA_trial_kind }
		TVA_calculate_trial_price = yes
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_pp value = -1 }
		add_political_power = TVA_trial_price_pp
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_rifles value = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = TVA_trial_price_rifles }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_support value = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = TVA_trial_price_support }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_manpower value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_price_manpower value = -1 }
			add_manpower = TVA_trial_price_manpower
		}
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid
		TVA_refresh_administration = yes
		set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_chance value = TVA_trial_success_chance }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_cancel_field_trial = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid } }
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = TVA_trial_kind }
			TVA_calculate_trial_price = yes
			TVA_release_trial_receipt = yes
		}
		TVA_return_trial_payment = yes
	}
}

# ROOT is the living successor or annexer; the receipt is still held by TVA.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_settle_trial_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_receipt_kind value = TVA.TVA_trial_kind }
		TVA_calculate_trial_price = yes
		TVA = { TVA_release_trial_receipt = yes }
		TVA_return_trial_payment = yes
	}
}

# The completed programme follows its paid protocol, not a later focus reward.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_trial_success = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_trial_is_battalion = yes } }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_bonus bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = infantry_weapons }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_trial_battalion_cadres_tt
		hidden_effect = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 15 } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_trial_is_fire_control = yes } }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_bonus bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = electronics }
		army_experience = 15
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_trial_is_logistics = yes } }
		add_tech_bonus = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_bonus bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = support_tech }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worx_adaptive_logistics_trial days = 70 }
	}
	else = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_bonus bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = industry }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_legitimacy_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.25 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_trial_failure = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_failed_tt
	army_experience = 10
	hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.26 } }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_resolve_field_trial = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_context = yes } }
			hidden_effect = {
				clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_trial_paid
				set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_failure_chance value = 100 }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_failure_chance value = TVA_trial_chance }
			}
			random_list = {
				TVA_trial_chance = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_trial_success = yes }
				TVA_trial_failure_chance = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_field_trial_failure = yes }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_trial_wear_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_iteration_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				clear_variable = TVA_trial_kind
				clear_variable = TVA_trial_chance
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 10 }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 5 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_add_iteration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_cancel_field_trial = yes }
	}
}

# District decisions run in VAD with their state in FROM. A shared receipt
# reserves the chancery; its state pointer lets native peace hooks refund the
# project without depending on the hook's unrelated FROM scope.

ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = {
	set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_state value = FROM }
	FROM = { set_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAD_oath_state }
		var:VAD_oath_state = { clr_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
	}
	VAD_release_foreign_offer = yes
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_pp
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_manpower
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_support
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_rifles
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_squad_weapons
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_kind
	clear_variable = VAD_oath_state
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_seal_district_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 1 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_charter_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_charter_result_tt
			add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = 0.15 }
			hidden_effect = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_charters value = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.03 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
				FROM = {
					set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_oath
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chartered_district }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAD_district_recruited } }
						set_variable = { var = VAD_district_recruited value = 0 }
					}
					add_to_array = { array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states value = THIS }
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_seal_commandant_rule = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 2 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_commandant_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_result_tt
			add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = -0.15 }
			hidden_effect = {
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandants value = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.02 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
				FROM = {
					set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_oath
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
					if = {
						limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAD_district_recruited } }
						set_variable = { var = VAD_district_recruited value = 0 }
					}
					set_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 20 }
					add_to_array = { array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states value = THIS }
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# The oaths belong to the civil war: the local modifiers end with it, whoever
# holds the district by then.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_retire_district_oaths = {
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chartered_district } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chartered_district }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
		}
		VAD_clear_district_unrest = yes
		VAD_clear_recruitment_disruption = yes
		VAD_clear_staff_programmes = yes
		VAD_clear_crown_programmes = yes
		VAD_clear_local_militia = yes
		VAD_clear_march_commitment = yes
		VAD_corps_clear_district_programmes = yes
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_oath
	}
	clear_array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
}

# The receipt reserves councils and their full personnel and equipment cost.
# It survives leadership changes; only delivery, cancellation or handoff settles it.
WKR_begin_council_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_council_order_context = yes
			WKR_council_order_free = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_has_working_council = yes
			WKR_council_order_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		add_manpower = -3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -2700 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_pp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_manpower value = 3000 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_rifles value = 2700 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_units value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_kind value = 1 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = yes
		WKR_lock_council_template = yes
	}
}

WKR_begin_council_batch = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_council_order_context = yes
			has_completed_focus = WKR_requisition_in_the_republics_name
			WKR_council_order_free = yes
			WKR_has_two_working_councils = yes
			WKR_council_batch_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		add_manpower = -6000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -5400 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_pp value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_manpower value = 6000 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_rifles value = 5400 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_units value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_kind value = 2 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = yes
		WKR_lock_council_template = yes
	}
}

WKR_release_council_order = {
	clear_variable = WKR_council_order_pp
	clear_variable = WKR_council_order_manpower
	clear_variable = WKR_council_order_rifles
	clear_variable = WKR_council_order_units
	clear_variable = WKR_council_order_kind
}

WKR_return_reserved_councils = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized value = WKR_council_order_units }
	WKR_release_council_order = yes
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = yes
}

WKR_refund_council_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = WKR_council_order_pp } }
		add_political_power = WKR_council_order_pp
		add_manpower = WKR_council_order_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = WKR_council_order_rifles producer = ROOT }
		hidden_effect = { WKR_return_reserved_councils = yes }
	}
}

# ROOT already owns its first state when reunifying, or is the native annexer.
WKR_settle_council_order_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_variable = WKR_council_order_pp } }
		add_political_power = WKR.WKR_council_order_pp
		add_manpower = WKR.WKR_council_order_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = WKR.WKR_council_order_rifles producer = ROOT }
		WKR = { WKR_return_reserved_councils = yes }
	}
}

WKR_finish_council_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = WKR_council_order_pp } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_council_order_context = yes } }
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_delivered_councils value = WKR_council_order_units }
				set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_delivered_order_kind value = WKR_council_order_kind }
				WKR_release_council_order = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = WKR_delivered_order_kind value = 3 compare = equals } } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_assault_column_result_tt
				hidden_effect = { WKR_create_assault_column = yes }
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_council_delivered_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					for_loop_effect = {
						end = WKR_delivered_councils
						value = WKR_council_formation_index
						ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_council_battalion = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else = { WKR_refund_council_order = yes }
	}
}

# Ordinary recruitment remains available, but paid formations retain their
# three-battalion composition until the campaign closes every pending order.
WKR_lock_council_template = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_ensure_worker_home_guard_template = yes
	set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Worker Home Guard" is_locked = yes }
	set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Worker Home Guard" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
}

WKR_lock_assault_column_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Worker Assault Column" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Worker Assault Column"
			template_counter = 4
			division_names_group = WRK_INF_01
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
			regiments = {
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 0 y = 2 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 1 y = 1 }
				ADISCORD_militia = { x = 1 y = 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Worker Assault Column" is_locked = yes }
	set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Worker Assault Column" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
}

WKR_begin_assault_column = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_council_order_context = yes
			WKR_presidential_course = yes
			has_completed_focus = WKR_break_them_with_numbers
			WKR_council_order_free = yes
			WKR_has_two_working_councils = yes
			WKR_assault_column_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -60
		add_manpower = -6000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -5400 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_pp value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_manpower value = 6000 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_rifles value = 5400 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_units value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_order_kind value = 3 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_withdraw_council = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_councils = yes
		WKR_lock_assault_column_template = yes
	}
}

# Two councils supply one larger unit; the receipt count remains the number
# of councils withdrawn from production for cancellation and veteran accounting.
WKR_create_assault_column = {
	WKR_lock_assault_column_template = yes
	capital_scope = {
		create_unit = {
			division = "name = \"WKR_assault_column_unit\" division_template = \"Worker Assault Column\" start_experience_factor = 0.30 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
			owner = PREV
			allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
		}
	}
}

WKR_initialize_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized }
		}
		set_country_flag = WKR_administration_initialized
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_doctrine_conscription value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_doctrine_training_time_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_doctrine_army_attack_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_doctrine_army_org_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_council_doctrine_experience_gain_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_foreign_send_volunteer_size value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_foreign_send_volunteers_tension value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_foreign_political_power_gain value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
			set_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop set_value = -0.30 }
		}
		else = { set_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop set_value = 0.30 } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.1 days = 7 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# The chairman's course exists only while Nikita stays with the western forces.
# Without him the executive keeps the government; settlement and the leader
# repair reach this effect without checking his fate themselves.
WKR_appoint_chairman = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_nikita_worcker = yes
		set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = pragmatism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_civilwar
		}
		set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_emergency
		WKR_party_representatives_chairman = yes
	}
	else = { WKR_appoint_executive = yes }
}

WKR_appoint_executive = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_promote_anton_bagley = yes
	set_politics = { ruling_party = utilitarism elections_allowed = no }
	set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_utilitarian_republic
	set_country_leader_portrait = {
		ideology = utilitarism
		portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Anton_Bagley
	}
	WKR_party_representatives_executive = yes
}

# Range entry dispatches the leadership event; there is no periodic election.
# Its immediate block rechecks the mandate because another event may be open.
WKR_resolve_balance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			WKR_mandate_open = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_chairman_has_majority = yes
				WKR_presidential_course = no
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.5 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_executive_has_majority = yes
				WKR_executive_course = no
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.6 }
		}
	}
}

WKR_close_administration = {
	WKR_close_council_orders = yes
	WKR_close_railway_councils = yes
	WKR_measure_close_forward_depots = yes
	clr_country_flag = WKR_front_rising_prepared
	WKR_vow_clear_story_state = yes
	WKR_last_clear_story_state = yes
	WKR_tally_clear_story_state = yes
	WKR_close_recognition_offers = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = WKR
			has_completed_focus = WKR_send_republican_volunteers
		}
		set_rule = { can_send_volunteers = no }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_reserves_forward }
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_reserves_forward
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
		remove_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

WKR_close_recognition_offers = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		ROM = { clr_country_flag = WKR_recognition_offer_pending }
		TRU = { clr_country_flag = WKR_recognition_offer_pending }
	}
}

WKR_deliver_coalition_supply = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				WKR_coalition_supply_context = yes
				WKR_coalition_supply_affordable = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { add_political_power = -25 }
		send_equipment = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1000 target = FROM }
		send_equipment = { type = support_equipment amount = 50 target = FROM }
	}
}

WKR_offer_republic_recognition = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_can_offer_recognition = yes }
		FROM = {
			set_country_flag = WKR_recognition_offer_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.17 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: the signatory. The pending offer is consumed in the same effect as
# payment, so another event copy cannot ratify or charge the agreement again.
WKR_accept_republic_recognition = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_recognition_offer_valid = yes } }
		hidden_effect = {
			clr_country_flag = WKR_recognition_offer_pending
			set_country_flag = WKR_independence_treaty
		}
		WKR = {
			add_political_power = -50
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT } }
				diplomatic_relation = { country = ROOT relation = non_aggression_pact active = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.04 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

WKR_begin_railway_council = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_railway_context = yes
			WKR_railway_slot_free = yes
			WKR_railway_target_free = yes
			WKR_railway_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -500
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -40 producer = ROOT }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = -5 producer = ROOT }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_support value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_trains value = 5 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_kind value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_state value = FROM }
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_railway_in_progress }
	}
}

WKR_release_railway_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WKR_railway_order_state }
		var:WKR_railway_order_state = { clr_state_flag = WKR_railway_in_progress }
	}
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_pp
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_manpower
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_support
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_trains
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_state
	clear_variable = WKR_railway_order_kind
}

WKR_refund_railway_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = WKR_railway_order_pp } }
		add_political_power = WKR_railway_order_pp
		add_manpower = WKR_railway_order_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = WKR_railway_order_support producer = ROOT }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = WKR_railway_order_trains producer = ROOT }
		hidden_effect = { WKR_release_railway_order = yes }
	}
}

# The recipient is already alive: either united WRK after its first transfer,
# or the native annexer. The same receipt guards delayed decision callbacks.
WKR_settle_railway_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_variable = WKR_railway_order_pp } }
		add_political_power = WKR.WKR_railway_order_pp
		add_manpower = WKR.WKR_railway_order_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = WKR.WKR_railway_order_support producer = ROOT }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = WKR.WKR_railway_order_trains producer = ROOT }
		WKR = { WKR_release_railway_order = yes }
	}
}

WKR_finish_railway_council = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_railway_order_matches_target = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = {
				hidden_trigger = {
					WKR_railway_context = yes
					FROM = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council } } }
				}
			}
			FROM = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council } }
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = { add_to_array = { array = global.WKR_railway_council_states value = THIS } }
				WKR_release_railway_order = yes
			}
		}
		else = { WKR_refund_railway_order = yes }
	}
}

# STATE. A battle or territorial handoff can precede the decision's cancel
# callback. In reunification the first transferred state already belongs to
# the living recipient, so its pending payment must not return to dormant WKR.
WKR_lose_railway_council = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = WKR } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WKR_railway_in_progress }
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_phase_reunification
					is_owned_by = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				}
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { WKR_settle_railway_handoff = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { WKR = { exists = yes } }
				WKR = { WKR_refund_railway_order = yes }
			}
		}
		WKR_clear_airfield_workshop = yes
		WKR_clear_prepared_interchange = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_array = { array = global.WKR_railway_council_states value = THIS } }
			remove_from_array = { array = global.WKR_railway_council_states value = THIS }
		}
	}
}

WKR_close_railway_councils = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		WKR_refund_railway_order = yes
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.WKR_railway_council_states
			WKR_clear_airfield_workshop = yes
			WKR_clear_prepared_interchange = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_railway_council }
			}
		}
		clear_array = global.WKR_railway_council_states
	}
}

WKR_close_council_orders = {
	WKR_refund_council_order = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Worker Home Guard" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Worker Home Guard" is_locked = no }
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Worker Home Guard" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Worker Assault Column" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Worker Assault Column" is_locked = no }
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Worker Assault Column" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
	}
}

# --- superevent_observer ---
# The daily hook is country-scoped. The global guard permits at most one human
# scan per day, only while a presentation is overdue. No request-country or
# client-local state is used, so annexation and mixed human sessions stay safe.
ADISCORD_superevent_observer_tick = {
	# on_daily is country-scoped, so claim the once-per-day global guard before
	# evaluating presentation ages. Without this, every country evaluates the
	# full timeout OR on quiet days when no superevent is overdue.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_superevent_observer_checked_today }
		}
		set_global_flag = { flag = ADISCORD_superevent_observer_checked_today days = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_civilwar days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_vlad_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_dorian_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_empire days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_resource_war days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_last_empire days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_val_commonwealth days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_val_council_proclamation days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_war days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_great days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_restoration days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_black_banner days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_vorkerland_war days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_civilwar days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_khan_defeated days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_sotnikov_civilwar days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_sotnikov_directory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_sotnikov_charter days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_republic days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_tikhoreva days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_resource_war_service days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_resource_war_district days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_houses days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_staff days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_wallets days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_silence days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_regent days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory_festival days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_houses days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_cities days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_nur days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_regency days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_commune days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_conclave days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_service days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_charter days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_contracts days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_rump days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_revenge days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_radical days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_free_islands days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_boards days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_restitution days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_worker days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_petrichev days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_veyran days > 6 }

					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_iron days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_civil_service days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_federation days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_oil_union days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_contract_republic days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_directorate days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_nam days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_council days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_world_empire days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_veyran_proclamation days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_legion_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_committee_victory days > 6 }
					has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_stalk_portal days > 6 }
				}
				NOT = { any_country = { is_ai = no } }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_civilwar days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_civilwar }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_dirty_opening
				if = {
					limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened }
					RUS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_rus_campaign.39 } }
				}
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_worker_victory }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_utilitarian_victory }

			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_empire days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_empire }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_party_victory }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_shabrat_victory }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_resource_war days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_nam_resource_war }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_last_empire days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_rus_last_empire }
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_val_commonwealth days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_val_commonwealth }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_val_council_proclamation days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_val_council_proclamation
			}

			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_war days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_war
			}
			if = { limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_stelander_great days > 6 } } clr_global_flag = superevent_stelander_great }
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_restoration days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_rus_restoration
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_rus_black_banner days > 6 } }

			}
