				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_free_islands
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_boards days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_boards
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_restitution days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_restitution
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_worker days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_worker
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_petrichev days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_petrichev
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_veyran days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_veyran
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_dorian days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_dorian
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_anton days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_anton
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_shield days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_shield
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_emergency days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_emergency
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_revanche days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_revanche
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_committee days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_committee
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_vorkerland_world_empire days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_vorkerland_world_empire
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_iron days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_iron
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_civil_service days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_civil_service
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_federation days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_federation
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_oil_union days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_oil_union
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_contract_republic days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_contract_republic
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_directorate days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_directorate
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_south_nam days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_south_nam
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_council days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_council
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_west_world_empire days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_west_world_empire
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_nam_veyran_proclamation days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_nam_veyran_proclamation
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_legion_victory days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_itora_legion_victory
			}

			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_committee_victory days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_itora_committee_victory
			}

			if = {
				limit = { has_global_flag = { flag = superevent_itora_stalk_portal days > 6 } }
				clr_global_flag = superevent_itora_stalk_portal
			}
			ADISCORD_superevent_dispatch_next = yes
		}
	}
}

# --- postwar_expansion_effects ---
# The coup does not wait for the victory news window to close.
# Recheck the cabinet before applying the political transition once.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_establish_world_empire = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_can_form = yes }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_joint_succession_settled
		set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_world_empire
		retire_character = WRK_VAD_Joint_Council
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev }
			retire_character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev
		}
		VAD = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev }
				retire_character = WRK_Vlad_Petrichev
			}
		}
		# The transferred pragmatist needs an explicit role in the new ideology.
		add_country_leader_role = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			promote_leader = yes
			country_leader = {
				ideology = etatism_ideology
				expire = "2200.1.1.1"
				traits = { Emergency_Powers }
			}
		}
		set_politics = {
			ruling_party = etatism
			elections_allowed = no
		}
		set_popularities = { etatism = 100 }
		set_party_name = {
			ideology = etatism
			name = WRK_world_empire_party
			long_name = WRK_world_empire_party_long
		}
		set_portraits = {
			character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker
			civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_victory }
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = etatism
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Nikita_Worcker_victory
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_world_empire = yes
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5 hours = 1 }
	}
	WRK_party_representatives_technocracy = yes
	WRK_party_representatives_joint_government = yes
	WRK_party_representatives_world_empire = yes
}

# Both founders enter here once, after their cabinet and ruler are settled.
# Queue the proclamation in WRK scope before its news reaches individual countries.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_world_empire = {
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_attack value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_defence value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_org value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_supply value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_output value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_stability value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_empire_politics value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop } }
		}
		set_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop set_value = 0.20 }
	}
	load_focus_tree = {
		tree = ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_focus
		keep_completed = no
	}
	mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_superevent_request = 61 }
	ADISCORD_superevent_enqueue = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_establish_petrichev_empire = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_petrichev_empire_can_form = yes }
		set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_vorkerland_world_empire
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_initialize_world_empire = yes
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.5 hours = 1 }
	}
	WRK_party_representatives_technocracy = yes
	WRK_party_representatives_joint_government = yes
	WRK_party_representatives_world_empire = yes
}

# COUNTRY WRK at its route's Unity Tower. The delayed callback reads the
# completed settlement before loading the next campaign tree.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_schedule_route_tree = {
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.6 days = 1 }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_refresh_industry = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_industry } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_industry }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_refresh_army = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_army } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_army }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_refresh_court = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_court } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_court }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY WRK. The officer corps schedules one plot at a time; the event
# reads the balance again when it fires.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_schedule_coup = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_empire_coup_pending } }
		set_country_flag = WRK_empire_coup_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.60 days = 14 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK at the Veyran order focus: the court and the corps are settled
# and the balance with its pending plot ends.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_settle_shadow = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop } }
		remove_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop }
	}
	clr_country_flag = WRK_empire_coup_pending
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# STATE. A grant survives occupation as a land title, while its modifier is
# active only under the restoration empire. Repeated grants cannot raise men twice.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_grant_estate = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_empire_officer_estate } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_empire_officer_estate }
		ROOT = {
			add_manpower = 2000
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# STATE target of WRK_empire_appoint_governor; ROOT is WRK. The governor focus
# choice selects the administration; a governor stays for the whole campaign.
# Governor appointments continue after the court and corps reach their final settlement.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_install_governor = {
	hidden_effect = { set_state_flag = WRK_empire_governor_seat }
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_officer_estates } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_grant_estate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_military_governors } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_empire_military_governorate }
		ROOT = { add_manpower = 4000 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_civil_governors } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_empire_civil_governorate }
		ROOT = { add_manpower = 2000 }
	}
	else = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_empire_crown_governorate }
		ROOT = { add_manpower = 2000 }
	}
	ROOT = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop } }
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_military_governors }
				add_power_balance_value = {
					id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop
					value = 0.02
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_empire_civil_governors }
				add_power_balance_value = {
					id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop
					value = -0.05
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_power_balance_value = {
					id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_empire_bop
					value = -0.03
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Imperial legitimacy is settled by the cabinet's focus route.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_rename_after_itora = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_leader = {
				character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
				ruling_only = yes
			}
		}
		set_portraits = {
			character = TVA_Dorian_Worx
			civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_WRK_Dorian_Worx_singularity_victory }
		}
		set_country_leader_portrait = {
			ideology = technocracy
			portrait = GFX_portrait_WRK_Dorian_Worx_singularity_victory
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_world_empire_active = no }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker }
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_western_eastern_union
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.61 hours = 6 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			set_cosmetic_tag = WRK_forul_technical_union
			news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.61 hours = 6 }
		}
	}
}

# Postwar institutions begin with the first funded programme. Reopening a
# branch never resets accumulated reform, including after ordinary save/load.
# Unified country's scope at settlement, before the defeated claimants are annexed: the
# claimant's war systems become fixed postwar deltas that the settlement
# focuses apply and preview exactly.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_record_worker_councils = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_council_output value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_council_output value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_council_output value = 0.02 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_council_veterans value = WKR.ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_councils_mobilized }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_council_veterans value = 5000 }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_record_district_oaths = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_charter_construction value = VAD.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_charters }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_charter_construction value = 0.03 }
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_amnesty_manpower value = VAD.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandants }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_amnesty_manpower value = 3000 }
}

# Up to eight iterations survive as laboratory practice, scaled by the trained
# cadres retained at reunification. Read TVA before its annexation.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_record_trial_iterations = {
	set_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_iteration_efficiency value = TVA.ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_iterations }
	clamp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_iteration_efficiency min = 0 max = 8 }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_iteration_efficiency value = 0.01 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = TVA_inherited_cadre_share value = TVA.TVA_cadre_reserve }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = TVA_inherited_cadre_share value = TVA.TVA_protected_cadre }
	clamp_temp_variable = { var = TVA_inherited_cadre_share min = 0 max = 100 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = TVA_inherited_cadre_share value = 0.01 }
	multiply_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_iteration_efficiency value = TVA_inherited_cadre_share }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_ensure_institutions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar_institutions } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar_institutions }
	}
}

# The southern expedition uses the existing country files and native callbacks.
# Only Ebern and Afrela can become members; there is no recurring world scan.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_member = {
	set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				is_major = no
				has_country_flag = RUS_crisis_added_major
				RUS_crisis_other_major_required = yes
			}
		}
		set_major = yes
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_added_major
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_start_ebern_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_can_attack_ebern = yes }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active
		EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_member = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { EBA = { is_in_faction_with = PIV } }
			PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_member = yes }
		}
		declare_war_on = {
			target = EBA
			type = annex_everything
		}
		if = {
			limit = { PIV = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member } }
			PIV = {
				add_to_war = {
					targeted_alliance = EBA
					enemy = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					single_target_only = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_join_subjects = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.1
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_start_afrela_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_can_attack_afrela = yes }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active
		PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_member = yes }
		declare_war_on = {
			target = PIV
			type = annex_everything
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_join_subjects = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.1
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

# Subjects enter the same war as their overlord. Peace ends only that front.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_join_subjects = {
	every_subject_country = {
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_war_with = EBA } }
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				enemy = EBA
				single_target_only = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_war_with = PIV } }
			add_to_war = {
				targeted_alliance = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				enemy = PIV
				single_target_only = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_end_member_wars = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member }
		every_enemy_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					is_in_faction_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				}
			}
			white_peace = PREV
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_clear_member = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_added_major }
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_added_major
		RUS_crisis_release_other_major = yes
	}
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_close_expedition = {
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active
	EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_end_member_wars = yes }
	PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_end_member_wars = yes }
	EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_clear_member = yes }
	PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_clear_member = yes }
}

# The active receipt is cleared before white peace can invoke on_peace.
# Subject creation waits one day for faction membership caches to settle.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_finish_expedition = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_expedition_won = yes }
		EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_settlement = yes }
		PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_settlement = yes }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_settlement_scheduled
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_close_expedition = yes
		country_event = {
			id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.2
			days = 1
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_prepare_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member }
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_subject_pending
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				dismantle_faction = yes
			}
			else = { leave_faction = yes }
		}
	}
}

# ROOT/FROM are the native loser and victor. The receipt also reserves a late
# callback after immediate white peace, while other wars keep their own peace.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_handle_capitulation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active }
			ROOT = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member
				has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			}
			FROM = {
				OR = {
					tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
					is_in_faction_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				}
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_capitulation_reserved
				days = 2
				value = 1
			}
		}
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_finish_expedition = yes }
		# White peace may release a capitulated country before the late callback.
		ROOT = {
			set_country_flag = {
				flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_capitulation_reserved
				days = 2
				value = 1
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active }
		}
		# Keep the war intact so the final native router can consume the loser.
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active }
		EBA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_clear_member = yes }
		PIV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_clear_member = yes }
	}
}

# Native changes to either participant terminate only this expedition. A
# target annexed or subjugated by a third party never becomes a free reward.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_close_external = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_active
				OR = {
					NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes }
					EBA = {
						has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member
						OR = {
							exists = no
							is_subject = yes
							NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
						}
					}
					PIV = {
						has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_campaign_member
						OR = {
							exists = no
							is_subject = yes
							NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_close_expedition = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_settlement_scheduled
				NOT = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes }
			}
		}
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_settlement_scheduled }
		EBA = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_subject_pending }
		PIV = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_subject_pending }
	}
}

# COUNTRY target; WRK is fixed, because the delayed event owns the settlement.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_create_subject = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_subject_pending
			exists = yes
			is_subject = no
			is_in_faction = no
			NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes }
		}
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
			puppet = {
				target = PREV
				end_wars = no
				end_civil_wars = no
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_apply_subject_government = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_subject_pending
}

# COUNTRY already-subjugated participant. Government follows the actual
# ruler of WRK, while the institutional route determines subject obligations.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_apply_subject_government = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_subject_of = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker } }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = PREV
					autonomy_state = autonomy_WRK_confederate_republic
					freedom_level = 0.40
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint } }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
				set_autonomy = {
					target = PREV
					autonomy_state = autonomy_WRK_military_administration
					freedom_level = 0.10
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian } }
			if = {
				limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { WRK_worx_civil_terms = yes } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = PREV
						autonomy_state = autonomy_WRK_civil_directorate
						freedom_level = 0.10
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
					set_autonomy = {
						target = PREV
						autonomy_state = autonomy_WRK_technical_trusteeship
						freedom_level = 0.10
						end_wars = no
						end_civil_wars = no
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = humanism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = humanism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = utilitarism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = utilitarism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = chauvinism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = chauvinism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = pragmatism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = pragmatism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = anarchism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = anarchism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = technocracy } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = technocracy
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = etatism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = etatism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { has_government = hedonism } }
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = hedonism
				elections_allowed = no
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { is_faction_leader = yes } }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_to_faction = PREV }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

# RUS. Only held perimeter states receive fortifications and anti-air batteries.
# STATE target; ROOT is WRK. The slot stays reserved until the programme ends.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_begin_rebuild = {
	set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_in_progress
	ROOT = {
		add_to_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_active
			value = 1
		}
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is WRK. Completion and cancellation both release the slot.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_release_rebuild = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_in_progress }
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_in_progress
		ROOT = {
			add_to_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_active
				value = -1
			}
			clamp_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_active
				min = 0
			}
		}
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is WRK. The route decides what a rebuilt district becomes:
# cooperative workshops, a garrison arsenal or a measured utility network.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_reward = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			free_building_slots = {
				building = infrastructure
				size > 0
			}
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_joint } }
		add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		add_building_construction = {
			type = arms_factory
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
		add_building_construction = {
			type = industrial_complex
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_worker } }
		ROOT = { add_manpower = 4000 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian }
			free_building_slots = {
				building = infrastructure
				size > 0
			}
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is WRK. A district lost during the programme keeps its
# right to a later programme; only a delivered reward closes it.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_finish_rebuild = {
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_release_rebuild = yes
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_site = yes }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_rebuild_reward = yes
		set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_district_rebuilt
		ROOT = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY. Cooperative works of the Worker settlement: rolling stock for the
# reopened lines and a fixed term of repair brigades.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cooperative_works_reward = {
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = train_equipment_1
		amount = 10
		producer = ROOT
	}
	add_timed_idea = {
		idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cooperative_brigades
		days = 60
	}
}

# COUNTRY. The native decision cost is the only debit; the deposit is its
# receipt until the brigades are delivered or the payment is returned.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_refund_cooperative_works = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_works_deposit }
		add_political_power = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_works_deposit
		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_works_deposit
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_finish_cooperative_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_works_deposit
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_sovereign = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cooperative_works_reward = yes
		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_works_deposit
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_refund_cooperative_works = yes
	}
}

# Orders of the Executive Committee. The native decision cost is the only
# debit; WRK_executive_order_deposit is its receipt from payment to delivery
# or refund, and one receipt serves every civil order. The cadre orders of
# the army keep a receipt of their own further below.
WRK_executive_clear_order = {
	clear_variable = WRK_executive_order_deposit
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_norm_order_pending
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_republic_contract_pending
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_district_audit_pending
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_arsenal_order_pending
}

WRK_executive_refund_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_executive_order_deposit }
		add_political_power = WRK_executive_order_deposit
		WRK_executive_clear_order = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY. The Central Order Bureau returns its overhead on every accepted
# civil order; it is part of the delivery, so the preview shows it as well.
WRK_executive_order_rebate = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_executive_order_bureau }
		add_political_power = 10
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is the unified country.
WRK_executive_norm_reward = {
	add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
	add_building_construction = {
		type = industrial_complex
		level = 1
		instant_build = yes
	}
	# A serial plant leaves the next bay of the same site ready.
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_executive_norm_series }
		}
		add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
	}
	ROOT = { WRK_executive_order_rebate = yes }
}

# STATE target; ROOT is the unified country. The site flag ties the receipt to
# this region, so a callback from another region or another order settles nothing.
WRK_executive_finish_norm_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_norm_order_pending }
		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
				WRK_executive_norm_site = yes
				WRK_executive_norm_room = yes
			}
			WRK_executive_norm_reward = yes
			set_state_flag = WRK_executive_norm_built
			ROOT = {
				WRK_executive_clear_order = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_norm_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_norm_order_pending }
		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is the unified country. The audit puts idle premises on
# the books of this region and hands the unlisted depot stock to the army.
# The opened slot is what an arsenal order later builds in.
WRK_executive_audit_reward = {
	add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
	ROOT = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment_0
			amount = 600
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
	# A rolling audit follows the supply line itself: the road is put in
	# order and the stock found along it is taken on the books too.
	if = {
		limit = {
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_executive_rolling_audit }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = infrastructure
					size > 0
				}
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		ROOT = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_0
				amount = 300
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
	ROOT = { WRK_executive_order_rebate = yes }
}

WRK_executive_finish_district_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_district_audit_pending }
		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
				WRK_executive_audit_site = yes
			}
			WRK_executive_audit_reward = yes
			set_state_flag = WRK_executive_district_audited
			ROOT = { WRK_executive_clear_order = yes }
		}
		else = {
			ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_district_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_district_audit_pending }
		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY. Workshops of the surviving republics deliver against the contract.
WRK_executive_contract_reward = {
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = infantry_equipment_0
		amount = 5000
		producer = ROOT
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = support_equipment_1
		amount = 150
		producer = ROOT
	}
	# A standing supply chain adds the guns the republics used to keep.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_focus_executive_supply_chain }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = 100
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
	WRK_executive_order_rebate = yes
}

# COUNTRY. A free republic that still trades with the country leans towards it
# with a paid delivery. Five deliveries count: trade supports the western
# policy of the focuses and decisions, it does not replace it.
WRK_executive_contract_lean = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_executive_contract_lean_open = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_executive_contract_leans value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_west_push value = 5 }
		if = {
			limit = { ROM = { WRK_executive_contract_partner = yes } }
			ROM = { ADISCORD_west_push_lean = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { TRU = { WRK_executive_contract_partner = yes } }
			TRU = { ADISCORD_west_push_lean = yes }
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_finish_republic_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_republic_contract_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
			WRK_executive_contract_reward = yes
			WRK_executive_contract_lean = yes
			WRK_executive_clear_order = yes
		}
		else = {
			WRK_executive_refund_order = yes
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_republic_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_republic_contract_pending }
		WRK_executive_refund_order = yes
	}
}

# STATE target; ROOT is the unified country. The arsenal is built in a free
# slot of an audited region; it adds none of its own.
WRK_executive_arsenal_reward = {
	add_building_construction = {
		type = arms_factory
		level = 1
		instant_build = yes
	}
	ROOT = { WRK_executive_order_rebate = yes }
}

			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site

		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		if = {
			limit = {
				ROOT = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
				WRK_executive_arsenal_site = yes
				WRK_executive_arsenal_room = yes
			}
			WRK_executive_arsenal_reward = yes
			set_state_flag = WRK_executive_arsenal_built
			ROOT = {
				WRK_executive_clear_order = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_arsenal_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
			ROOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_arsenal_order_pending }
		}
		clr_state_flag = WRK_executive_order_site
		ROOT = { WRK_executive_refund_order = yes }
	}
}

# Cadre orders of the paid army. They run beside the civil orders on their own
# receipt, WRK_executive_cadre_deposit, under the same accounting.
WRK_executive_clear_cadre_order = {
	clear_variable = WRK_executive_cadre_deposit
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_cadre_course_pending
	clr_country_flag = WRK_executive_depot_issue_pending
}

WRK_executive_refund_cadre_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_executive_cadre_deposit }
		add_political_power = WRK_executive_cadre_deposit
		WRK_executive_clear_cadre_order = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY. A paid course of the cadre: officers leave it with a common drill.
WRK_executive_cadre_course_reward = {
	army_experience = 40
	add_command_power = 15
}

WRK_executive_finish_cadre_course = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_cadre_course_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
			WRK_executive_cadre_course_reward = yes
			WRK_executive_clear_cadre_order = yes
		}
		else = {
			WRK_executive_refund_cadre_order = yes
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_cadre_course = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_cadre_course_pending }
		WRK_executive_refund_cadre_order = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY. One depot issue is the heavy kit of a cadre brigade.
WRK_executive_depot_issue_reward = {
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = artillery_equipment
		amount = 250
		producer = ROOT
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = support_equipment_1
		amount = 250
		producer = ROOT
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = motorized_equipment_1
		amount = 150
		producer = ROOT
	}
}

WRK_executive_finish_depot_issue = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_depot_issue_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_executive_orders_open = yes }
			WRK_executive_depot_issue_reward = yes
			WRK_executive_clear_cadre_order = yes
		}
		else = {
			WRK_executive_refund_cadre_order = yes
		}
	}
}

WRK_executive_cancel_depot_issue = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WRK_executive_depot_issue_pending }
		WRK_executive_refund_cadre_order = yes
	}
}

# --- preparation_effects ---

# Prewar crisis. The balance between the centre and the districts lives on
# WRK; camp influence is global, so the packages survive WRK's annexation.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_initialized }
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_crisis_initialized
		# Choose the AI course before its focus plan starts; focus selection keeps
		# the same course flags authoritative if a player takes over the country.
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes }
			random_list = {
				35 = {
					set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_wrk_compact_course
				}
				65 = {
					set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_wrk_hardline_course
				}
			}
		}
		set_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop set_value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers value = 30 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.40 days = 35 random_days = 14 }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_clamp = {
	clamp_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers min = 0 max = 100 }
}

# COUNTRY WRK. The patron's camp gains what the other two lose.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_stake_on_patron = {
	if = {
		limit = { 40 = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff_add_20 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees_sub_10 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers_sub_10 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 40 = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers_add_20 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees_sub_10 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff_sub_10 = yes
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees_add_20 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff_sub_10 = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers_sub_10 = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK. collapse.1 records the lean before WRK is annexed; the heir's
# package and opening legitimacy read it. -1 centre, 0 even, 1 districts.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_snapshot = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop } }
		if = {
			limit = { power_balance_value = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop value < -0.29 } }
			set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_lean value = -1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { power_balance_value = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop value > 0.29 } }
			set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_lean value = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_balance_lean value = 0 }
		}
		remove_power_balance = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prewar_bop }
	}
}

# Each claimant receives its own camp's package once, after the verified
# split. Influence spent on a rival camp arms that rival.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_grant_camp_packages = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_packages_granted } }
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_packages_granted
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = WKR
				check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			WKR = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 6000 producer = WKR }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					add_manpower = 5000
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = WKR }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = VAD
				check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			VAD = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 6000 producer = VAD }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					add_manpower = 5000
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = VAD }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = TVA
				check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			TVA = {
				add_political_power = 50
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 6000 producer = TVA }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					add_manpower = 5000
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = TVA }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# --- capital unrest ---
# The committees' preparation mechanic. Eight capital districts carry
# WRK_unrest (0-100) from the moment preparation.1 names the committees until
# the collapse; the Tower has no ledger. The 30-day ledger event drives growth, the
# branch decisions lower it, and the heir is paid for every district still calm.

# COUNTRY WRK. Runs once: the district variable is its own guard. The country
# variables are the numbers the committees' focuses change; the report and
# the tooltips print them, so a number is never stated twice.
WRK_unrest_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { 32 = { NOT = { has_variable = WRK_unrest } } }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_growth value = 5 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_growth_crisis value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_patrol_limit value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_cooldown value = 45 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_calm_manpower value = 2000 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_calm_rifles value = 1500 }
		32 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 25 } }
		33 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 30 } }
		34 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 35 } }
		35 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 30 } }
		36 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 40 } }
		37 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 35 } }
		38 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 30 } }
		39 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 45 } }
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK, from the 30-day ledger event preparation.63.
WRK_unrest_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_unrest_active = yes }
		32 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		33 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		34 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		35 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		36 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		37 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		38 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
		39 = { WRK_unrest_grow = yes }
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. A patrol holds its district. A riot no longer grows but
# costs the centre stability every month until it is put down.
WRK_unrest_grow = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot }
		PREV = { add_stability = -0.01 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_delta value = PREV.WRK_unrest_growth }
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { has_stability < 0.4 } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_delta value = PREV.WRK_unrest_growth_crisis }
		}
		WRK_unrest_change = yes
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. Input: temp WRK_unrest_delta.
WRK_unrest_change = {
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = WRK_unrest_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot }
		}
		WRK_unrest_start_riot = yes
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. An object still in dispute goes to its rival at once; a
# settled one is forfeited when the prewar phase closes. The report event is
# reading only: every consequence is already applied here.
WRK_unrest_start_riot = {
	set_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_yield_object = yes
	save_event_target_as = WRK_unrest_riot_district
	PREV = {
		add_stability = -0.03
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_committees_sub_10 = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.60 }
		}
	}
}

# STATE, ROOT = WRK. An instant measure closes its district for the cooldown
# the report prints. A flag takes a literal duration, so each value of the
# country variable has its own line here.
WRK_unrest_mark_measure = {
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { check_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_cooldown value = 45 compare = less_than } } }
		set_state_flag = { flag = WRK_unrest_measure_recent days = 30 value = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		set_state_flag = { flag = WRK_unrest_measure_recent days = 45 value = 1 }
	}
}

# STATE. The measure that restores order names the result; the district keeps
# its remaining unrest and can rise again.
WRK_unrest_end_riot = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WRK_unrest_end_riot_tt
		hidden_effect = { clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot }
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK. A plenipotentiary pushes the anger into the other districts.
WRK_unrest_spill = {
	32 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	33 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	34 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	35 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	36 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	37 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	38 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
	39 = { WRK_unrest_spill_district = yes }
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. The district the plenipotentiary was sent to is passed
# over, and a district under patrol holds.
WRK_unrest_spill_district = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_spill_source }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_spill_source
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_unrest_delta value = 8 }
		WRK_unrest_change = yes
	}
}

# STATE. A patrol cut short before the collapse returns its price to the
# confederation. The flag is the receipt: the caller that clears it pays, so
# the price is never returned twice. A patrol the collapse itself cuts short
# is returned to the heir by the settlement below.
WRK_unrest_refund_patrol = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_political_power = 40 }
	}
}

# STATE, when the prewar phase closes. A rioting district hands its finished
# object to the camp that contested it.
WRK_unrest_forfeit_object = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot
			has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
			has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
		}
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WKR, in the heir's carry-over. A district the committees kept
# calm pays its package; the ledger entry is then closed. WRK is gone by now,
# so the size of the package is read from the focus record copied to WKR; the
# same focuses set the numbers WRK's report printed before the collapse.
WRK_unrest_settle_district = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_unrest }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_unrest value = 40 compare = less_than }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_district_musters } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_manpower = 2500 }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_manpower = 2000 }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_store_keys } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = WKR }
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1500 producer = WKR }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = 40 }
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_unrest
		clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_riot
		clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_patrol
		clr_state_flag = WRK_unrest_measure_recent
	}
}

# --- court intrigue ---
# The staff's preparation mechanic. Six power brokers of the confederation each
# hold a loyalty to the staff (0-100) from the moment preparation.1 names
# Petrichev until the collapse: 1 Yastrebtsev, 2 Filopo, 3 Gordon, 4 Wither,
# 5 Zogby, 6 Silver. The ledger lives on the Unity Tower (state 40), which
# outlasts WRK; the heir is paid for every figure at 65 or above.

# COUNTRY WRK. Runs once: the first figure's variable is the guard.
WRK_intrigue_initialize = {
	# The numbers the staff's focuses change; the court category, the cards and
	# the tooltips print them, so a number is never stated twice.
	if = {
		limit = { 40 = { NOT = { has_variable = WRK_intrigue_1 } } }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_decay value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_decay_crisis value = 5 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_dossier_limit value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_reception value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_stability value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_people value = 3000 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_power value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_support value = 150 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_rifles value = 4000 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_gift_command value = 25 }
		37 = { set_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_stock value = 0 } }
	}
	40 = {
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_variable = WRK_intrigue_1 } }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_1 value = 25 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_2 value = 55 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_3 value = 45 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_4 value = 40 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_5 value = 35 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_6 value = 60 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_dossiers value = 0 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK, from the 30-day ledger event. The committees and the engineers court
# the same people, faster when the centre is visibly failing.
WRK_intrigue_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_intrigue_active = yes }
		40 = {
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_stability < 0.4 } }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = PREV.WRK_intrigue_decay_crisis }
			}
			else = {
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = PREV.WRK_intrigue_decay }
			}
			WRK_intrigue_change_all = yes
		}
	}
}

# STATE 40, PREV = WRK. Input: temp WRK_intrigue_delta.
WRK_intrigue_change_all = {
	WRK_intrigue_change_1 = yes
	WRK_intrigue_change_2 = yes
	WRK_intrigue_change_3 = yes
	WRK_intrigue_change_4 = yes
	WRK_intrigue_change_5 = yes
	WRK_intrigue_change_6 = yes
}

# STATE 40, PREV = WRK. Input: temp WRK_intrigue_delta. One effect per figure:
# each moves its own variable and hands the crossing to the shared register.
WRK_intrigue_change_1 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_1 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_1 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_1 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_1 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

WRK_intrigue_change_2 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_2 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_2 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_2 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_2 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

WRK_intrigue_change_3 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_3 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_3 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_3 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_3 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

WRK_intrigue_change_4 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_4 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_4 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_4 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_4 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

WRK_intrigue_change_5 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_5 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_5 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_5 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_5 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

WRK_intrigue_change_6 = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = WRK_intrigue_6 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_6 value = WRK_intrigue_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_6 min = 0 max = 100 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = WRK_intrigue_6 }
	WRK_intrigue_register_shift = yes
}

# STATE 40, PREV = WRK. Inputs: temp WRK_intrigue_before and WRK_intrigue_after.
# Falling below 35 loses the figure to a rival camp; reaching 65 wins it. One
# pass reports at most once; the report event is reading only.
WRK_intrigue_register_shift = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = 35 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = 35 compare = less_than }
		}
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff_sub_5 = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_reported value = 1 compare = less_than }
				PREV = { is_ai = no }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_reported value = 1 }
			PREV = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.62 } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_before value = 65 compare = less_than }
			check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_after value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		PREV = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_staff_add_5 = yes }
	}
}

# STATE 40; ROOT is the confederation. Output: temp WRK_intrigue_delta. A ready
# dossier is spent on the conversation. The approaches are open from the day
# the staff is named; the garrison rolls tell it whom each figure listens to.
WRK_intrigue_set_approach_delta = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = 25 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_dossiers value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_dossiers value = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = 45 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_collect_officer_returns } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_delta value = 5 }
	}
}

# STATE 40, PREV = WKR, in the heir's carry-over. Every figure that stands with
# the staff brings its own contribution; the ledger is then closed. WRK is
# gone by now, so the size of a contribution is read from the focus record
# copied to WKR; the same focuses set the numbers WRK's texts printed.
WRK_intrigue_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_intrigue_1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_1 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_council_of_allies } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_stability = 0.06 }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_stability = 0.04 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_2 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_district_pledges } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_manpower = 4500 }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_manpower = 3000 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_3 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_council_of_allies } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = 90 }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = 60 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_4 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_district_pledges } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 250 producer = WKR } }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 150 producer = WKR } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_5 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_district_pledges } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 6000 producer = WKR } }
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 4000 producer = WKR } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_intrigue_6 value = 65 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_council_of_allies } }
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
					add_command_power = 40
					army_experience = 40
				}
			}
			else = {
				event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
					add_command_power = 25
					army_experience = 25
				}
			}
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_1
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_2
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_3
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_4
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_5
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_6
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_intrigue_work_paid }
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_dossiers
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_work_paid
		clr_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work
		clr_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_reception_recent
	}
}

# STATE 40. A conversation or dossier cut short before the collapse returns
# the price recorded when it began. The flag is the receipt: the caller that
# clears it pays. Work the collapse cuts short is returned to the heir by the
# settlement above.
WRK_intrigue_refund_work = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_intrigue_work
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_intrigue_work_paid }
		clear_variable = WRK_intrigue_work_paid
	}
}

# --- capital cells ---
# The engineers' preparation mechanic. Each of the eight capital districts
# carries WRK_cell_level (0-3) and WRK_cell_heat (0-100, the committees'
# attention) from the moment preparation.1 names the network until the
# collapse. A hidden cell feeds the workshops and draws attention; at 100 it is
# exposed and loses a level every month. The heir is paid for what survives.

# COUNTRY WRK. Runs once: the district variable is its own guard. The country
# variables are the numbers the network's focuses change; the report and the
# tooltips print them, so a number is never stated twice.
WRK_cells_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { 32 = { NOT = { has_variable = WRK_cell_level } } }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield_rate value = 8 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat_rate value = 4 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cells_work_limit value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_cover value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_people_rate value = 600 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_support_rate value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_limit value = 2 }
		32 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 30 }
		}
		33 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 10 }
		}
		34 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 15 }
		}
		35 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 10 }
		}
		36 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 15 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_stock value = 0 }
		}
		37 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 10 }
		}
		38 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 10 }
		}
		39 = {
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 25 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK, from the 30-day ledger event. Working cells hand parts to the centre's stores;
# the levels are summed in a country variable for one delivery.
WRK_cells_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_cells_active = yes }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield value = 0 }
		32 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		33 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		34 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		35 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		36 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		37 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		38 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		39 = { WRK_cell_tick = yes }
		# Exposure follows the whole round of ticks, so the alarm raised by one
		# district cannot expose another in the same pass.
		32 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		33 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		34 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		35 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		36 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		37 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		38 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		39 = { WRK_cell_expose_due = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield value = WRK_cells_yield_rate }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment_1
				amount = WRK_cells_yield
				producer = WRK
			}
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_cells_yield
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. An exposed cell is combed out level by level. A legal
# collegium works in the open. A dormant cell neither works nor draws attention.
WRK_cell_tick = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 compare = less_than } }
				clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed
				set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 40 }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal }
			PREV = { add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield value = PREV.WRK_cell_level } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_dormant } }
			PREV = { add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cells_yield value = PREV.WRK_cell_level } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = WRK_cell_level }
			multiply_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = PREV.WRK_cell_heat_rate }
			WRK_cell_heat_mark = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_delta value = -5 }
		WRK_cell_heat_mark = yes
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. Input: temp WRK_cell_delta. A hidden cell that reaches
# 100 is marked for exposure.
WRK_cell_heat_mark = {
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = WRK_cell_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal }
		}
		set_state_flag = WRK_cell_due
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. Input: temp WRK_cell_delta. Outside the monthly ledger
# the exposure follows the change at once.
WRK_cell_heat_change = {
	WRK_cell_heat_mark = yes
	WRK_cell_expose_due = yes
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK.
WRK_cell_expose_due = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_due }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_due
		WRK_cell_expose = yes
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WRK. The committees comb the district and tighten the watch on
# every other hidden cell. The report event is reading only.
WRK_cell_expose = {
	set_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed
	save_event_target_as = WRK_cell_exposed_district
	PREV = {
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_camp_engineers_sub_5 = yes
		WRK_cells_alarm = yes
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = no }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.61 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WRK.
WRK_cells_alarm = {
	32 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	33 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	34 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	35 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	36 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	37 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	38 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
	39 = { WRK_cell_alarm_district = yes }
}

# STATE. The alarm only raises attention. A cell it fills to 100 is exposed
# by its own next change, never in the pass that raised the alarm.
WRK_cell_alarm_district = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal }
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat value = 10 }
		clamp_variable = { var = WRK_cell_heat min = 0 max = 100 }
	}
}

# STATE. Planting or expansion cut short before the collapse returns the price
# recorded when it began. The flag is the receipt: the caller that clears it
# pays. Work the collapse cuts short is returned to the heir by the settlement
# below.
WRK_cell_refund_work = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_cell_work_paid }
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_work_paid
	}
}

# STATE, PREV = WKR, in the heir's carry-over. A hidden cell pays for every
# level, a legal collegium as one level, an exposed cell nothing; the entry is
# then closed. WRK is gone by now, so what a level pays is read from the focus
# record copied to WKR; the same focuses set the numbers WRK's report printed.
WRK_cell_settle_district = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_cell_level }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 0 compare = greater_than }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal }
				set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_level value = 1 }
			}
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_people value = WRK_cell_level }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_reserve_rolls } }
				multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_people value = 800 }
			}
			else = {
				multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_people value = 600 }
			}
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_support value = WRK_cell_level }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_completed_focus = WRK_network_trusted_storekeepers } }
				multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_support value = 80 }
			}
			else = {
				multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cell_pay_support value = 50 }
			}
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_manpower = PREV.WRK_cell_pay_people
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = PREV.WRK_cell_pay_support
					producer = WKR
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cell_work }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_cell_work_paid }
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_level
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_heat
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_pay_people
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_pay_support
		clear_variable = WRK_cell_work_paid
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_due
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_exposed
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_legal
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_dormant
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cell_work
	}
}

# --- cadre regiments ---
# The staff's own programme at the garrison object (state 37). A regiment is
# paid when it begins; the district flag WRK_cadre_work is the receipt and
# WRK_cadre_paid the price it records. Finished regiments are counted in
# WRK_cadre_stock on the state, which outlasts WRK, and paid to the heir.

# STATE 37. A regiment cut short before the collapse returns its recorded
# price. The caller that clears the receipt pays; a regiment the collapse
# cuts short is returned to the heir by the settlement below.
WRK_cadre_refund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_cadre_paid }
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_paid
	}
}

# STATE 37. A finished regiment joins the reserve; a receipt already settled adds nothing.
WRK_cadre_deliver = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_paid
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_stock value = 1 }
	}
}

# STATE 37, in the heir's carry-over. Every finished regiment pays its men and
# rifles, an unfinished one returns its price; the entry is then closed.
WRK_cadre_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_cadre_stock }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_stock value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_pay_people value = WRK_cadre_stock }
			multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_pay_people value = 2500 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_pay_rifles value = WRK_cadre_stock }
			multiply_variable = { var = WRK_cadre_pay_rifles value = 2000 }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_manpower = PREV.WRK_cadre_pay_people
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment_0
					amount = PREV.WRK_cadre_pay_rifles
					producer = WKR
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_cadre_paid }
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_stock
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_pay_people
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_pay_rifles
		clear_variable = WRK_cadre_paid
		clr_state_flag = WRK_cadre_work
	}
}

# --- prototype series ---
# The network's own programme at the industry object (state 36). A series is
# paid when it begins; the district flag WRK_prototype_work is the receipt and
# WRK_prototype_paid the price it records. Finished series are counted in
# WRK_prototype_stock on the state, which outlasts WRK, and paid to the heir.

# STATE 36. A series cut short before the collapse returns its recorded price.
# The caller that clears the receipt pays; a series the collapse cuts short is
# returned to the heir by the settlement below.
WRK_prototype_refund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_prototype_paid }
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_paid
	}
}

# STATE 36. A finished series joins the stock; a receipt already settled adds nothing.
WRK_prototype_deliver = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work }
		clr_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_paid
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_stock value = 1 }
	}
}

# STATE 36, in the heir's carry-over. Every finished series pays its guns and
# tooling, an unfinished one returns its price; the entry is then closed.
WRK_prototype_settle = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_prototype_stock }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_stock value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_pay_guns value = WRK_prototype_stock }
			multiply_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_pay_guns value = 30 }
			set_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_pay_support value = WRK_prototype_stock }
			multiply_variable = { var = WRK_prototype_pay_support value = 40 }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = artillery_equipment_1
					amount = PREV.WRK_prototype_pay_guns
					producer = WKR
				}
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment_1
					amount = PREV.WRK_prototype_pay_support
					producer = WKR
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work }
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation_heir = { add_political_power = PREV.WRK_prototype_paid }
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_stock
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_pay_guns
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_pay_support
		clear_variable = WRK_prototype_paid
		clr_state_flag = WRK_prototype_work
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_release_slot = {
	event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
		subtract_from_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_active
			value = 1
		}
		clamp_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_active
			min = 0
			max = 3
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_refund_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending
		event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = {
			add_political_power = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_pp
			add_command_power = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_command
			add_manpower = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_people
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_0
				amount = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_rifles
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment_1
				amount = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_support
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment_1
				amount = PREV.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_trains
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_release_slot = yes
		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_clear_receipt = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_clear_receipt = {
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_pp
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_command
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_people
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_rifles
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_support
	clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_trains
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_yield_object = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_contested }
		clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_contested
		set_variable = {
			var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
			value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 40 }
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
				value = 2
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_finish_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }

		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_release_slot = yes

		clear_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_paid_command
		set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_contested
		if = {
			limit = { state = 32 }
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
				value = 2
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
						value = 2
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
					value = 3
				}
			}

		}
		if = {

			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
				value = 3
			}
			if = {
				limit = {

					value = 2
				}
			}

		}
		if = {

				value = 2

			if = {
				limit = {

					}

					value = 3
				}
			}
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_contest_garrison }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { state = 38 }
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rival
				value = 1
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
						value = 1
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_variable = {

					value = 3
				}
			}
			event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation = { activate_mission = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_contest_archives }
		}
		if = {

				value = 2
			}
			if = {
