ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_resolve_fate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			40 = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
					value = 1
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }
			}
			WRK_unrest_civil_route_clear = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			40 = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
					value = 2
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }
			}
			WRK_unrest_military_route_clear = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
	}
	# The network takes the chairman off the grid through its own cell in the
	# Central District; without that cell the roll decides, as a riot does for
	# the two evacuations.
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			40 = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
					value = 3
					compare = equals
				}
				NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }
			}
			WRK_network_exfiltration_route_clear = yes
		}
		set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_missing
	}
	else = {
		# The tower guard keeps Worker with the loyalists; a dispersed government
		# hides him in the districts but cannot lose him in the blast. The chosen
		# apparatus adds its own weight: the chairman's mandate, the staff escort
		# or a reserve headquarters that no longer plans around him, a staff that
		# leaves the Tower undefended, the network's shelter under the Tower or a
		# Tower it has cut off from every caller.
		random_list = {
			60 = {
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_completed_focus = WRK_reinforce_the_tower_guard }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_completed_focus = WRK_committee_chairmans_mandate }
				modifier = { factor = 1.25 has_completed_focus = WRK_network_tower_shelter }
				modifier = { factor = 0.75 has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_let_the_tower_fall }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_safe_with_loyalists
			}
			25 = {
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_escort_for_the_chairman }
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_reserve_headquarters }
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 has_completed_focus = WRK_network_blind_the_tower }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_rescued_by_vlad
			}
			10 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.5 has_completed_focus = WRK_reinforce_the_tower_guard }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_completed_focus = WRK_disperse_the_government }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_completed_focus = WRK_network_blind_the_tower }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_missing
			}
			5 = {
				modifier = { factor = 0.4 has_completed_focus = WRK_reinforce_the_tower_guard }
				modifier = { factor = 0 has_completed_focus = WRK_disperse_the_government }
				modifier = { factor = 0.2 has_completed_focus = WRK_network_tower_shelter }
				modifier = { factor = 2 has_completed_focus = WRK_staff_let_the_tower_fall }
				set_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_killed
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settle_object = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
			NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_pending }
			NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settled }
		}
		set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settled
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
					value = 1
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			WKR = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_receive_asset = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
					value = 2
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_receive_asset = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_recipient
					value = 3
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			TVA = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_receive_asset = yes }
		}
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_clear_receipt = yes
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_activate_rail = {
	33 = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_project
					value = 1
					compare = equals
				}
				has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settled
				NOT = { has_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rail_activated }
			}
			set_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rail_activated
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_rail_crews
				days = 180
			}
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_accept_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_valid = yes }
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending
		set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_signed
		if = {
			limit = { tag = EYR }
			FROM = {
				add_political_power = -35
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = -13500
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_0
				amount = 13500
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = EGC }
			FROM = {
				add_political_power = -35
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = support_equipment
					amount = -100
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment_1
				amount = 100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = RIV }
			FROM = {
				add_political_power = -35
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = train_equipment
					amount = -5
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = train_equipment_1
				amount = 5
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = YOR }
			FROM = {
				add_political_power = -35
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
					type = infantry_equipment
					amount = -18000
				}
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment_0
				amount = 18000
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
				value = FROM.ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_patron
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { tag = WKR }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
				value = 1
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = { tag = VAD }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
				value = 2
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_variable = {
				var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
				value = 3
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					FROM = { tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation }
				}
			}
			FROM = { set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preserve_wartime_faction }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_coalition_of_FROM = yes
		}
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_settle_treaties = {
	EYR = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_signed
				exists = yes
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction_with = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 1
							compare = equals
						}
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_won
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 2
							compare = equals
						}
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vlad_won
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = {
							var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_treaty_patron
							value = 3
							compare = equals
						}
						has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dorian_won
					}
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_independence_guaranteed
		}
		clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending
	}
	EGC = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending }
	RIV = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending }
	YOR = { clr_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_offer_pending }
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_dispatch_treaties = {
	WKR = {
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_diplomacy_open = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.21 }
		}
	}
	VAD = {
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_diplomacy_open = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.21 }
		}
	}
	TVA = {
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_prep_diplomacy_open = yes }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_preparation.21 }
		}
	}
}

# --- Worx postwar research and personal rule ---
# Called only by the postwar tree handover; reopening never resets this mandate.
WRK_worx_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_route_utilitarian
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WRK_worx_initialized }
		}
		set_country_flag = WRK_worx_initialized
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 20 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_demand value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_results value = 0 }
		set_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_total_results
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_story_stage
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_growth value = 1 }
		set_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_growth_bonus
			value = 0
		}
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_result_threshold value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_basic_relief value = 20 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_expanded_relief value = 40 }
		WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.20 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# The gauge and prices are derived from authoritative country variables.
# Economy invalidation is needed only when the modifier's band changes.
WRK_worx_refresh = {
	set_variable = {
		var = WRK_worx_growth_total
		value = 0
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_demand min = 1 max = 4 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_basic_cost value = WRK_worx_demand }
	multiply_variable = { var = WRK_worx_basic_cost value = 25 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_basic_cost value = 50 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_expanded_cost value = WRK_worx_basic_cost }
	multiply_variable = { var = WRK_worx_expanded_cost value = 2 }
	set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_needle_x value = WRK_worx_madness }
	multiply_variable = { var = WRK_worx_needle_x value = 4.1 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_needle_x value = 20 }
	set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_next_band value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
		set_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_growth_total
			value = WRK_worx_growth
		}
		add_to_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_growth_total
			value = WRK_worx_growth_bonus
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_next_band value = 3 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_next_band value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = WRK_worx_next_band value = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_personal_rule } } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_personal_rule }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_personal_rule } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_personal_rule }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_band value = WRK_worx_next_band compare = equals } } }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_band value = WRK_worx_next_band }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_stability value = WRK_worx_band }
		multiply_variable = { var = WRK_worx_stability value = -0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_output value = WRK_worx_band }
		multiply_variable = { var = WRK_worx_output value = -0.02 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# Tag-specific daily hook: no country or state scans, no startup recovery.
WRK_worx_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = WRK_worx_growth }
		add_to_variable = {
			var = WRK_worx_madness
			value = WRK_worx_growth_bonus
		}
		WRK_worx_story_progress = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			WRK_worx_crisis = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_emergency_days value = 1 }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_emergency_days value = 0 compare = less_than_or_equals } }
				WRK_worx_emergency_failure = yes
			}
		}
	}
	WRK_worx_refresh = yes
}

# Crisis settlement precedes its optional reading event, including an unattended window.
WRK_worx_crisis = {
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_crises value = 1 }
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_demand value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WRK_worx_dissenting_protocol }
		add_stability = -0.05
		add_political_power = -50
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 60 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.21 hours = 1 }
	}
	else = {
		add_stability = -0.10
		add_political_power = -100
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 70 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.22 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# The receipt stores the price at payment; changing demands cannot change a refund.
WRK_worx_refund = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt }
		add_political_power = WRK_worx_receipt
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = WRK_worx_support_deposit }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment
				amount = WRK_worx_support_deposit
				producer = WRK
			}
			clear_variable = WRK_worx_support_deposit
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = WRK_worx_manpower_deposit }
			add_manpower = WRK_worx_manpower_deposit
			clear_variable = WRK_worx_manpower_deposit
		}
		clear_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
		clear_variable = WRK_worx_operation
	}
}

WRK_worx_record_result = {
	add_to_variable = {
		var = WRK_worx_total_results
		value = 1
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_total_results value = 1 compare = equals } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.35 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_total_results value = 3 compare = equals } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.36 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_total_results value = 6 compare = equals } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.37 days = 1 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_results value = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_results value = WRK_worx_result_threshold compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_results value = 0 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_demand value = 1 }
	}
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_operation
	WRK_worx_refresh = yes
}

WRK_worx_finish_basic = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = WRK_worx_basic_relief }
			add_stability = -0.01
			army_experience = 5
			WRK_worx_record_result = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_expanded = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 2 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = WRK_worx_expanded_relief }
			add_stability = -0.025
			add_tech_bonus = { name = WRK_worx_expanded_bonus bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 category = industry }
			WRK_worx_record_result = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_oversight = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 3 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK_worx_active = yes
				WRK_worx_oversight_ready = yes
			}
			clear_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			clear_variable = WRK_worx_operation
			set_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised
			set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 0 }
			add_stability = 0.05
			WRK_worx_refresh = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.23 hours = 1 }
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

# The country pulse dispatches at most one new stage before crisis settlement.
# Reading and dismissing its event never advances or stops the madness clock.
WRK_worx_story_progress = {
	if = {
		limit = { WRK_worx_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_story_stage value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 25 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = WRK_worx_story_stage
				value = 1
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.32 hours = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_story_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = WRK_worx_story_stage
				value = 2
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.33 hours = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_story_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			}
			set_variable = {
				var = WRK_worx_story_stage
				value = 3
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.34 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# Focus rewards write deltas before refreshing; their completion marker is not needed.
WRK_worx_refresh_directorate = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_directorate } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WRK_worx_directorate }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

WRK_worx_clear_receipt = {
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_operation
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_support_deposit
	clear_variable = WRK_worx_manpower_deposit
}

WRK_worx_begin_emergency = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WRK_worx_active = yes
			WRK_worx_slot_free = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days }
		}
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_emergency_days value = 35 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.26 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# The failed ultimatum is settled by the clock, never by closing its event.
WRK_worx_emergency_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days }
		WRK_worx_refund = yes
		clear_variable = WRK_worx_emergency_days
		set_country_flag = WRK_worx_supervised
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WRK_worx_constitution value = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_production value = -0.15 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WRK_worx_authority value = -0.5 }
		add_stability = -0.15
		WRK_worx_refresh_directorate = yes
		WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_postwar.28 hours = 1 }
	}
}

# Changes apply once to current subjects and at the existing settlement dispatcher.
WRK_worx_update_subject_terms = {
	every_subject_country = {
		limit = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_WRK_technical_trusteeship }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_pw_apply_subject_government = yes
	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_review = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 4 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK_worx_review_valid = yes
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 15 }
			WRK_worx_clear_receipt = yes
			WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_accelerated = {

		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 5 compare = equals }

			limit = {
				WRK_worx_accelerated_valid = yes
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 25 }

			army_experience = 10
			WRK_worx_record_result = yes
			WRK_worx_clear_receipt = yes
			WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

			}

		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}

		}

	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_industrial = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 8 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK_worx_industrial_valid = yes
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = WRK_worx_scientific_materials
				bonus = 0.5
				uses = 1
				category = industry
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					WRK_worx_active = yes
					has_completed_focus = WRK_worx_human_variable
				}
				subtract_from_variable = { var = WRK_worx_madness value = 35 }
				add_stability = -0.02
				WRK_worx_record_result = yes
			}
			WRK_worx_clear_receipt = yes
			WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

WRK_worx_finish_civil_science = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WRK_worx_receipt
			check_variable = { var = WRK_worx_operation value = 9 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK_worx_civil_science_valid = yes
			}
			add_tech_bonus = {
				name = WRK_worx_scientific_materials
				bonus = 0.5
				uses = 1
				category = electronics
			}
			WRK_worx_clear_receipt = yes
			WRK_worx_refresh = yes
		}
		else = { WRK_worx_refund = yes }
	}
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_refresh_measured_republic = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measured_republic_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measured_republic_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

ADISCORD_vorkerland_wkr_initialize_measured_republic = {
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_army_attack_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_army_defence_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_army_org_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_army_org_regain value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_conscription value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_consumer_goods_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_industry_repair_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_land_reinforce_rate value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_max_dig_in_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_max_planning_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_production_speed_buildings_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_stability_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_training_time_army_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = WKR_norm_war_support_factor value = 0 }
}

# Administration inputs persist for the current campaign. Reopening a decision
# category never resets the balance, network or trained reserve.
TVA_initialize_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = TVA
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_administration_initialized }
		}
		set_country_flag = TVA_administration_initialized
		set_power_balance = { id = TVA_bop set_value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_crew_standards value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protected_cadre value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_attack_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_defence_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_leader_start_level value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_morale_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_org_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_org_regain value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_army_speed_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_attrition value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_breakthrough_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_conscription value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_experience_gain_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_experience_loss_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_land_reinforce_rate value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_line_change_production_efficiency_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_max_dig_in_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_production_factory_max_efficiency_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_research_speed_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_stability_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_supply_consumption_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_training_time_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_air_cas_efficiency value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_air_accidents_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_air_intercept_efficiency value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_policy_air_range_factor value = 0 }
		TVA_refresh_administration = yes
	}
}

TVA_refresh_administration = {
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_network_load min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve min = 0 max = 100 }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_crew_standards min = 0 max = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_crew_organization value = TVA_crew_standards }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_crew_organization value = 0.01 }
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_crew_organization value = TVA_policy_army_org_factor }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_crew_experience_loss value = TVA_crew_standards }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_crew_experience_loss value = -0.03 }
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_crew_experience_loss value = TVA_policy_experience_loss_factor }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_network_output value = TVA_network_load }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_network_output value = 50 }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_network_output min = 0 max = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_network_output value = -0.002 }
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_output value = TVA_policy_industrial_capacity_factory }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_research value = TVA_cadre_reserve }
	subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_research value = 50 }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_research value = 0.001 }
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_research value = TVA_policy_research_speed_factor }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_iterations }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 15 }
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 40 }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance min = 40 max = 85 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 90 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 35 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 20 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 75 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 10 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 30 compare = less_than } }
		subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance value = 15 }
	}
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_trial_success_chance min = 5 max = 95 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_administration_active = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TVA_administration_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TVA_administration_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

TVA_clear_programme = {
	clear_variable = TVA_programme_pp
	clear_variable = TVA_programme_support
	clear_variable = TVA_programme_manpower
	clear_variable = TVA_programme_kind
}

TVA_refund_programme = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = TVA_programme_pp } }
		add_political_power = TVA_programme_pp
		add_manpower = TVA_programme_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = TVA_programme_support producer = ROOT }
		hidden_effect = { TVA_clear_programme = yes }
	}
}

# Country ROOT receives the dormant programme before TVA is consumed.
TVA_settle_administration_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { has_variable = TVA_programme_pp } }
		add_political_power = TVA.TVA_programme_pp
		add_manpower = TVA.TVA_programme_manpower
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = TVA.TVA_programme_support producer = ROOT }
		TVA = { TVA_clear_programme = yes }
	}
	TVA_lab_settle_handoff = yes
}

TVA_send_specialist_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_exchange_payment_available = yes
			FROM = {
				TVA_exchange_recipient_available = yes
				NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			set_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.18 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

TVA_accept_specialist_exchange = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA_specialist_exchange_valid = yes }
		add_manpower = -600
		TVA = {
			add_political_power = -30
			send_equipment = {
				target = ROOT
				type = support_equipment
				amount = 100
			}
			add_manpower = 600
			add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 10 }
			TVA_refresh_administration = yes
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending
}

TVA_close_exchange_offers = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = TVA }
		EYR = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		EGC = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		RIV = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		REV = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		YOR = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		NDN = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		SWB = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		VHV = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		OSV = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		ZAO = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		WPA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		WPS = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		PWR = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		PSD = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		VLA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		EBA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		TGD = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		SOL = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		SRA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		CSL = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		DVA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
		ZTA = { clr_country_flag = TVA_exchange_offer_pending }
	}
}

TVA_clear_accountability_results = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = TVA_public_confidence }
		remove_ideas = TVA_public_confidence
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = TVA_public_accountability_crisis }
		remove_ideas = TVA_public_accountability_crisis
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = TVA_research_diversion }
		remove_ideas = TVA_research_diversion
	}
}

TVA_close_administration = {
	TVA_clear_accountability_results = yes
	TVA_lab_close_programmes = yes
	TVA_endgame_clear_story = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_line_replacement }
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_line_replacement
	}
	TVA_close_exchange_offers = yes
	TVA_refund_programme = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = TVA_bop } }
		remove_power_balance = { id = TVA_bop }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TVA_administration_dynamic } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TVA_administration_dynamic }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

TVA_begin_public_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_public_audit_available = yes
			TVA_public_audit_affordable = yes
			TVA_programme_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -60 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_support value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 5 }
	}
}

TVA_finish_public_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = TVA_programme_pp
				check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_public_audit_available = yes } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = 0.10 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = TVA_public_audit_healthy_tt
						TVA_public_audit_healthy = yes
					}
				}
				add_timed_idea = { idea = TVA_public_confidence days = 70 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_public_audit_success_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.08 }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.27 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_timed_idea = { idea = TVA_public_accountability_crisis days = 70 }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_public_audit_failure_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.04 }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.28 days = 1 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = { TVA_clear_programme = yes }
		}
		else = { TVA_refund_programme = yes }
	}
}

TVA_begin_research_conversion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_research_conversion_available = yes
			TVA_research_conversion_affordable = yes
			TVA_programme_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_manpower = -600
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -100 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_support value = 100 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_manpower value = 600 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 6 }
	}
}

TVA_finish_research_conversion = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = TVA_programme_pp
				check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 6 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_research_conversion_available = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1800 producer = TVA }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = TVA_research_diversion days = 70 }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = -0.10 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_research_conversion_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 15 }
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.05 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
				TVA_clear_programme = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.29 days = 1 }
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_refund_programme = yes }
	}
}

TVA_begin_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_maintenance_available = yes
			TVA_maintenance_affordable = yes
			TVA_programme_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_support value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 1 }
	}
}

TVA_finish_maintenance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = TVA_programme_pp
				check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 1 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_maintenance_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_maintenance_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_hold_back_civil_power }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 35 }
				}
				else = { subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 25 } }
				TVA_clear_programme = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_refund_programme = yes }
	}
}

TVA_begin_cadre_training = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_cadre_training_available = yes
			TVA_cadre_training_affordable = yes
			TVA_programme_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_manpower = -600
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_manpower value = 600 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 2 }
	}
}

TVA_finish_cadre_training = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = TVA_programme_pp
				check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 2 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_cadre_training_available = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_crew_training_can_improve = yes } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_crew_training_level_tt
				effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_fewer_better_crews }
				hidden_effect = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_crew_standards value = 1 } }
			}
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_cadre_training_result_tt
			add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = 0.10 }
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_build_the_engineering_service }
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 35 }
				}
				else = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 20 } }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 5 }
				TVA_clear_programme = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_refund_programme = yes }
	}
}

# Reissuing a shipment starts a fresh interval rather than accumulating time.
TVA_frontline_dispatch_reward = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_line_replacement }
			remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_line_replacement
		}
	}
	add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_tva_doctrine_line_replacement days = 21 }
}

TVA_begin_emergency_series = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_emergency_series_available = yes
			TVA_emergency_series_affordable = yes
			TVA_programme_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_pp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 3 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_run_the_grid_at_war_load }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 4 }
		}
	}
}

# Programme kinds 3 and 4 preserve the quoted production tier until settlement.
TVA_finish_emergency_series = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = TVA_programme_pp
				OR = {
					check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 3 compare = equals }
					check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 4 compare = equals }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_emergency_series_order_available = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_programme_kind value = 4 compare = equals } } }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 6000 producer = ROOT }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 90 producer = ROOT }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_war_load_series_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 30 }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 15 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 4500 producer = ROOT }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 60 producer = ROOT }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_emergency_series_result_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 20 }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 10 }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_frontline_dispatch_context = yes } }
				TVA_frontline_dispatch_reward = yes
			}
			add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = -0.10 }
			hidden_effect = {
				TVA_clear_programme = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_refund_programme = yes }
	}
}

VAD_initialize_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAD
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_administration_initialized }
		}
		set_country_flag = VAD_administration_initialized
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_experience_gain_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_army_leader_start_level value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_planning_speed value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_conscription value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_training_time_army_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_doctrine_army_org_factor value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_planning value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_recovery value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_reinforce value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_speed value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_supply value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_authority value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_stability value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_command value = 0 }
		set_power_balance = { id = VAD_bop set_value = -0.15 }
	}
}

VAD_refund_oath = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = VAD_oath_pp } }
		add_political_power = VAD_oath_pp
		add_manpower = VAD_oath_manpower
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = VAD_oath_rifles } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = VAD_oath_rifles }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = VAD_oath_squad_weapons } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = VAD_oath_squad_weapons }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = VAD_oath_support } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = VAD_oath_support }
		}
		hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes }
	}
}

# ROOT is the living successor or the annexer, never the disappearing VAD.
VAD_settle_oath_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD = { has_variable = VAD_oath_pp } }
		add_political_power = VAD.VAD_oath_pp
		add_manpower = VAD.VAD_oath_manpower
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD = { has_variable = VAD_oath_rifles } } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = VAD.VAD_oath_rifles }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD = { has_variable = VAD_oath_squad_weapons } } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = VAD.VAD_oath_squad_weapons }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD = { has_variable = VAD_oath_support } } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = VAD.VAD_oath_support }
		}
		VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_close_administration = {
	VAD_refund_oath = yes
	if = {
		limit = { has_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" }
		set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" is_locked = no }
		set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
	}
	# Shared claimant cleanup also calls this effect from WKR and TVA.
	if = {
		limit = { tag = VAD }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_retire_district_oaths = yes
		# A Second Department operation in progress returns its escrow.
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = VAD_corps_op_kind }
			VAD_corps_abort_operation = yes
		}
		VAD_crownless_clear_story = yes
		VAD_standard_clear_story = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_collegial_staff_dynamic } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_collegial_staff_dynamic }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_power_balance = { id = VAD_bop } }
		remove_power_balance = { id = VAD_bop }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VAD_joint_operational_orders }
		remove_ideas = VAD_joint_operational_orders
	}
}

VAD_begin_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_charter_available = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			VAD_oath_target_free = yes
			VAD_charter_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 1 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_begin_commandant = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_commandant_available = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			VAD_oath_target_free = yes
			VAD_commandant_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_manpower = -1000
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 1000 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 2 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

# A control edge settles paid work immediately. Recapture may rebuild the
# administration for its full price; the history flag is not a permanent lock.
VAD_lose_district_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = VAD } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			VAD = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
		}
		VAD_remove_district_administration = yes
	}
}

# State scope; the receipt is settled by the caller before the institution is removed.
VAD_remove_district_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chartered_district } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_chartered_district }
		VAD = { subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_charters value = 1 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
		VAD = { subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandants value = 1 } }
	}
	VAD_clear_district_unrest = yes
	VAD_clear_recruitment_disruption = yes
	VAD_clear_staff_programmes = yes
	VAD_clear_crown_programmes = yes
	VAD_clear_local_militia = yes
	VAD_clear_march_commitment = yes
	VAD_corps_clear_district_programmes = yes
	clr_state_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_oath
	if = {
		limit = { is_in_array = { array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states value = THIS } }
		remove_from_array = { array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states value = THIS }
	}
	VAD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

VAD_clear_district_unrest = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising }
	}
	clear_variable = VAD_district_unrest
}

# State scope; callers collect newly raised districts before dispatching one notice.
VAD_check_district_uprising = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising }
		VAD = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# The existing monthly VAD pulse visits only registered oath states. A fresh
# revolt reports once per pulse, even when several states cross the threshold.
VAD_monthly_district_unrest = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_administration_active = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
			if = {
				limit = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = VAD
					has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
					NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { VAD = { power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value > 0.10 } } }
					add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 10 }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { VAD = { power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value < -0.60 } } }
					add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 30 }
				}
				else = { add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 20 } }
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_local_militia } }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 10 }
				}
				clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
		}
	}
}

VAD_begin_district_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_district_order_available = yes
			VAD_district_order_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 3 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_district_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 3 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_district_order_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_district_order_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 60 }
					clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
					if = {
						limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
						remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising }
					}
				}
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_withdraw_district_commandant = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_can_withdraw_commandant = yes }
		FROM = { VAD_remove_district_administration = yes }
	}
}

# Recruited cohorts remain state history after an institution is lost or rebuilt.
# Only the current administration's production disruption is reversible.
VAD_clear_recruitment_disruption = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_recruitment_disruption } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_recruitment_disruption }
		VAD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -60 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 4 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_crown_recruitment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 4 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		VAD_finish_district_recruitment = yes
	}
}

VAD_begin_joint_recruitment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_joint_recruitment_available = yes
			VAD_joint_recruitment_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 5 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_joint_recruitment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		VAD_finish_district_recruitment = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_district_recruitment = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 4 compare = equals }
						VAD_crown_recruitment_available = yes
					}
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 5 compare = equals }
						VAD_joint_recruitment_available = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 4 compare = equals } } }
			add_manpower = 3000
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_crown_recruitment_disruption_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				FROM = {
					set_variable = { var = VAD_district_recruited value = 3000 }
					add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_recruitment_disruption }
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_manpower = 4500
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_joint_recruitment_unrest_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
				FROM = {
					set_variable = { var = VAD_district_recruited value = 4500 }
					add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 30 }
					clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
					VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes }
	}
	else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
}

VAD_begin_joint_congress = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_joint_congress_available = yes
			VAD_joint_congress_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 6 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 0 }
	}
}

VAD_finish_joint_congress = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 6 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_joint_congress_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_joint_congress_result_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = VAD
							has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
						}
						subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 20 }
						clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
						if = {
							limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
							remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising }
						}
					}
				}
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_begin_joint_operations = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_joint_operations_available = yes
			VAD_joint_operations_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -100 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 7 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 100 }
	}
}

VAD_finish_joint_operations = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 7 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_joint_operations_available = yes } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_joint_operations_result_tt
			add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_joint_operational_orders days = 35 }
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
				for_each_scope_loop = {
					array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_owned_and_controlled_by = VAD
							has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
						}
						add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 10 }
						clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
						VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
				}
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_ratify_collegial_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_collegial_administration_active = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_require_collective_directives }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_planning value = 0.10 }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_recovery value = 0.03 }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_authority value = -0.05 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = VAD_delegate_to_front_commands }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_reinforce value = 0.03 }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_speed value = 0.05 }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_supply value = 0.03 }
		}
		VAD_apply_collegial_mandate = yes
	}
}

VAD_apply_collegial_mandate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_collegial_government = yes
			NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_collegial_staff_dynamic } }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_collegial_staff_dynamic }
	}
}

VAD_ratify_staff_constitution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			VAD_collegial_government = yes
			has_completed_focus = WRK_joint_restore_unity_tower
			NOT = { has_completed_focus = WRK_joint_ratify_staff_constitution }
		}
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_stability value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_staff_command value = 0.10 }
		VAD_apply_collegial_mandate = yes
		set_cosmetic_tag = VAD_vorkerland_staff_republic
		news_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_story.49 }
	}
	VAD_party_representatives_republic = yes
}

	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_district_collateral } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_district_collateral }
	}
}

# State scope. The local industry allocation ends with its council.
WKR_clear_airfield_workshop = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_airfield_workshop } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_airfield_workshop }
		WKR = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

WKR_begin_airfield_workshop = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_airfield_workshop_available = yes
			WKR_airfield_workshop_affordable = yes
			WKR_railway_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WKR_railway_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -400
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_manpower value = 400 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_support value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_trains value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_kind value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_state value = FROM }
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_railway_in_progress }
	}
}

WKR_finish_airfield_workshop = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_airfield_workshop_order_matches = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_airfield_workshop_available = yes } }
			FROM = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_airfield_workshop } }
			hidden_effect = {
				WKR_release_railway_order = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
		else = { WKR_refund_railway_order = yes }
	}
}

WKR_begin_armoured_train = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WKR_armoured_train_available = yes
			WKR_armoured_train_affordable = yes
			WKR_railway_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_manpower = -600
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -120 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_manpower value = 600 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_support value = 120 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_trains value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_railway_order_kind value = 3 }
	}
}

WKR_finish_armoured_train = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_armoured_train_order_matches = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { WKR_armoured_train_available = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = armored_train_equipment_1 amount = 8 producer = WKR }
			hidden_effect = {
				WKR_release_railway_order = yes
			}
		}
		else = { WKR_refund_railway_order = yes }
	}
}

VAD_clear_local_militia = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_local_militia } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_local_militia }
		VAD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

VAD_lock_guards_template = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" } }
		division_template = {
			name = "Imperial Guard Regiment"
			template_counter = 78
			division_names_group = ADISCORD_INF_01
			is_locked = yes
			force_allow_recruiting = yes
			regiments = {
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
				infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
			}
			support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
		}
	}
	set_division_template_lock = { division_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" is_locked = yes }
	set_division_force_allow_recruiting = { division_template = "Imperial Guard Regiment" force_allow_recruiting = yes }
}

VAD_begin_guards = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_guards_available = yes
			VAD_guards_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -60
		add_manpower = -1300
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -3870 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -90 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 1300 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_rifles value = 3870 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 90 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 14 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = -32 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_squad_weapons value = 32 }
		VAD_lock_guards_template = yes
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_guards = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 14 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_guards_available = yes } }
			VAD_lock_guards_template = yes
			FROM = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "name = \"VAD_guard_regiment_unit\" division_template = \"Imperial Guard Regiment\" start_experience_factor = 0.35 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = PREV
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
				set_variable = { var = VAD_district_recruited value = 3000 }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crown_recruitment_disruption }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_begin_local_militia = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_local_militia_available = yes
			VAD_local_militia_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -35
		add_manpower = -1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -900 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -60 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 35 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 1000 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_rifles value = 900 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 60 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 15 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_finish_local_militia = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				has_variable = VAD_oath_pp
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 15 compare = equals }
				FROM = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { VAD_local_militia_available = yes } }
			FROM = {
				subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 20 }
				clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_local_militia }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# State scope. There can be only one district sustaining the national march plan.
VAD_clear_march_commitment = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_march_commitment } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_march_commitment }
		VAD = {
			remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_march_on_the_capital
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

VAD_begin_district_march = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_district_march_available = yes
			VAD_district_march_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -45
		add_manpower = -1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -120 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 45 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 1000 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 120 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 16 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_city_redoubts_available = yes
			VAD_city_redoubts_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -800
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -150 }

		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 800 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 150 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 17 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}

VAD_finish_district_march = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_district_march_order_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_district_march_available = yes }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_march_on_the_capital days = 70 }
			FROM = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_march_commitment days = 70 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
					add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 30 }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.33 days = 71 }
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_finish_city_redoubts = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_city_redoubts_order_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_city_redoubts_available = yes }
			FROM = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = bunker
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
					province = {
						all_provinces = yes
						limit_to_victory_point = yes
					}
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# An open recipient event owns its pending marker until dismissal. This prevents
# a later offer to the same country from being mistaken for that open event.
VAD_release_foreign_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAD_oath_foreign_stage }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
				has_event_target = VAD_foreign_partner
			}
			event_target:VAD_foreign_partner = { clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending }
		}
		clear_global_event_target = VAD_foreign_partner
		clear_variable = VAD_oath_foreign_stage
		clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_clock
	}
}

# ROOT is the recipient. A close-only stale event cannot settle another receipt.
VAD_refuse_foreign_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_foreign_recipient_matches = yes
			VAD = { check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 2 compare = equals } }
		}
		VAD = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending
}

VAD_accept_coalition_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_coalition_guarantees_reply_valid = yes }
		VAD = { set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 3 } }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_join_coalition_of_FROM = yes
	}
}

# Called after the existing verifier either confirms membership or exhausts its
# bounded retry. The sponsor's deposit is separate from a regional self-entry fee.
VAD_settle_coalition_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_foreign_recipient_matches = yes
			VAD = {
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 18 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 3 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_failed }
			VAD = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
			clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				is_in_faction_with = VAD
				NOT = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_regional_auxiliary_retry_used }
				VAD = {
					NOT = { any_enemy_country = { NOT = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
				}
			}
			VAD = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes }
			clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending
		}
	}
}

VAD_accept_foreign_observers = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_foreign_observers_reply_valid = yes }
		add_intel = { target = VAD army_intel = 20 }
		set_country_flag = VAD_observer_report_received
		VAD = {
			army_experience = 20
			set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta value = 0.06 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending
	}
}

VAD_begin_coalition_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_coalition_guarantees_context = yes
			VAD_coalition_guarantees_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending } }
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 18 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 1 }
		set_country_flag = VAD_foreign_offer_clock
		FROM = {
			save_global_event_target_as = VAD_foreign_partner
			set_country_flag = { flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending days = 35 }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.21 days = 30 }
	}
}

VAD_dispatch_coalition_guarantees = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_coalition_guarantees_receipt_matches = yes
			check_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 1 compare = equals }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_coalition_guarantees_context = yes }
			set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_foreign_stage value = 2 }
			FROM = {
				set_country_flag = { flag = VAD_foreign_offer_pending days = 21 }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_diplomacy.19 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# Route-specific names belong only to non-story party representatives.
WRK_party_representatives_worcker = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Nikita_Worcker ruling_only = yes }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = WRK_party_representative_utilitarism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_party_representative_utilitarism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = WRK_party_representative_utilitarism
					name = WRK_party_representatives_worcker_utilitarism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = WRK_party_representative_anarchism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_party_representative_anarchism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = WRK_party_representative_anarchism
					name = WRK_party_representatives_worcker_anarchism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = WRK_party_representative_technocracy
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_party_representative_technocracy } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = WRK_party_representative_technocracy
					name = WRK_party_representatives_worcker_technocracy
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = WRK_party_representative_etatism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_party_representative_etatism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = WRK_party_representative_etatism
					name = WRK_party_representatives_worcker_etatism
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_character = WRK_party_representative_hedonism
					NOT = { has_country_leader = { character = WRK_party_representative_hedonism } }
				}
				set_character_name = {
					character = WRK_party_representative_hedonism
					name = WRK_party_representatives_worcker_hedonism
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

WRK_party_representatives_yastrebtsev = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = event_target:ADISCORD_vorkerland_nation
			has_country_leader = { character = WRK_Mark_Yastrebtsev ruling_only = yes }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {

# COUNTRY WKR. Evacuated fuel waits outside normal depots until storage is available.
# The existing monthly country hook draws only from this finite shipment.
ADISCORD_vorkerland_release_evacuated_fuel = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { WKR_evacuated_fuel_reserve > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room value = max_fuel_k }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room value = fuel_k }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room value = 1000 }
		clamp_temp_variable = { var = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room min = 0 max = WKR_evacuated_fuel_reserve }
		add_fuel = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room
		subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_evacuated_fuel_reserve value = WKR_evacuated_fuel_room }
	}
}

# --- leader_branch_effects ---
# Called first by the leader hub, before any reward adds to the inputs.
# Zeroes the WKR_siege_regime_dynamic inputs once per campaign; rewards only add to them.
WKR_srm_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_srm_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = WKR_srm_initialized
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_political_power_gain = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_stability_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_war_support_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_conscription = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_attack_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_defence_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_org_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_org_regain = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_morale_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_breakthrough_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_planning_speed = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_training_time_army_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_land_reinforce_rate = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_supply_consumption_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_industrial_capacity_factory = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_production_factory_efficiency_gain_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_production_speed_buildings_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_research_speed_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_experience_gain_army_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_max_dig_in_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_army_speed_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_command_power_gain_mult = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_consumer_goods_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { WKR_srm_attrition = 0 }
	}
}

# Attaches WKR_siege_regime_dynamic on first use and recalculates it after a change.
WKR_srm_refresh = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_siege_regime_dynamic } } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_siege_regime_dynamic }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}
