# --- WKR siege regime: chairman's whims ---
# Both counters only grow during one campaign. Route capstones read them, and
# the final mandate settles their balance once.
WKR_siege_count_indulged = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_whim_indulged_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_siege_whims_indulged value = 1 }
	}
}

WKR_siege_count_restrained = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_whim_restrained_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_siege_whims_restrained value = 1 }
	}
}

# Called from every answer of a demand (inside hidden_effect), never from a timer.
# The parade opens the queue from the focus; the other four demands fire once each
# in random order 40-60 days apart. The third refusal puts the chairman's outburst
# ahead of the queue once. The final mandate closes the queue.
WKR_siege_schedule_next_whim = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whims_closed }
			WKR_siege_chairman_present = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_outburst_done }
				check_variable = { WKR_siege_whims_restrained > 2 }
			}
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.6 days = 10 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_train_done }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_poet_done }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_renaming_done }
					NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_prisoners_done }
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_train_done
					}
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.2 days = 40 random_days = 20 }
				}
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_poet_done
					}
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.3 days = 40 random_days = 20 }
				}
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_renaming_done
					}
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.4 days = 40 random_days = 20 }
				}
				25 = {
					modifier = {
						factor = 0
						has_country_flag = WKR_siege_whim_prisoners_done
					}
					country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.5 days = 40 random_days = 20 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# The final mandate pays out whichever side of the whims ledger is heavier.
WKR_siege_settle_whim_ledger = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = WKR_siege_whims_indulged
				value = WKR_siege_whims_restrained
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_ledger_chairman_tt
		add_political_power = 75
		add_war_support = 0.03
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = -0.10 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = WKR_siege_whims_restrained
				value = WKR_siege_whims_indulged
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_ledger_councils_tt
		add_stability = 0.05
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.10 }
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_legitimacy_plus_5_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.05 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_ledger_even_tt
		add_political_power = 40
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_war_support = 0.02
	}
}

# --- WKR siege regime: revolutionary security commission ---
# Destroys one revealed cell and returns its share of the sabotage penalty to the
# siege aggregate. An agent is taken alive only while informants work for us.
WKR_siege_destroy_enemy_cell = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_siege_cells_remain = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_cell_destroyed_tt
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_srm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_srm_w1_cell_cleared_delta_idea
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { WKR_siege_has_informants = yes }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_agent_captured_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { WKR_siege_has_informants = yes }
				add_to_variable = { var = WKR_siege_captured_agents value = 1 }
			}
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_siege_enemy_cells value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0.01 }
			WKR_srm_refresh = yes
		}
	}
}

# Staff arrests and a turned courier always deliver a live agent.
WKR_siege_break_cell_with_capture = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_siege_cells_remain = yes }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_cell_destroyed_tt
		effect_tooltip = {
			swap_ideas = {
				remove_idea = WKR_srm_dummy_idea
				add_idea = WKR_srm_w1_cell_cleared_delta_idea
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_siege_enemy_cells value = 1 }
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_srm_industrial_capacity_factory value = 0.01 }
			WKR_srm_refresh = yes
		}
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_siege_agent_taken_alive_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_siege_captured_agents value = 1 }
	}
}

# --- WKR siege regime: language of the front ---
# Moves the erased-peoples legacy one step along the existing relief ladder. The
# archive and council decisions read the same ideas, so they continue from the
# new step instead of repeating it.
WKR_siege_step_community_rights = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations
			add_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1 }
		swap_ideas = {
			remove_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_1
			add_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2 }
		remove_ideas = ADISCORD_vorkerland_erased_nations_relief_2
	}
}

# Convict columns repair one road network in a core state. Without a free
# infrastructure level the same labour goes to field workshops instead.
WKR_siege_convict_road_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = ROOT
				infrastructure < 5
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				infrastructure < 5
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 80
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# --- W2: armed people (WKR presidential course) ---

# COUNTRY WKR. Input: temp WKR_ranks_treasury_amount (positive). Both helpers
# record the transaction for the monthly budget and invalidate the economy cache.
WKR_ranks_spend_treasury = {
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

WKR_ranks_add_treasury = {
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Block 1: training camps, shop workshops, onslaught and quarters.
# Each paid programme keeps one country flag as its receipt. Delivery,
# cancellation and refund clear it; prices are fixed, so no stored amount.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

WKR_ranks_begin_training_camp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending }
			WKR_ranks_training_camp_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -20
		add_manpower = -3000
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending
	}
}

WKR_ranks_refund_training_camp = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending
		add_political_power = 20
		add_manpower = 3000
	}
}

# The recruits come back to the pool; the camp itself is paid for in time.
WKR_ranks_finish_training_camp = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_camp_pending
		add_manpower = 3000
		army_experience = 20
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_proving_ground_camps }
			army_experience = 10
		}
	}
	else = { WKR_ranks_refund_training_camp = yes }
}

WKR_ranks_begin_workshop_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending }
			WKR_ranks_workshop_order_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -15
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount value = 40 }
		WKR_ranks_spend_treasury = yes
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending
	}
}

WKR_ranks_refund_workshop_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending
		add_political_power = 15
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount value = 40 }
		WKR_ranks_add_treasury = yes
	}
}

# Shown as the preview in the decision and executed at delivery.
WKR_ranks_workshop_order_delivery = {
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 2500 producer = WKR }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 60 producer = WKR }
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_repair_trophy_arms }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = WKR }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 12 producer = WKR }
	}
}

WKR_ranks_finish_workshop_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_workshop_order_pending
		WKR_ranks_workshop_order_delivery = yes
	}
	else = { WKR_ranks_refund_workshop_order = yes }
}

# The wave and the existing breakthrough programme never run together; the
# decision checks both ideas before payment.
WKR_ranks_onslaught_wave_idea_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_wave_after_wave }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_ranks_onslaught_wave_2_idea days = 30 }
	}
	else = {
		add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_ranks_onslaught_wave_idea days = 30 }
	}
}

WKR_ranks_begin_onslaught_wave = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_ranks_onslaught_affordable = yes }
		add_command_power = -25
		add_manpower = -2000
		WKR_ranks_onslaught_wave_idea_effect = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM is the district. One district is fortified at a time.
WKR_ranks_begin_fortify_quarter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_order_pending }
			WKR_ranks_fortify_affordable = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -1000 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -40 }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_order_pending
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_in_progress }
	}
}

# STATE: the fortified district. The modifier expires on its own; its enable
# stops it as soon as WKR loses the district or the administration ends.
WKR_ranks_fortify_quarter_reward = {
	add_building_construction = {
		type = bunker
		level = 1
		instant_build = yes
		province = {
			all_provinces = yes
			limit_to_victory_point > 0
		}
	}
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_fortified_quarter days = 180 }
	if = {
		limit = { WKR = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_city_as_fortress } }
		add_building_construction = {
			type = bunker
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
			province = {
				all_provinces = yes
				limit_to_victory_point > 0
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { anti_air_building < 5 }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = anti_air_building
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

WKR_ranks_finish_fortify_quarter = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_order_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_order_pending
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_ranks_quarter_in_progress }
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			FROM = { WKR_ranks_fortify_quarter_reward = yes }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1000 producer = WKR }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = WKR }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Block 2: partisan war. COUNTRY WKR, FROM is the enemy region.
# Immediate operations pay at completion and leave a timed state flag as
# the local cooldown. Timed operations reserve one national slot.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

WKR_ranks_raid_success = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { owner = { NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 1200 } } } } }
		FROM = { owner = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -1200 } } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1200 producer = WKR }
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 400 producer = WKR }
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 30 producer = WKR }
	army_experience = 3
}

WKR_ranks_raid_failure = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_manpower < 400 } }
		add_manpower = -400
	}
	add_war_support = -0.01
}

WKR_ranks_begin_raid = {
	add_command_power = -15
	add_political_power = -10
	FROM = { set_state_flag = { flag = WKR_ranks_depot_raided value = 1 days = 60 } }
}

WKR_ranks_begin_deserter_contact = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_pending } }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_pending
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_contact }
	}
}

# The enemy loses the defectors only from a pool that still holds them.
WKR_ranks_deserters_cross_over = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_deserters_into_the_ranks }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { owner = { NOT = { has_manpower < 2300 } } } }
			FROM = { owner = { add_manpower = -2300 } }
		}
		add_manpower = 2300
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { owner = { NOT = { has_manpower < 1500 } } } }
			FROM = { owner = { add_manpower = -1500 } }
		}
		add_manpower = 1500
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 900 producer = WKR }
}

# A region taken by WKR yields local volunteers; a region lost to a third
# party breaks the contact and returns the price.
WKR_ranks_finish_deserter_contact = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_pending
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_ranks_deserters_contact }
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { WKR_ranks_enemy_claimant_region = yes }
			}
			WKR_ranks_deserters_cross_over = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			add_manpower = 1000
		}
		else = { add_political_power = 30 }
	}
}

WKR_ranks_begin_rail_sabotage = {
	add_command_power = -20
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -60 }
	FROM = { set_state_flag = { flag = WKR_ranks_rails_blown value = 1 days = 60 } }
}

# STATE: the enemy region. ROOT is WKR.
WKR_ranks_blow_junction_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { infrastructure > 0 }
		damage_building = {
			type = infrastructure
			damage = 2
			repair_speed_modifier = -0.25
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			infrastructure > 0
			ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_explosives_workshops }
		}
		damage_building = {
			type = infrastructure
			damage = 1
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { supply_node > 0 }
		damage_building = {
			type = supply_node
			damage = 1
		}
	}
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_cut_rails days = 30 }
}

WKR_ranks_begin_arsenal_sabotage = {
	add_command_power = -25
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 }
	FROM = { set_state_flag = { flag = WKR_ranks_arsenal_hit value = 1 days = 90 } }
}

# STATE: the enemy region with an arms factory.
WKR_ranks_arsenal_strike_full = {
	damage_building = {
		type = arms_factory
		damage = 2
		repair_speed_modifier = -0.5
	}
	if = {
		limit = { ROOT = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_explosives_workshops } }
		damage_building = {
			type = arms_factory
			damage = 1
		}
	}
}

WKR_ranks_arsenal_strike_partial = {
	damage_building = {
		type = arms_factory
		damage = 1
	}
}

WKR_ranks_begin_underground_cell = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_pending }
			WKR_ranks_cell_affordable = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -1000 }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_pending
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_in_progress }
	}
}

# The rifles stay with the cell; if the region falls to WKR first, the
# members enlist with them, and any other outcome returns the price.
WKR_ranks_finish_underground_cell = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_pending
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_ranks_cell_in_progress }
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { WKR_ranks_enemy_claimant_region = yes }
			}
			FROM = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_underground_cell days = 180 } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			add_manpower = 800
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1000 producer = WKR }
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = 30
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1000 producer = WKR }
		}
	}
}

WKR_ranks_begin_uprising = {
	add_command_power = -25
	add_political_power = -20
}

# The cell is spent; the bonus applies only against the claimant that owns
# the risen region.
WKR_ranks_uprising_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_owned_by = VAD } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_ranks_uprising_vs_vad_idea days = 30 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_owned_by = TVA } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_ranks_uprising_vs_tva_idea days = 30 }
	}
	FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_underground_cell } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_underground_cell }
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_uprising days = 30 }
		if = {
			limit = { infrastructure > 0 }
			damage_building = {
				type = infrastructure
				damage = 1
			}
		}
	}
	add_manpower = 500
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_deserters_into_the_ranks }
		add_manpower = 1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 600 producer = WKR }
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Block 3: railway war. Repair bases, railway batteries, medical runs.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM is the rail district.
WKR_ranks_begin_repair_yard = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_pending }
			WKR_ranks_repair_yard_affordable = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -40 }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = -2 }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_pending
		FROM = { set_state_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_in_progress }
	}
}

# STATE: the rail district. The depot keeps working for a year.
WKR_ranks_repair_yard_reward = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			free_building_slots = {
				building = infrastructure
				size > 0
			}
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = infrastructure
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
		}
	}
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_ranks_repair_yard days = 365 }
}

WKR_ranks_finish_repair_yard = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_pending
		FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_ranks_yard_in_progress }
		if = {
			limit = {
				WKR_administration_active = yes
				FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
			}
			FROM = { WKR_ranks_repair_yard_reward = yes }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 40 producer = WKR }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 2 producer = WKR }
		}
	}
}

WKR_ranks_begin_railway_gun = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending }
			WKR_ranks_railway_gun_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		add_manpower = -1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -100 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount value = 60 }
		WKR_ranks_spend_treasury = yes
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending
	}
}

WKR_ranks_refund_railway_gun = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending
		add_political_power = 50
		add_manpower = 1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 100 producer = WKR }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ranks_treasury_amount value = 60 }
		WKR_ranks_add_treasury = yes
	}
}

# The battery appears in the capital, as a railway gun produced at home would.
WKR_ranks_finish_railway_gun = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			WKR_ranks_railway_gun_site = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_gun_pending
		create_railway_gun = { equipment = railway_gun_equipment_1 }
	}
	else = { WKR_ranks_refund_railway_gun = yes }
}

WKR_ranks_begin_hospital_trains = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending }
			WKR_ranks_hospital_trains_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -20
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = -4 }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending
	}
}

WKR_ranks_refund_hospital_trains = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending
		add_political_power = 20
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 4 producer = WKR }
	}
}

WKR_ranks_finish_hospital_trains = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_ranks_hospital_pending
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment_1 amount = 4 producer = WKR }
		add_manpower = 2000
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_ranks_medical_echelons }
			add_manpower = 1000
		}
	}
	else = { WKR_ranks_refund_hospital_trains = yes }
}

# --- WKR_front: Worker siege economy, free republics and the return to Vorkensberg ---

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# War communism
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = destination state. Dismantles one arms shop in the
# Central district, else in the South-Eastern district. The stored source and
# target are the receipt: no second evacuation starts until the finish effect
# consumes it, and the shop is not lost if the destination falls meanwhile.
WKR_front_begin_evacuation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 32
			controls_state = 32
			32 = { arms_factory > 0 }
		}
		32 = {
			remove_building = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = WKR_front_evacuation_source value = 32 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 33
			controls_state = 33
			33 = { arms_factory > 0 }
		}
		33 = {
			remove_building = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = WKR_front_evacuation_source value = 33 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WKR_front_evacuation_source }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_front_evacuation_target value = FROM }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY WKR. Re-erects the evacuated shop at its destination, else back in its
# district, else hands the machine tools over as equipment. Every branch clears
# the receipt, so a later callback cannot deliver the same shop twice.
WKR_front_finish_evacuation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WKR_front_evacuation_source }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = WKR_front_evacuation_target
				var:WKR_front_evacuation_target = { WKR_front_arms_site = yes }
			}
			var:WKR_front_evacuation_target = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				var:WKR_front_evacuation_source = { WKR_front_arms_site = yes }
			}
			var:WKR_front_evacuation_source = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 1500
				producer = ROOT
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment
				amount = 40
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			clear_variable = WKR_front_evacuation_source
			clear_variable = WKR_front_evacuation_target
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. After the Great Subbotnik each drive also lays one level of
# infrastructure in the home rear first; a fully built rear yields tools instead.
WKR_front_finish_subbotnik = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_front_great_subbotnik }
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
					infrastructure < 5
				}
			}
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { infrastructure < 5 }
				prioritize = { 200 201 33 32 }
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment
				amount = 40
				producer = ROOT
			}
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Union of free republics
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = partner. Payment and delivery happen in one effect after
# both sides are checked again.
WKR_front_deliver_arms_to_republic = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				FROM = { WKR_front_partner_republic = yes }
				WKR_front_arms_delivery_affordable = yes
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = { add_political_power = -15 }
		send_equipment = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 2000
			target = FROM
		}
		send_equipment = {
			type = artillery_equipment
			amount = 24
			target = FROM
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_front_equal_allies }
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_2_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_1_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.01 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = partner. Nothing is charged here: the partner decides.
WKR_front_send_volunteer_request = {
	FROM = {
		set_country_flag = WKR_front_volunteer_request_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.64 days = 1 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: the partner. The pending request is consumed together with both
# transfers, so a duplicate event copy cannot settle it again.
WKR_front_accept_volunteer_request = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_front_volunteer_offer_valid = yes }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_front_volunteer_request_pending
		add_manpower = -1500
		WKR = {
			add_political_power = -25
			add_manpower = 1500
			army_experience = 5
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_volunteers value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = partner. The levy is debited only if the partner agrees.
WKR_front_send_levy_demand = {
	FROM = {
		set_country_flag = WKR_front_levy_request_pending
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.64 days = 1 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: the partner. Same single-settlement contract as the volunteer request.
WKR_front_accept_levy_demand = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_front_levy_offer_valid = yes }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_front_levy_request_pending
		add_manpower = -1000
		send_equipment = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1000
			target = WKR
		}
		WKR = {
			add_political_power = -10
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.01 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			add_manpower = 1000
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_levy value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65 }
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: the partner refuses or lets the request expire.
WKR_front_refuse_partner_request = {
	clr_country_flag = WKR_front_volunteer_request_pending
	clr_country_flag = WKR_front_levy_request_pending
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = WKR
			WKR = { WKR_administration_active = yes }
		}
		WKR = {
			add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_answer_refused value = 1 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.65 }
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Return to Vorkensberg
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

# COUNTRY WKR. Counts the districts of Vorkensberg (states 32-39) the republic
# controls. Eight explicit checks keep the count bounded.
WKR_front_count_city_districts = {
	set_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 32 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 33 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 34 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 35 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 36 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 37 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 38 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { controls_state = 39 }
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_front_city_districts value = 1 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. The assault pays off only if the republic holds more districts
# than at the operation's start; the baseline is cleared either way.
WKR_front_finish_assault_operation = {
	hidden_effect = { WKR_front_count_city_districts = yes }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WKR_front_assault_baseline
			check_variable = {
				var = WKR_front_city_districts
				value = WKR_front_assault_baseline
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_3_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.03 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		}
		add_war_support = 0.02
	}
	hidden_effect = { clear_variable = WKR_front_assault_baseline }
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the blockaded district. The harsher variant follows the
# completed hunger focus.
WKR_front_begin_blockade = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_front_hunger_breaks_the_garrisons }
		FROM = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_front_starving_district
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		FROM = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_front_blockaded_district
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the blockaded district. A garrison that surrendered while
# the ring held hands over its weapons and men.
WKR_front_finish_blockade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1000
			producer = ROOT
		}
		add_manpower = 1500
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_front_legit_plus_1_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.01 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the district. The cells strike only while an enemy at war
# still holds it; a liberated district gives its fighters to the army, and a
# district lost to a third party returns the smuggled rifles.
WKR_front_finish_underground = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } }
		}
		FROM = {
			damage_building = {
				type = arms_factory
				damage = 1
			}
			damage_building = {
				type = infrastructure
				damage = 1
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike
				days = 30
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT }
		}
		add_manpower = 1500
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 500
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. Every district held by an enemy at war rises at once.
WKR_front_raise_the_districts = {
	if = {
		limit = { 32 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		32 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 33 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		33 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 34 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		34 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 35 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		35 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 36 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		36 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 37 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		37 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 38 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		38 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { 39 = { controller = { has_war_with = ROOT } } }
		39 = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_front_underground_strike days = 30 } }
	}
}

# --- executive_ledger_effects ---
# Opens the executive's calculation-trust ledger once per campaign. Every block
# root of the ledger branch calls it; whichever root is taken first sets 50.
WKR_ledger_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_ledger_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_initialized
		set_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_trust value = 50 }
	}
}

# Caller sets the temp WKR_ledger_trust_delta. The ledger stays within 0-100;
# every reader compares against fixed thresholds, so no derived value is kept.
WKR_ledger_add_trust = {
	WKR_ledger_initialize = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_trust value = WKR_ledger_trust_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_trust min = 0 max = 100 }
}

WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_trust_up_3_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_ledger_trust_delta = 3 }
		WKR_ledger_add_trust = yes
	}
}

WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_trust_up_4_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_ledger_trust_delta = 4 }
		WKR_ledger_add_trust = yes
	}
}
