# Builds one arms factory in an owned core district with a free slot, the
# Central and South-East districts first. Without a site the workshops deliver
# rifles instead, so the reward is never lost to a full map.
WKR_ledger_build_arms_factory = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_ledger_has_arms_site = yes }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_ledger_arms_site = yes }
			prioritize = { 32 33 }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1500
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
}

# Civilian counterpart; without a site the cost of the plant goes to the treasury.
WKR_ledger_build_civilian_factory = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_ledger_has_civilian_site = yes }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_ledger_civilian_site = yes }
			prioritize = { 32 33 }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_treasury_200_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 200 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# Remove callback of WKR_ledger_district_audit; FROM is the audited district.
# The interval flag was set at payment, so a lost district stays on its clock.
WKR_ledger_finish_audit = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_treasury_150_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 150 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 150 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 800
			producer = ROOT
		}
		FROM = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_ledger_audited_district
				days = 120
			}
		}
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
	}
}

# VAD keeps its own legitimacy ledger; the executive's answers can only damage it.
WKR_ledger_vad_legitimacy_down_3 = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_vad_legitimacy_down_3_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VAD }
			VAD = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.03 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

WKR_ledger_vad_legitimacy_down_4 = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_vad_legitimacy_down_4_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { country_exists = VAD }
			VAD = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.04 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# Remove callback of WKR_ledger_rescue_operation. One outcome flag is set and
# consumed by the option of event 5 in the same day. A war with VAD makes the
# crossing harder; without VAD there is nobody to reach and the price returns.
WKR_ledger_resolve_rescue = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = VAD }
		random_list = {
			40 = { set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_archive }
			35 = { set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_refused }
			25 = {
				modifier = {
					factor = 1.6
					has_war_with = VAD
				}
				set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_rescue_failed
			}
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.5 }
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_ledger_rescue_refund_tt
		add_political_power = 50
		add_command_power = 25
	}
}

# Remove callback of WKR_ledger_search_commission_tour. The tour counter is the
# commission's mandate (three tours); the finding flag is consumed by event 8.
WKR_ledger_resolve_search_tour = {
	add_to_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_search_tours value = 1 }
	random_list = {
		45 = { set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_survivors }
		35 = { set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_papers }
		20 = { set_country_flag = WKR_ledger_search_false_trail }
	}
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_executive_cw.8 }
}

# Remove callback of WKR_ledger_evacuate_district; FROM is the district. People
# who trust the calculation board the trains: 1500 plus 30 per trust point. A
# district lost before the last train releases half of them and no modifier.
WKR_ledger_finish_evacuation = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_evacuees value = WKR_ledger_trust }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_evacuees value = 30 }
	add_to_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_evacuees value = 1500 }
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT } }
		add_manpower = WKR_ledger_evacuees
		FROM = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_ledger_evacuated_district
				days = 120
			}
		}
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_5 = yes
	}
	else = {
		divide_temp_variable = { var = WKR_ledger_evacuees value = 2 }
		add_manpower = WKR_ledger_evacuees
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
	}
	FROM = { clr_state_flag = WKR_ledger_evacuating }
	clear_variable = WKR_ledger_evacuation_state
}

# --- B2 executive army: the Army of Norms, contract motorization, staff science ---

# COUNTRY (WKR). Fortifies the border provinces of up to two controlled states
# that face an enemy. Without a live front the home state 33 is fortified; if
# WKR has lost it, the sappers' stores go to the depots instead.
WKR_measure_build_border_bunkers = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = { WKR_measure_frontline_state = yes }
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_measure_frontline_state = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = WKR_measure_fortified_now
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				WKR_measure_frontline_state = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = WKR_measure_fortified_now }
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
			}
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = WKR_measure_fortified_now }
			clr_state_flag = WKR_measure_fortified_now
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			owns_state = 33
			controls_state = 33
		}
		33 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 50
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY (WKR). Turns the victory-point towns of up to three frontline states
# into strongpoints. The marker flag only keeps one state from being picked
# twice inside this effect and is cleared before it returns.
WKR_measure_build_depth_belt = {
	set_temp_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left = 3 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left > 0 }
			any_controlled_state = { WKR_measure_depth_belt_site = yes }
		}
		random_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_measure_depth_belt_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_victory_point = yes
				}
			}
			set_state_flag = WKR_measure_depth_belt_marked
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left = 1 }
	}
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WKR_measure_depth_belt_marked }
		clr_state_flag = WKR_measure_depth_belt_marked
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left > 2 }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment_1
			amount = 60
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY (WKR). One military factory in the first home state with a free slot.
# Callers test WKR_measure_arms_works_available and pay their own fallback.
WKR_measure_build_arms_works = {
	if = {
		limit = { 33 = { WKR_measure_arms_works_site = yes } }
		33 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 32 = { WKR_measure_arms_works_site = yes } }
		32 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 200 = { WKR_measure_arms_works_site = yes } }
		200 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 201 = { WKR_measure_arms_works_site = yes } }
		201 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

# COUNTRY (WKR). Settles a casualty audit: 150 manpower per thousand casualties
# suffered since the previous audit, counting at most 40 thousand, then moves the
# baseline to the current total. casualties_k covers every war of the country.
WKR_measure_settle_casualty_audit = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_measure_audit_k value = casualties_k }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = WKR_measure_audit_k value = WKR_measure_audited_casualties_k }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = WKR_measure_audit_k
		min = 0
		max = 40
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_measure_audit_manpower value = WKR_measure_audit_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { var = WKR_measure_audit_manpower value = 150 }
	add_manpower = var:WKR_measure_audit_manpower
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { WKR_measure_audit_k > 9 }
		}
		army_experience = 10
	}
	set_variable = { var = WKR_measure_audited_casualties_k value = casualties_k }
}

# COUNTRY. Debits the temporary input WKR_measure_treasury_cost from the treasury
# and records it as this month's action cost for the economy report.
WKR_measure_spend_treasury = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_measure_treasury_cost }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = WKR_measure_treasury_cost }
	hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

# COUNTRY (WKR). One level of infrastructure in up to two held railway-hub
# states below level 5; with no such state the railway receives trains instead.
# The marker flag only prevents picking a state twice inside this effect.
WKR_measure_improve_hub_roads = {
	set_temp_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left = 2 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left > 0 }
			any_owned_state = { WKR_measure_hub_road_site = yes }
		}
		random_owned_state = {
			limit = { WKR_measure_hub_road_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
			set_state_flag = WKR_measure_hub_road_marked
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left = 1 }
	}
	every_owned_state = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = WKR_measure_hub_road_marked }
		clr_state_flag = WKR_measure_hub_road_marked
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { WKR_measure_sites_left > 1 }
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = train_equipment_1
			amount = 8
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY (WKR). Removes every forward depot when the executive administration
# closes; call it from WKR_close_administration. The array is filled when a depot
# is delivered, once per state.
WKR_measure_close_forward_depots = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = WKR }
		for_each_scope_loop = {
			array = global.WKR_measure_forward_depot_states
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measure_forward_depot } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = WKR_measure_forward_depot }
			}
		}
		clear_array = global.WKR_measure_forward_depot_states
	}
}

# --- WKR contract branch effects (Anton Bagley's executive course) ---

# Treasury movements of the contract programmes. Input: temp WKR_contract_cash,
# a positive amount. The monthly accounting records each movement once.
WKR_contract_treasury_credit = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_contract_cash }
	hidden_effect = {
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = WKR_contract_cash }
}

WKR_contract_treasury_debit = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_contract_cash }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = WKR_contract_cash }
	hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the target state. The stored state is both the receipt
# and the slot: no other target can start until this order settles.
WKR_contract_begin_reconstruction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = WKR_contract_rebuild_state }
			FROM = { WKR_contract_building_site = yes }
			WKR_contract_reconstruction_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -1500
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_contract_cash = 200 }
		WKR_contract_treasury_debit = yes
		set_variable = { var = WKR_contract_rebuild_state value = FROM }
	}
}

# Delivery rechecks the site; a lost or full site hands the crews' output
# over as rifles instead of a factory.
WKR_contract_finish_reconstruction = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WKR_contract_rebuild_state
			FROM = { state = var:WKR.WKR_contract_rebuild_state }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { WKR_contract_building_site = yes } }
			FROM = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = arms_factory
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1500 producer = WKR }
		}
		clear_variable = WKR_contract_rebuild_state
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the partner. The stored payment is the receipt and the
# slot: one partner order runs at a time and settles exactly once.
WKR_contract_begin_partner_order = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = WKR_contract_order_cash }
			FROM = { WKR_contract_is_partner = yes }
			WKR_contract_partner_order_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_contract_cash = 150 }
		WKR_contract_treasury_debit = yes
		set_variable = { var = WKR_contract_order_cash value = 150 }
	}
}

# Delivery rechecks the partner; a lapsed agreement refunds the payment instead.
WKR_contract_finish_partner_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WKR_contract_order_cash }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { WKR_contract_is_partner = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment_0 amount = 1500 producer = WKR }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 80 producer = WKR }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment_1 amount = 12 producer = WKR }
			clear_variable = WKR_contract_order_cash
		}
		else = {
			WKR_contract_refund_partner_order = yes
		}
	}
}

WKR_contract_refund_partner_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = WKR_contract_order_cash }
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_contract_cash value = WKR_contract_order_cash }
		WKR_contract_treasury_credit = yes
		clear_variable = WKR_contract_order_cash
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the buyer. Payment and delivery happen in one effect after
# both sides are rechecked.
WKR_contract_sell_export_lot = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = { WKR_contract_is_partner = yes }
			WKR_contract_export_lot_affordable = yes
		}
		send_equipment = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1500 target = FROM }
		send_equipment = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 target = FROM }
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_contract_cash = 180 }
		WKR_contract_treasury_credit = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the supplier. The flag is the slot of the raw-material
# contract and is cleared when the contract ends or is cancelled.
WKR_contract_begin_raw_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_contract_raw_contract_running }
			FROM = { WKR_contract_is_partner = yes }
			WKR_contract_raw_material_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		send_equipment = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1000 target = FROM }
		set_country_flag = WKR_contract_raw_contract_running
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

WKR_contract_close_raw_contract = {
	clr_country_flag = WKR_contract_raw_contract_running
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the partner. One running guarantee per partner.
WKR_contract_send_guarantee_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				WKR_contract_is_partner = yes
				NOT = { has_idea = WKR_contract_guaranteed_partner_idea }
			}
			WKR_contract_guarantee_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		send_equipment = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 600 target = FROM }
		FROM = { add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_contract_guaranteed_partner_idea days = 120 } }
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = a coalition partner in the same war.
WKR_contract_open_joint_staff = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				WKR_contract_is_coalition_ally = yes
				NOT = { has_idea = WKR_contract_joint_staff_idea }
			}
			WKR_contract_joint_staff_affordable = yes
		}
		add_command_power = -20
		send_equipment = { type = support_equipment amount = 50 target = FROM }
		FROM = { add_timed_idea = { idea = WKR_contract_joint_staff_idea days = 60 } }
		army_experience = 15
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR, FROM = the target state. The stored state is the receipt and the
# slot of the fortified bound.
WKR_contract_begin_fortified_jump = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = WKR_contract_jump_state }
			FROM = { WKR_contract_front_state = yes }
			WKR_contract_fortified_jump_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_command_power = -20
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 producer = WKR }
		set_variable = { var = WKR_contract_jump_state value = FROM }
	}
}

# Delivery requires the region to stay ours; a lost region returns the price
# that was spent on its works.
WKR_contract_finish_fortified_jump = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = WKR_contract_jump_state
			FROM = { state = var:WKR.WKR_contract_jump_state }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = WKR } }
			FROM = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = bunker
					level = 2
					instant_build = yes
					province = {
						all_provinces = yes
						limit_to_border = yes
					}
				}
				add_building_construction = {
					type = anti_air_building
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = 30
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = 80 producer = WKR }
		}
		clear_variable = WKR_contract_jump_state
	}
}


# --- VAD army institution (P2): academy, districts and the Second Department ---

# COUNTRY VAD. Adds the temporary VAD_corps_unrest_delta to every held
# commandant district. The array holds only VAD's district oaths, so the loop
# stays bounded. A rise to 100 starts the existing revolt and reports it once.
VAD_corps_shift_commandant_unrest = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
	for_each_scope_loop = {
		array = global.ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_oath_states
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = VAD
				has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = VAD_corps_unrest_delta }
			clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
			VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY VAD. Intelligence on the two rival claimants that still exist.
VAD_corps_gain_rival_intel_small = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		add_intel = {
			target = WKR
			army_intel = 20
			civilian_intel = 10
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		add_intel = {
			target = TVA
			army_intel = 20
			civilian_intel = 10
		}
	}
}

VAD_corps_gain_rival_intel_large = {
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = WKR }
		add_intel = {
			target = WKR
			army_intel = 40
			civilian_intel = 20
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		add_intel = {
			target = TVA
			army_intel = 40
			civilian_intel = 20
		}
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Army programmes.

# A general without a staff course gains planning; without any general the
# graduates reinforce the command reserve instead.
VAD_corps_graduate_academy_class = {
	army_experience = 20
	if = {
		limit = { any_army_leader = { is_corps_commander = yes } }
		random_army_leader = {
			limit = { is_corps_commander = yes }
			add_planning = 1
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_command_power = 10
	}
}

VAD_corps_deliver_armour_cadres = {
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = ADISCORD_armored_carrier_2163
		amount = 40
		producer = VAD
	}
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
		type = ADISCORD_combat_platform_2170
		amount = 20
		producer = VAD
	}
	army_experience = 10
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# District programmes. Payment writes the shared chancery receipt; delivery
# re-checks the district and otherwise refunds it in full.

VAD_corps_begin_veteran_colony = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_colony_available = yes
			VAD_corps_colony_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		add_manpower = -1500
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -600 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 1500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_rifles value = 600 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 30 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_corps_finish_veteran_colony = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_colony_receipt_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_corps_colony_available = yes }
			FROM = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_veteran_colony }
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 15 }
					clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				}
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# Immediate call-up: the settlement returns its veterans and their rifles.
VAD_corps_apply_colony_call_up = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_call_up_available = yes }
		add_manpower = 3000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 600
			producer = VAD
		}
		army_experience = 10
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
		FROM = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_veteran_colony } }
				remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_veteran_colony }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
				add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 10 }
				clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAD_corps_begin_requisition = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_requisition_available = yes
			VAD_corps_requisition_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 31 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

# A commandant district pays with unrest; a chartered district sees the charter
# broken and the chancery overruled.
VAD_corps_finish_requisition = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_requisition_receipt_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_corps_requisition_available = yes }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment
				amount = 120
				producer = VAD
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = motorized_equipment
				amount = 60
				producer = VAD
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_receive_100 = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
			FROM = {
				set_state_flag = { flag = VAD_corps_requisitioned days = 120 }
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
					add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 25 }
					clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
					VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					FROM = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.02 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
				add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = -0.05 }
			}
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

VAD_corps_begin_loyalty_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_loyalty_charter_available = yes
			VAD_corps_loyalty_charter_affordable = yes
			VAD_oath_slot_free = yes
			FROM = { NOT = { has_state_flag = VAD_oath_in_progress } }
		}
		add_political_power = -50
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_pp value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_oath_kind value = 32 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_begin_district_oath = yes
	}
}

VAD_corps_finish_loyalty_charter = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_loyalty_charter_receipt_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { VAD_corps_loyalty_charter_available = yes }
			FROM = {
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_loyal_district }
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
					set_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 0 }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising } }
					remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_district_uprising }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.02 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			add_power_balance_value = { id = VAD_bop value = 0.05 }
			ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_release_district_oath = yes
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_refund_oath = yes }
	}
}

# STATE scope. Removes this branch's district institutions together with the
# charter or commandanture. Call from VAD_remove_district_administration and
# from the loop in ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_retire_district_oaths.
VAD_corps_clear_district_programmes = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_veteran_colony } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_veteran_colony }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_loyal_district } }
		remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_loyal_district }
	}
	clr_state_flag = VAD_corps_requisitioned
	VAD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Second Department.

VAD_corps_apply_cell_raid = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_raid_available = yes }
		add_stability = 0.01
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes
		FROM = {
			set_state_flag = { flag = VAD_corps_cells_raided days = 60 }
			if = {
				limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } }
				subtract_from_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 20 }
				clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
			}
		}
	}
}

VAD_corps_clear_operation = {
	clear_variable = VAD_corps_op_kind
	clear_variable = VAD_corps_op_state
}

# Cancellation returns the material part of the price: the demolition stores
# or the money that never reached the enemy. Political and command power stay spent.
VAD_corps_abort_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 200 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 3 compare = equals }
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_100 = yes
	}
	VAD_corps_clear_operation = yes
}

VAD_corps_begin_sabotage = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_op_slot_free = yes
			VAD_corps_sabotage_affordable = yes
			FROM = { VAD_corps_front_target = yes }
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_command_power = -10
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -200 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_state value = FROM }
	}
}

VAD_corps_finish_sabotage = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_sabotage_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { VAD_corps_enemy_rear_state = yes } }
			FROM = {
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure > 0 }
					damage_building = {
						type = infrastructure
						damage = 2
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { arms_factory > 0 }
					damage_building = {
						type = arms_factory
						damage = 1
						repair_speed_modifier = -0.5
					}
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_sabotaged_rear days = 30 }
			}
			VAD_corps_clear_operation = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_corps_abort_operation = yes }
	}
}

VAD_corps_begin_bribe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_op_slot_free = yes
			VAD_corps_bribe_affordable = yes
			FROM = { VAD_corps_front_target = yes }
		}
		add_political_power = -40
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_100 = yes
		set_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 2 }
		set_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_state value = FROM }
	}
}

# The garrison's stores change hands: the controller loses what VAD receives.
VAD_corps_finish_bribe = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_bribe_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { FROM = { VAD_corps_enemy_rear_state = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = infantry_equipment
				amount = 1200
				producer = VAD
			}
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
				type = support_equipment
				amount = 80
				producer = VAD
			}
			add_manpower = 800
			FROM = {
				CONTROLLER = {
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = -1200 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -80 }
				}
				add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_corps_bribed_garrison days = 30 }
			}
			VAD_corps_clear_operation = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_corps_abort_operation = yes }
	}
}

VAD_corps_begin_defectors = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_corps_op_slot_free = yes
			VAD_corps_defectors_affordable = yes
			FROM = { has_war_with = ROOT }
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_100 = yes
		set_variable = { var = VAD_corps_op_kind value = 3 }
	}
}

# FROM is the target country.
VAD_corps_finish_defectors = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_defectors_matches = yes }
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					exists = yes
					has_war_with = ROOT
				}
			}
			add_manpower = 2500
			army_experience = 10
			FROM = { add_manpower = -2000 }
			VAD_corps_clear_operation = yes
		}
		else = { VAD_corps_abort_operation = yes }
	}
}

# --- P3: crown works, eastern garrisons and the final campaign ---

# Country scope (VAD). Builds one military factory, preferring the capital;
# without a free site the army receives the rifles that factory would have made.
VAD_realm_build_military_factory = {
	if = {
		limit = { capital_scope = { VAD_realm_arsenal_site = yes } }
		capital_scope = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { any_owned_state = { VAD_realm_arsenal_site = yes } }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { VAD_realm_arsenal_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1500 producer = VAD }
	}
}

# Country scope (VAD). One level of land forts along the outer border of a
# fully held district; support equipment for field works if none qualifies.
VAD_realm_build_border_fort = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
				any_neighbor_state = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = {
				is_fully_controlled_by = ROOT
				any_neighbor_state = { NOT = { is_owned_by = ROOT } }
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 40 producer = VAD }
	}
}

# Country scope (VAD); FROM is the target state of VAD_realm_found_crown_arsenal.
# The decision debits political power visibly; the treasury part is taken here.
# The price is never refunded, so the reservation keeps only the site; it
# blocks every new arsenal until completion or cancellation.
VAD_realm_begin_arsenal = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = -120 }
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 120 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	set_variable = { var = VAD_realm_arsenal_state value = FROM }
	# The flag outlives the 35-day decision only briefly, so an annexed VAD
	# cannot leave a permanent reservation behind.
	FROM = { set_state_flag = { flag = VAD_realm_arsenal_in_progress days = 40 } }
}

VAD_realm_release_arsenal_site = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAD_realm_arsenal_state }
		var:VAD_realm_arsenal_state = { clr_state_flag = VAD_realm_arsenal_in_progress }
	}
	clear_variable = VAD_realm_arsenal_state
}

# Country scope (VAD); FROM is the reserved state. Ownership and the slot are
# checked again at delivery; a lost slot pays out in rifles.
VAD_realm_finish_arsenal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			FROM = {
				is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
				free_building_slots = {
					building = arms_factory
					size > 0
					include_locked = no
				}
			}
		}
		FROM = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1500 producer = VAD }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_realm_arsenals_founded value = 1 }
	VAD_realm_release_arsenal_site = yes
}

# Country scope (VAD); FROM is the requisitioned state. Commandant districts pay
# in the existing unrest track, chartered districts through national stability.
VAD_realm_requisition_local_cost = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_vad_commandant_district } } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAD_realm_requisition_unrest_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			# VAD_check_district_uprising counts a fresh revolt in this temp
			# variable; the existing notice is sent outside the monthly pulse too.
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 0 }
			FROM = {
				add_to_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest value = 25 }
				clamp_variable = { var = VAD_district_unrest min = 0 max = 100 }
				VAD_check_district_uprising = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = VAD_new_district_revolts value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
				country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_claimant.15 hours = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_stability = -0.02
	}
}

# Country scope (VAD); FROM is the occupied state. Rechecked at delivery.
VAD_realm_finish_occupation_directorate = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
		FROM = {
			add_compliance = 10
			add_resistance = -20
		}
		add_manpower = 600
	}
}

# --- Worx protocol: leader-branch inputs (TVA_protocol_ focuses and decisions) ---
# COUNTRY TVA. Zeroes the protocol inputs once per campaign. Every block root calls
# it, so the first block taken sets the baseline and later roots keep progress.
TVA_protocol_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_protocol_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = TVA_protocol_initialized
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 10 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_leak_risk value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells_total value = 0 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Input: temp TVA_protocol_detachment_delta. Only the single-operator
# route and its programmes add to the gauge. Each threshold reading event fires
# once per campaign; reaching 100 settles the crisis at once and leaves the gauge
# at 60, so another crisis needs renewed pressure.
TVA_protocol_add_detachment = {
	TVA_protocol_initialize = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = TVA_protocol_detachment_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		TVA_protocol_detachment_crisis = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 70 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 2 compare = less_than }
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 2 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.3 days = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 40 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 1 compare = less_than }
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 1 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.2 days = 1 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. The silent console is settled before its reading event, so an
# unattended event window cannot skip the cost.
TVA_protocol_detachment_crisis = {
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 60 }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment_stage value = 2 }
	add_stability = -0.05
	add_political_power = -50
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 10 }
	TVA_refresh_administration = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.4 days = 1 }
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Input: temp TVA_protocol_leak_delta. Before the archive choice only
# the discovery event sets a starting value below the threshold; the disclosure
# focus zeroes the gauge and nothing adds to it afterwards. Reaching 60 settles
# the leak at once and keeps 25 points of residual risk.
TVA_protocol_add_leak_risk = {
	TVA_protocol_initialize = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_leak_risk value = TVA_protocol_leak_delta }
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_leak_risk min = 0 max = 100 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_leak_risk value = 60 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		TVA_protocol_archive_leak = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Settles a leaked copy of the 2130 log before its reading event.
TVA_protocol_archive_leak = {
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_leak_risk value = 25 }
	add_stability = -0.03
	set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.06 }
	ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.7 days = 1 }
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Called once by the census focus. Cells exist only in the
# Vorkensberg districts the directorate holds at that moment; the initial counts
# are passed through temp TVA_protocol_cell_delta.
TVA_protocol_initialize_districts = {
	TVA_protocol_initialize = yes
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_protocol_districts_initialized } }
		set_country_flag = TVA_protocol_districts_initialized
		set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_cell_delta = 1 }
		36 = { TVA_protocol_open_district = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_cell_delta = 2 }
		37 = { TVA_protocol_open_district = yes }
		38 = { TVA_protocol_open_district = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_cell_delta = 3 }
		39 = { TVA_protocol_open_district = yes }
		TVA_protocol_recount_cells = yes
	}
}

# STATE 36-39. Input: temp TVA_protocol_cell_delta as the starting count.
TVA_protocol_open_district = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = TVA
			NOT = { has_variable = TVA_protocol_cells }
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells value = TVA_protocol_cell_delta }
		TVA_protocol_refresh_district = yes
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = TVA_protocol_district_cells }
	}
}

# STATE. Recomputes the penalties read by TVA_protocol_district_cells.
TVA_protocol_refresh_district = {
	clamp_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells min = 0 max = 3 }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cell_output value = TVA_protocol_cells }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cell_output value = -0.04 }
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cell_recruits value = TVA_protocol_cells }
	multiply_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cell_recruits value = -0.05 }
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
}

# STATE 36-39. Input: temp TVA_protocol_cell_delta. A district lost after the
# census keeps its value but is not changed until it is retaken. The caller
# recounts the city total.
TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = TVA_protocol_cells
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = TVA
		}
		add_to_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells value = TVA_protocol_cell_delta }
		TVA_protocol_refresh_district = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Input: temp TVA_protocol_cell_delta for every tracked district.
TVA_protocol_change_all_district_cells = {
	36 = { TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = yes }
	37 = { TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = yes }
	38 = { TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = yes }
	39 = { TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = yes }
	TVA_protocol_recount_cells = yes
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Only districts the directorate currently holds count towards the
# city total read by the final city focus. Local factory output changes with
# the cells, so the economy cache is invalidated here.
TVA_protocol_recount_cells = {
	set_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells_total value = 0 }
	36 = { TVA_protocol_count_district = yes }
	37 = { TVA_protocol_count_district = yes }
	38 = { TVA_protocol_count_district = yes }
	39 = { TVA_protocol_count_district = yes }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# STATE. PREV is the district inside the TVA scope.
TVA_protocol_count_district = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = TVA_protocol_cells
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = TVA
		}
		TVA = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_cells_total value = PREV.TVA_protocol_cells } }
	}
}

# --- tva_machine_army_effects ---
# STATE scope; ROOT is TVA. The works are delivered only to a district the
# administration still holds; otherwise the full price returns.
TVA_machine_finish_engineer_sector_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
			hidden_trigger = {
				ROOT = { TVA_administration_active = yes }
			}
			# A province already at the bunker cap would swallow the order.
			any_province_building_level = {
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
				building = bunker
				level < 10
			}
		}
		add_building_construction = {
			type = bunker
			level = 1
			instant_build = yes
			province = {
				all_provinces = yes
				limit_to_border = yes
			}
		}
		ROOT = { army_experience = 3 }
	}
	else = { TVA_machine_refund_engineer_sector_works = yes }
}

TVA_machine_refund_engineer_sector_works = {
	ROOT = {
		add_political_power = 30
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 60 producer = TVA }
	}
}

			level = 1
			instant_build = yes

			limit = {
				free_building_slots = {
					building = anti_air_building
					size > 0
				}
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = anti_air_building
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			ROOT = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_anti_air_equipment_2163 amount = 40 producer = TVA } }
		}
	}
	else = { TVA_machine_refund_automatic_strongpoint = yes }
}

# --- TVA laboratories and allies: block 1, laboratory orders ---
TVA_lab_clear_order = {
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_order_kind
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_order_pp
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_order_treasury
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_order_manpower
}

# Returns the held price of the current laboratory order and frees the slot.
TVA_lab_refund_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = TVA_lab_order_kind }
		add_political_power = TVA_lab_order_pp
		add_manpower = TVA_lab_order_manpower
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = TVA_lab_order_treasury }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = TVA_lab_order_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		TVA_lab_clear_order = yes
	}
}

# The series tier (kinds 1-3) is quoted at payment and kept until delivery.
TVA_lab_begin_prototype_series = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_prototype_series_available = yes
			TVA_lab_prototype_series_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_order_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -30
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = -75 }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 75 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_pp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_treasury value = 75 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_manpower value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_lab_applied_bureaus }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_lab_fundamental_groundwork }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 3 }
		}
	}
}

TVA_lab_finish_prototype_series = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_order_is_series = yes } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_prototype_series_available = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 2 compare = equals } } }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 2250 producer = TVA }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 45 producer = TVA }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = artillery_equipment amount = 12 producer = TVA }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_up_8_tt
				hidden_effect = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 8 } }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 3 compare = equals } } }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1000 producer = TVA }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 20 producer = TVA }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_cadre_up_6_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_up_3_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 6 }
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 3 }
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 1500 producer = TVA }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 producer = TVA }
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_up_5_tt
				hidden_effect = { add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 5 } }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				TVA_lab_clear_order = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_order = yes }
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_theory_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_theory_cycle_available = yes
			TVA_lab_theory_cycle_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_order_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -45
		add_manpower = -500
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_pp value = 45 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_treasury value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_manpower value = 500 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 4 }
	}
}

TVA_lab_finish_theory_cycle = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_order_kind value = 4 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_theory_cycle_available = yes } }
			add_tech_bonus = { name = TVA_lab_order_theory_cycle bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 category = computing_tech }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_cadre_up_12_tt
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_down_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 12 }
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -5 }
				TVA_lab_clear_order = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_order = yes }
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_range_trials = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_range_trials_available = yes
			TVA_lab_range_trials_affordable = yes
			NOT = { has_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp }
		}
		add_command_power = -15
		add_political_power = -25
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_cp value = 15 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_pp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_kind value = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_lab_applied_bureaus }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_kind value = 2 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_lab_fundamental_groundwork }
			set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_kind value = 3 }
		}
	}
}

TVA_lab_refund_range = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp }
		add_command_power = TVA_lab_range_cp
		add_political_power = TVA_lab_range_pp
		clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp
		clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_pp
		clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_kind
	}
}

TVA_lab_finish_range_trials = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_range_trials_available = yes } }
			army_experience = 12
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_kind value = 2 compare = equals } } }
				add_tech_bonus = { name = TVA_lab_order_range_trials bonus = 0.35 uses = 1 category = artillery }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_range_kind value = 3 compare = equals } } }
				add_tech_bonus = { name = TVA_lab_order_range_trials bonus = 0.35 uses = 1 category = electronics }
			}
			else = {
				add_tech_bonus = { name = TVA_lab_order_range_trials bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 category = support_tech }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp
				clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_pp
				clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_kind
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_range = yes }
	}
}

# --- TVA laboratories and allies: block 2, partners ---
# Each offer marks the partner as pending until its reply event settles or
# declines the deal; the reply event's first option is the timeout default.
TVA_lab_send_holden_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA_lab_holden_offer_available = yes }
		WPS = {
			set_country_flag = TVA_lab_holden_offer_pending
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.64 days = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# WPS scope. The syndicate's engineers and TVA's weapons change hands together.
TVA_lab_accept_holden_exchange = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA_lab_holden_exchange_valid = yes }
		add_manpower = -800
		TVA = {
			send_equipment = { target = ROOT type = support_equipment amount = 30 }
			send_equipment = { target = ROOT type = infantry_equipment amount = 1200 }
			add_manpower = 800
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_lab_engineering_union }
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 15 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 10 }
			}
			TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			set_country_flag = { flag = TVA_lab_reply_holden_accepted days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.67 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_holden_offer_pending
}

TVA_lab_decline_holden_exchange = {
	clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_holden_offer_pending
	if = {
		limit = { country_exists = TVA }
		TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.67 days = 1 } }
	}
}

TVA_lab_send_firnov_offer = {

		}

			send_equipment = { target = ROOT type = artillery_equipment amount = 24 }

			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 60 }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 5 }
			add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -8 }
			TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			set_country_flag = { flag = TVA_lab_reply_firnov_accepted days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.68 days = 1 }
		}

			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		TVA = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = -150 }
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 150 }
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
			add_power_balance_value = { id = TVA_bop value = -0.05 }
			set_country_flag = { flag = TVA_lab_reply_kefreyt_accepted days = 3 }
			country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.69 days = 1 }
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_kefreyt_offer_pending
}

	clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_kefreyt_offer_pending

		TVA = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.69 days = 1 } }
	}
}

# --- TVA laboratories and allies: block 3, operations of the restart ---
TVA_lab_clear_operation = {
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_op_kind
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_op_cp
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_op_pp
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_op_support
	clear_variable = TVA_lab_op_treasury
}

# Returns the held price of the current operation and frees the slot.
TVA_lab_refund_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = TVA_lab_op_kind }
		add_command_power = TVA_lab_op_cp
		add_political_power = TVA_lab_op_pp
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = TVA_lab_op_support producer = TVA }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = TVA_lab_op_treasury }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = TVA_lab_op_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_blackout = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_blackout_available = yes
			TVA_lab_blackout_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_operation_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_command_power = -20
		add_political_power = -15
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_cp value = 20 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_pp value = 15 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_treasury value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 1 }
	}
}

TVA_lab_finish_blackout = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 1 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_blackout_available = yes } }
			army_experience = 5
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_up_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = 5 }
				TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_operation = yes }
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_offensive = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_offensive_available = yes
			TVA_lab_offensive_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_operation_slot_free = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_lab_offensive_executed }
		}
		add_command_power = -30
		add_political_power = -40
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_cp value = 30 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_pp value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_treasury value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 2 }
	}
}

# The offensive is spent once it runs its course; the capital decides its result.
TVA_lab_finish_offensive = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_offensive_available = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = TVA_lab_capital_held_tt
						controls_state = 32
					}
				}
				add_political_power = 60
				add_stability = 0.03
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_legitimacy_up_5_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.05 }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				army_experience = 15
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_country_flag = TVA_lab_offensive_executed
				TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_operation = yes }
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_repair_columns = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_repair_columns_available = yes
			TVA_lab_repair_columns_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_operation_slot_free = yes
		}
		add_command_power = -15
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = -40 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_cp value = 15 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_pp value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_support value = 40 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_treasury value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 3 }
	}
}

TVA_lab_finish_repair_columns = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_repair_columns_available = yes } }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 20 producer = TVA }
			army_experience = 5
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_down_5_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -5 }
				TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_operation = yes }
	}
}

TVA_lab_begin_grid_restart = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			TVA_lab_grid_restart_available = yes
			TVA_lab_grid_restart_affordable = yes
			TVA_lab_operation_slot_free = yes
			controls_state = 32
			NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_lab_capital_grid_restarted }
		}
		add_command_power = -25
		add_political_power = -50
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = -80 }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 80 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_cp value = 25 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_pp value = 50 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_support value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_treasury value = 80 }
		set_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 4 }
	}
}

# Without the Central District at delivery the crews return the unused
# treasury and work on the directorate's own grid instead; the restart can be
# ordered again after the capital is retaken.
TVA_lab_finish_grid_restart = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = TVA_lab_op_kind value = 4 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_lab_grid_restart_available = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = {
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = TVA_lab_capital_held_tt
						controls_state = 32
					}
				}
				add_timed_idea = { idea = TVA_lab_capital_under_current days = 70 }
				add_stability = 0.03
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_legitimacy_up_6_tt
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_down_20_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.06 }
					ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -20 }
					set_country_flag = TVA_lab_capital_grid_restarted
				}
			}
			else = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_grid_restart_fallback_tt
				hidden_effect = {
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = TVA_lab_op_treasury }
					add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = TVA_lab_op_treasury }
					ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
					add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -5 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
			}
		}
		else = { TVA_lab_refund_operation = yes }
	}
}

# --- Lifecycle hooks for the lead ---
# TVA scope: refunds every held laboratory, range and operation price. Safe to
# call repeatedly; intended for TVA_close_administration.
TVA_lab_close_programmes = {
	# Partner offers of the laboratories are answered by their own reply events;
	# an offer that never reached its partner must not block the next one.
	WPS = { clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_holden_offer_pending }
	TGD = { clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_firnov_offer_pending }
	VAL = { clr_country_flag = TVA_lab_kefreyt_offer_pending }
	TVA_lab_refund_order = yes
	TVA_lab_refund_range = yes
	TVA_lab_refund_operation = yes
}

# ROOT receives TVA's held prices before TVA is consumed by unification;
# intended next to TVA_settle_administration_handoff.
TVA_lab_settle_handoff = {
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { has_variable = TVA_lab_order_kind } }
		add_political_power = TVA.TVA_lab_order_pp
		add_manpower = TVA.TVA_lab_order_manpower
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = TVA.TVA_lab_order_treasury }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = TVA.TVA_lab_order_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		TVA = { TVA_lab_clear_order = yes }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { has_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp } }
		add_command_power = TVA.TVA_lab_range_cp
		add_political_power = TVA.TVA_lab_range_pp
		TVA = {
			clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_cp
			clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_pp
			clear_variable = TVA_lab_range_kind
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { TVA = { has_variable = TVA_lab_op_kind } }
		add_command_power = TVA.TVA_lab_op_cp
		add_political_power = TVA.TVA_lab_op_pp
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = TVA.TVA_lab_op_support producer = ROOT }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = TVA.TVA_lab_op_treasury }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = TVA.TVA_lab_op_treasury }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		TVA = { TVA_lab_clear_operation = yes }
	}
}

# --- W4: Worker story arcs and round-two choices (WKR presidential course) ---
# Three event chains of ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.100-139 carry their choices
# in WKR_vow_* country flags and variables. Each chain clears its own state in
# its closing event; WKR_vow_clear_story_state clears all three when the
# administration closes before a chain has finished.

# --- The scar of the Tower: the chairman's strain and the inquiry ---
# COUNTRY WKR. The strain counts how hard Worker is driven after the Tower.
# The rostrum speech and the front rally read it; the Tower epilogue clears it.
WKR_vow_add_strain = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_strain_plus_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_vow_strain value = 1 }
	}
}

WKR_vow_ease_strain = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { WKR_vow_strain > 0 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_strain_minus_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_vow_strain value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

WKR_vow_rest_strain = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_strain_reset_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_variable = { WKR_vow_strain = 0 }
	}
}

WKR_vow_add_evidence = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_evidence_plus_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_vow_tower_evidence value = 1 }
	}
}

# Called from the answer that settles the inquiry (event 109 or 112). The
# epilogue waits for both the settled case and the capstone focus; whichever
# comes second dispatches it.
WKR_vow_close_tower_case = {
	set_country_flag = WKR_vow_tower_case_closed
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_vow_the_scar_of_the_tower }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.111 days = 3 }
	}
}

WKR_vow_clear_tower_story = {
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_suspect_vad
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_suspect_tva
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_suspect_councils
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_seal_severov
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_seal_zarnitskaya
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_tower_case_closed
	clear_variable = WKR_vow_strain
	clear_variable = WKR_vow_tower_evidence
}

# --- Commissars and poets ---

	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = WKR_vow_commissars_and_poets }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.123 days = 3 }

}

	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_ladova_silenced

# --- The voice of the communities ---
# Kept promises and deferred debts are counted until the community congress
# ("WKR_vow_voice_of_the_communities"); its event reads their balance.
WKR_vow_keep_promise = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_promise_kept_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_vow_promises_kept value = 1 }
	}
}

WKR_vow_owe_promise = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_promise_owed_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_vow_promises_owed value = 1 }
	}
}

# Pays every open debt at once: kept promises catch up with the owed ones.
WKR_vow_settle_promises = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_vow_promises_settled_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = WKR_vow_promises_owed
					value = WKR_vow_promises_kept
					compare = greater_than
				}
			}
			set_variable = { WKR_vow_promises_kept = WKR_vow_promises_owed }
		}
	}
}

WKR_vow_clear_community_story = {
	clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_delegation_suspected
	clear_variable = WKR_vow_promises_kept
	clear_variable = WKR_vow_promises_owed
}

# Called by WKR_close_administration: a chain cut short by the end of the
# administration leaves no state behind.
WKR_vow_clear_story_state = {
	WKR_vow_clear_tower_story = yes
	WKR_vow_clear_poets_story = yes
	WKR_vow_clear_community_story = yes
}

# --- Focus deliveries with a material fallback ---
# COUNTRY WKR. One arms factory in a rear region with a free slot; without a
# site the same labour delivers rifles.
WKR_vow_rear_arms_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { WKR_front_rear_arms_site = yes }
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_front_rear_arms_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1500
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. One civilian factory in a rear region; without a site the
# cooperatives pay its price into the treasury.
WKR_vow_rear_civilian_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { WKR_front_rear_civilian_site = yes }
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_front_rear_civilian_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else = {
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_100 = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. The first stone: one level of infrastructure in a Vorkensberg
# district the republic holds, the Central district first.
WKR_vow_capital_works = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { WKR_vow_capital_works_site = yes }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				32 = { WKR_vow_capital_works_site = yes }
			}
			32 = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { WKR_vow_capital_works_site = yes }
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 60
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# --- Programmes: receipts and refunds ---
# Every paid programme keeps one country flag as its receipt. Delivery,
# cancellation and refund clear it; prices are fixed, so no stored amount.

WKR_vow_begin_deputies_council = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending }
			WKR_vow_deputies_affordable = yes
		}
		add_command_power = -15
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_50 = yes
		set_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending
	}
}

WKR_vow_refund_deputies_council = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending
		add_command_power = 15
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes
	}
}

# The holder of the chairman's seal adds his own weight to the deputies' work.
WKR_vow_finish_deputies_council = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_deputies_pending
		add_stability = 0.02
		add_political_power = 30
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.03 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_seal_severov }
			army_experience = 10
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_seal_zarnitskaya }
			add_stability = 0.01
		}
	}
	else = { WKR_vow_refund_deputies_council = yes }
}

# STATE scope (FROM of the targeted decision) for the engineer brigades.
WKR_vow_begin_engineer_brigade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending }
			WKR_vow_brigade_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_300 = yes
		set_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending
	}
}

WKR_vow_refund_engineer_brigade = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending
		add_political_power = 25
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_300 = yes
	}
}

# Delivery rechecks the district: a civilian factory first, a road network
# when the factory slots are taken, the price back when neither is possible.
WKR_vow_finish_engineer_brigade = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			FROM = { WKR_vow_brigade_site = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_vow_engineer_brigade_pending
		if = {
			limit = {
				FROM = {
					free_building_slots = {
						building = industrial_complex
						size > 0
					}
				}
			}
			FROM = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			FROM = {
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else = { WKR_vow_refund_engineer_brigade = yes }
}

# --- B4 executive story: the price of one line, the councils, the man of numbers ---

# Treasury booking of the B4 programmes and events. The caller sets the temp
# variable WKR_audit_cash; every change is recorded in the monthly action ledger.
WKR_audit_spend_treasury = {
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_audit_cash }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = WKR_audit_cash }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

WKR_audit_receive_treasury = {
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = WKR_audit_cash }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = WKR_audit_cash }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Reward of WKR_audit_victory_loan: citizens subscribe in proportion to their
# trust in the executive's figures.
WKR_audit_victory_loan_payout = {
	hidden_effect = { WKR_ledger_initialize = yes }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { WKR_ledger_trust < 60 } } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_loan_high_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { WKR_audit_cash = 250 }
			WKR_audit_receive_treasury = yes
		}
	}
	else = {
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_loan_low_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			set_temp_variable = { WKR_audit_cash = 150 }
			WKR_audit_receive_treasury = yes
		}
	}
}

# Remove callback of WKR_audit_bond_subscription: 100 plus 2 per trust point.
WKR_audit_finish_bond_drive = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_bond_paid_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		WKR_ledger_initialize = yes
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_cash value = WKR_ledger_trust }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_cash value = 2 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_cash value = 100 }
		WKR_audit_receive_treasury = yes
	}
}

# Remove callback of WKR_audit_field_recount. The commission finds people
# missing from the ration lists: 500 plus 20 per trust point. A district lost
# before delivery cancels the recount and refunds it instead.
WKR_audit_finish_field_recount = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_field_recount_paid_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_found value = WKR_ledger_trust }
		multiply_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_found value = 20 }
		add_to_temp_variable = { var = WKR_audit_found value = 500 }
		add_manpower = WKR_audit_found
	}
	WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
}

# Remove callback of WKR_audit_control_raid; FROM is the district. The interval
# flag set at payment outlasts the modifier, so a raid never stacks on itself.
WKR_audit_finish_control_raid = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_audit_treasury_plus_80_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		set_temp_variable = { WKR_audit_cash = 80 }
		WKR_audit_receive_treasury = yes
	}
	WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = yes
	FROM = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = WKR_audit_inspected_district
			days = 120
		}
	}
}

# Prisoner works: one level of infrastructure in an owned core district below
# the cap, the Central, South-Eastern, Remmel and Kairholm districts first.
# Without a site the columns salvage support equipment instead.
WKR_audit_prisoner_works = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_audit_has_infrastructure_site = yes }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_audit_infrastructure_site = yes }
			prioritize = { 32 33 200 201 }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 40
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
}

	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_line_censored

# Chain B: strike path flags, cleared by every answer of event .116.

	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_talks
	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_force
	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_ration
	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_settled
	clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_strike_crushed
}

# Chain C finale: every option of event .134 calls this after its own choice.
# Bagley's remembered health, family and village visit pay out once, and every
# flag of the chain is cleared.
WKR_audit_settle_numbers_man = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_health_rested }
		add_political_power = 25
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_health_hidden }
		add_stability = -0.02
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_home }
		add_stability = 0.02
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_lost
				has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_renounced
			}
		}
		add_war_support = 0.02
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_refused }
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_letters }
		add_political_power = 10
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_audit_bagley_saw_the_village }
		WKR_ledger_trust_up_4 = yes
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_letters
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_renounced
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_exchange
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_exposed
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_paid
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_refused
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_home
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_family_lost
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_health_rested
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_health_ignored
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_health_hidden
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_portrait_kept
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_portrait_removed
		clr_country_flag = WKR_audit_bagley_saw_the_village
	}
}

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# P4: story arcs and programmes of Petrichev's branch (VAD_crownless_*).
# All effects are COUNTRY scope (VAD).

# Treasury bookings with an explicit temporary amount, recorded in the monthly
# action accounting like ADISCORD_economy_spend_*/receive_*.
VAD_crownless_receive_treasury = {
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAD_crownless_treasury_delta }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = VAD_crownless_treasury_delta }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

VAD_crownless_spend_treasury = {
	subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAD_crownless_treasury_delta }
	add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = VAD_crownless_treasury_delta }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# --- Chain A: the court's pull towards a coronation (0-6) ---

VAD_crownless_crown_pull_up = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_crownless_crown_pull value = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAD_crownless_crown_pull min = 0 max = 6 }
}

VAD_crownless_crown_pull_down = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_crownless_crown_pull value = -1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAD_crownless_crown_pull min = 0 max = 6 }
}

# The houses send their bill (event .106) only if their money was taken at event .102.
VAD_crownless_crown_after_rumours = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAD_crownless_court_debt }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.106 days = 25 }
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.107 days = 25 }
	}
}

# A staff split over an acclamation (event .108) needs a pull of at least 3.
VAD_crownless_crown_after_petition = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { VAD_crownless_crown_pull > 2 } }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.108 days = 20 }
	}
	else = {
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.109 days = 20 }
	}
}

# Called by every option of event .109; the result flag set there stays.
VAD_crownless_close_crown_chain = {
	clear_variable = VAD_crownless_crown_pull
	clr_country_flag = VAD_crownless_genealogy_kept
	clr_country_flag = VAD_crownless_vow_sworn
	clr_country_flag = VAD_crownless_regalia_held
	clr_country_flag = VAD_crownless_court_debt
}

}

# Called by every option of event .117. Removing the lean variable also tells the
# P4 crown-works focuses that the rivalry is settled.
VAD_crownless_close_staff_chain = {
