	clear_variable = VAD_crownless_staff_feud

# Terminal cleanup for the end of the VAD administration: closes unfinished

VAD_crownless_clear_story = {
	VAD_crownless_close_crown_chain = yes
	VAD_crownless_close_staff_chain = yes
	VAD_crownless_close_rear_chain = yes

	clr_country_flag = VAD_crownless_rear_lost
}

# --- Programmes ---

# Gendarmerie companies: delivery rechecks the charter and commandant districts;
# without one the companies return their men and rifles to the reserve. A post
# already standing in a district is not renewed early.
VAD_crownless_finish_gendarmerie = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_corps_has_administered_district = yes }
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				VAD_corps_administered_district = yes
				NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crownless_gendarmerie_post } }
			}
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAD_crownless_gendarmerie_post days = 120 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { VAD_corps_unrest_delta = -25 }
		VAD_corps_shift_commandant_unrest = yes
		add_stability = 0.01
	}
	else = {
		add_manpower = 1000
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 500 producer = VAD }
	}
}

# Open the gates from within: the capital must still be enemy-held during a live
# central showdown, otherwise the treasury and command power are returned.
VAD_crownless_finish_gates_operation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAD_realm_showdown_live_war = yes
			VAD_crownless_capital_held_by_enemy = yes
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAD_crownless_gates_opened days = 45 }
		32 = {
			controller = {
				if = {
					limit = { has_manpower > 2000 }
					add_manpower = -2000
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_command_power = 15
		set_temp_variable = { VAD_crownless_treasury_delta = 150 }
		VAD_crownless_receive_treasury = yes
	}
}

# --- TVA proof: story chains ---
# Country flags of the three story chains live only while their chain runs.
# Each chain finale clears its own flags; the last restart event clears all of
# them. Path flags of one chain are mutually exclusive and are switched only
# through the helpers below.

# Chain "the voice in the machine" (events 100-111).
TVA_proof_voice_to_trusted = {
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_corrected
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_sabotaged
	set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_trusted
}

TVA_proof_voice_to_corrected = {
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_trusted
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_sabotaged
	set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_corrected
}

TVA_proof_voice_to_sabotaged = {
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_trusted
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_corrected
	set_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_sabotaged
}

# Called once the console's path is chosen; the matching consequence arrives
# after the circuit has worked on that path for a while.
TVA_proof_voice_schedule_path = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_trusted }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.105 days = 30 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_corrected }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.106 days = 30 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_sabotaged }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.107 days = 21 }
	}
}

TVA_proof_voice_clear_flags = {
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_open
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_ranked
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_trusted
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_corrected
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_voice_sabotaged
	clr_country_flag = TVA_proof_koch_protected
}

# Chain finale. Webel's letter follows only on the single-operator route while
# Worx's detachment is still high; event 111 rechecks the gauge on arrival.
TVA_proof_voice_close = {
	TVA_proof_voice_clear_flags = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = TVA_protocol_single_operator
			check_variable = { var = TVA_protocol_detachment value = 50 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.111 days = 10 }
	}
}

# --- W5: the decisive days (WKR presidential course) ---
# The story line ADISCORD_vorkerland_worker_cw.140-167 carries its choices in
# WKR_last_* country flags and the WKR_last_exposure variable. Each thread
# clears what it has read; event 167 clears the core state, and
# WKR_last_clear_story_state clears everything when the administration closes.

# --- The chairman's exposure ---
# COUNTRY WKR. How far Worker exposes himself at the front in the last months.
# The breach, the shot at the water tower, the last speech and the decisive
# days read it.
WKR_last_add_exposure = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_last_exposure_plus_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_last_exposure value = 1 }
	}
}

WKR_last_ease_exposure = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { WKR_last_exposure > 0 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_last_exposure_minus_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			subtract_from_variable = { var = WKR_last_exposure value = 1 }
		}
	}
}

# --- Dispatchers ---

	}
}

# --- Focus deliveries with a material fallback ---
# COUNTRY WKR. The reopened works: one arms factory in a held district of
# Vorkensberg, the Industrial district first; without a slot the shifts
# deliver rifles.
WKR_last_capital_arms_works = {
	if = {
		limit = { 39 = { WKR_last_capital_arms_site = yes } }
		39 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			any_owned_state = { WKR_last_capital_arms_site = yes }
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_last_capital_arms_site = yes }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = arms_factory
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = infantry_equipment
			amount = 1500
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY WKR. One level of air defence over a held district, the captured
# Northern and Industrial districts first, then the Central one; without a
# site the batteries are handed over as guns.
WKR_last_fortify_the_capital = {
	if = {
		limit = { 36 = { WKR_last_capital_air_defence_site = yes } }
		36 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = anti_air_building
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 39 = { WKR_last_capital_air_defence_site = yes } }
		39 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = anti_air_building
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { 32 = { WKR_last_capital_air_defence_site = yes } }
		32 = {
			add_building_construction = {
				type = anti_air_building
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = anti_air_equipment
			amount = 24
			producer = WKR
		}
	}
}

# --- Programmes: receipts and refunds ---
# Every paid programme with a mixed price keeps one country flag as its
# receipt; delivery, cancellation and refund clear it. Prices are fixed.

WKR_last_begin_rear_session = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending }
			WKR_last_session_affordable = yes
		}
		add_political_power = -25
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_50 = yes
		set_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending
	}
}

WKR_last_refund_rear_session = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending }
		clr_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending
		add_political_power = 25
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes
	}
}

WKR_last_finish_rear_session = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_last_session_pending
		add_manpower = 2000
		add_stability = 0.02
		if = {
			limit = { has_power_balance = { id = WKR_bop } }
			add_power_balance_value = { id = WKR_bop value = 0.03 }
		}
	}
	else = { WKR_last_refund_rear_session = yes }
}

# Screened prisoners go to the march companies; an agent among them breaks a

			WKR_siege_destroy_enemy_cell = yes
		}
		else = {
			add_political_power = 25
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_political_power = 25
	}
}

# Delivery rechecks the district; a lost district returns the whole price.

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = WKR_last_bread_pending
			WKR_administration_active = yes
			FROM = { WKR_last_capital_district = yes }
		}
		clr_country_flag = WKR_last_bread_pending
		FROM = {
			add_dynamic_modifier = {
				modifier = WKR_last_fed_district
				days = 180
			}
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		add_stability = 0.01
		set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = 0.01 }
		ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
	}
	else = { WKR_last_refund_bread_train = yes }
}

# --- B5 executive story: the decisive days ---
# All effects are COUNTRY scope (WKR). Treasury changes go through
# WKR_audit_spend_treasury / WKR_audit_receive_treasury with the temp WKR_audit_cash.

# The rival claimants' legitimacy suffers only while they fight the executive.
WKR_tally_enemy_legitimacy_down_3 = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_tally_enemy_legitimacy_down_3_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = TVA
				has_war_with = TVA
			}
			TVA = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.03 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				country_exists = VAD
				has_war_with = VAD
			}
			VAD = {
				set_temp_variable = { ADISCORD_vorkerland_legitimacy_delta = -0.03 }
				ADISCORD_vorkerland_add_legitimacy = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

# The capital's hunger ledger: harsh answers in the taken districts raise it,
# relief lowers it. Event .171 reads it once; WKR_tally_close_story_chain clears it.
WKR_tally_city_debt_up = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_tally_city_debt_up_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_tally_city_debt value = 1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = WKR_tally_city_debt min = -10 max = 10 }
	}
}

WKR_tally_city_debt_down = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = WKR_tally_city_debt_down_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = WKR_tally_city_debt value = -1 }
		clamp_variable = { var = WKR_tally_city_debt min = -10 max = 10 }
	}
}

# Reward of WKR_tally_ledger_of_the_capital: one infrastructure level in an owned
# capital district below the cap, the Central and South-Eastern ones first.
# Without a site the engineers' stores go to the depots instead.
WKR_tally_build_city_infrastructure = {
	if = {
		limit = { WKR_tally_has_city_infrastructure_site = yes }
		random_owned_controlled_state = {
			limit = { WKR_tally_city_infrastructure_site = yes }
			prioritize = { 32 33 }
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = {
			type = support_equipment
			amount = 40
			producer = ROOT
		}
	}
}

# Terminal cleanup for the end of the WKR administration (WKR_close_administration):
# closes an unfinished story and removes the one-time markers of the district
# scenes (states 34-39) and of the collected council arsenals (32, 33, 200, 201).
# A running ring is cancelled by its decision, which clears the slot flag too;
# the flag is repeated here because the administration may end with the tag.
WKR_tally_clear_story_state = {
	WKR_tally_close_story_chain = yes
	clr_country_flag = WKR_tally_ring_running
	34 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_district_recorded }
	35 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_district_recorded }
	36 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_district_recorded }
	37 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_district_recorded }
	38 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_district_recorded }

	32 = { clr_state_flag = WKR_tally_arsenal_collected }

# --- P5: the decisive days (events .140-.170, VAD_standard_*) ---
# All effects are COUNTRY scope (VAD) unless a comment says otherwise.

# Severity of the decisive days (0-6). Field courts, executions, prisoners'
# companies and stripped works raise it; mercy and civil councils lower it.
# Read by the city epilogue .166 and cleared by VAD_standard_clear_story.
VAD_standard_severity_up = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity value = 1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity min = 0 max = 6 }
}

VAD_standard_severity_up_2 = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity value = 2 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity min = 0 max = 6 }
}

VAD_standard_severity_down = {
	add_to_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity value = -1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAD_standard_severity min = 0 max = 6 }
}

# Called once by the outcome of the leader's crisis (.149, .160 or .161).
# An epilogue whose trigger fails on its date is skipped; the closing event
# .169 comes last and clears the story state.
VAD_standard_dispatch_epilogues = {
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.164 days = 6 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.165 days = 10 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.166 days = 14 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.167 days = 18 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.168 days = 22 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.170 days = 26 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_restoration_cw.169 days = 32 }
}

# --- Programmes ---

# Filtration commissions: the screened prisoners join the reserve only while
# the army still holds a district of the capital; otherwise a smaller draft
# from the field camps arrives.
VAD_standard_finish_filtration = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_standard_holds_a_capital_district = yes }
		add_manpower = 2000
		add_stability = 0.01
		VAD_standard_severity_up = yes
	}
	else = {
		add_manpower = 800
	}
}

# Technical archives: delivered only while a technical district is still held;
# otherwise the full price is returned.
VAD_standard_finish_archives = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAD_standard_holds_a_technical_district = yes }
		add_tech_bonus = {
			name = VAD_standard_worx_archives
			bonus = 0.5
			uses = 1
			category = computing_tech
		}
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 40 producer = VAD }
	}
	else = {
		add_political_power = 30
		set_temp_variable = { VAD_crownless_treasury_delta = 100 }
		VAD_crownless_receive_treasury = yes
	}
}

# Terminal cleanup of the decisive days. Called by every option of the closing
# event .169; the lead also calls it where the VAD administration ends, so an
# unfinished line leaves no flags or variables behind.
VAD_standard_clear_story = {
	clear_variable = VAD_standard_severity
	clear_variable = VAD_standard_assault_checks
	clear_variable = VAD_standard_crisis_checks
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_central
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_aim_works
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_letters_open
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_old_city_shelled
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_rival_in_reserve
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_bearer_guards
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_bearer_line
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_bearer_pad
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_bearer_leader
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_teller_with_worker
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_wedel_prefect
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_prisoners_judged
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_commissars_shot
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_commissars_exiled
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_commissars_to_korsak
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_engineers_kept
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_petition_refused
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_petition_kept
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_crown_declined
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_crown_shown
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_silence_recorded
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_korsak_posts
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_card_index_copied
	clr_country_flag = VAD_standard_crisis_done
}

# --- TVA endgame: the decisive days (story line, events 140-178) ---
# Country flags of the endgame live from the opening scene of a chain to the
# handover of the shift (178), which clears them all together with any flags
# the earlier chains left open. The chain ledgers (150, 155, 160) need both
# their rejoin focus and the last scene of their chain; whichever comes second
# dispatches the ledger, and the ledger's own flag prevents a second copy.

# COUNTRY TVA. Called by the last scene of the siege chain (149).
TVA_endgame_siege_reach_end = {
	set_country_flag = TVA_endgame_siege_last_scene
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_endgame_map_of_the_siege }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.150 days = 7 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Called by the last scene of the grid chain (154).
TVA_endgame_grid_reach_end = {
	set_country_flag = TVA_endgame_grid_last_scene
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_endgame_the_grid_holds }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.155 days = 7 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Called by the last scene of the prisoner chain (159).
TVA_endgame_prisoners_reach_end = {
	set_country_flag = TVA_endgame_prisoners_last_scene
	if = {
		limit = { has_completed_focus = TVA_endgame_the_captured_staff }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.160 days = 7 }
	}
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Called once by TVA_endgame_the_last_shift. The three outcomes are
# queued for the same day; their triggers exclude each other and read the war
# on arrival, and the first to fire marks the reckoning as done.
TVA_endgame_schedule_reckoning = {
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.165 days = 21 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.166 days = 21 }
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.167 days = 21 }
}

# COUNTRY TVA. Called by every answer of the last log entry (168). The epilogue
# scenes are queued once and the handover (178) closes the story after them.
TVA_endgame_schedule_epilogues = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = TVA_endgame_epilogues_open } }
		set_country_flag = TVA_endgame_epilogues_open
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.169 days = 5 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.170 days = 9 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.171 days = 13 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.172 days = 17 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.173 days = 21 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.174 days = 25 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.175 days = 29 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.177 days = 33 }
		country_event = { id = ADISCORD_vorkerland_directorate_cw.178 days = 40 }
	}
}

# --- TVA endgame: programmes ---
# Prices are fixed, so a cancellation returns the exact price without a held
# deposit; delivery rechecks the administration, the war and the district.

# STATE 32-39 (FROM of TVA_endgame_house_block_lines); ROOT is TVA.
TVA_endgame_finish_house_block_lines = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
			hidden_trigger = {
				ROOT = { TVA_administration_active = yes }
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = TVA_endgame_fortified_district
			days = 120
		}
		# A province already at the bunker cap would swallow the order.
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_province_building_level = {
					province = {
						all_provinces = yes
						limit_to_border = yes
					}
					building = bunker
					level < 10
				}
			}
			add_building_construction = {
				type = bunker
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
				province = {
					all_provinces = yes
					limit_to_border = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = TVA_protocol_cells }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_protocol_target_cells_minus1_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				set_temp_variable = { TVA_protocol_cell_delta = -1 }
				TVA_protocol_change_district_cells = yes
				ROOT = { TVA_protocol_recount_cells = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	else = { TVA_endgame_refund_house_block_lines = yes }
}

TVA_endgame_refund_house_block_lines = {
	ROOT = {
		add_political_power = 20
		add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = 400 producer = TVA }
	}
}

# STATE 32-39 (FROM of TVA_endgame_calculated_withdrawal); ROOT is TVA. The
# evacuated district produces less, so the economy cache is invalidated.
TVA_endgame_finish_calculated_withdrawal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
			hidden_trigger = {
				ROOT = { TVA_administration_active = yes }
			}
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = {
			modifier = TVA_endgame_evacuated_district
			days = 120
		}
		ROOT = {
			add_manpower = 1500
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 40 producer = TVA }
			custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_cadre_up_6_tt
			hidden_effect = {
				add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 6 }
				TVA_refresh_administration = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else = { TVA_endgame_refund_calculated_withdrawal = yes }
}

TVA_endgame_refund_calculated_withdrawal = {
	ROOT = { add_political_power = 15 }
}

# Country scope. The copy reaches the rear only while the directorate works.

		}
		hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = TVA_endgame_archive_copied }
	}
	else = { TVA_endgame_refund_pack_the_archive = yes }
}

TVA_endgame_refund_pack_the_archive = {

	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 40 producer = TVA }
}

# Country scope. The new line goes to the Industrial District, then to the
# Northern one; without a free slot in either the cable stock returns.
TVA_endgame_finish_crew_rotation = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { TVA_administration_active = yes } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_network_down_8_tt
		custom_effect_tooltip = TVA_lab_cadre_up_5_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = TVA_network_load value = -8 }
			add_to_variable = { var = TVA_cadre_reserve value = 5 }
			TVA_refresh_administration = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				39 = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
					free_building_slots = {
						building = infrastructure
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
			}
			39 = { add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				36 = {
					is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT
					free_building_slots = {
						building = infrastructure
						size > 0
						include_locked = no
					}
				}
			}
			36 = { add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
		}
		else = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 producer = TVA }
		}
	}
	else = { TVA_endgame_refund_crew_rotation = yes }
}

TVA_endgame_refund_crew_rotation = {
	add_political_power = 20
	add_manpower = 400
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = 30 producer = TVA }
}
